### [1] [TurboEvolve: Towards Fast and Robust LLM-Driven Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18607)
*Yang Yang,Zining Zhong,Jindong Li,Jiemin Wu,Kaishen Yuan,Wenshuo Chen,Menglin Yang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: TurboEvolve是一个多岛屿进化框架，通过语言化采样和在线调度器提高LLM驱动程序进化的样本效率和鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的程序进化虽然能发现高质量程序，但其高成本和运行间方差阻碍了可靠进展，需要提高样本效率和鲁棒性

Method: 提出多岛屿进化框架，包括：1）语言化采样，让LLM生成K个多样化候选并分配采样权重；2）在线调度器动态调整K值；3）种子池注入技术，聚类种子并分配到各岛屿进行扰动和精英保留

Result: 在多个程序优化基准测试中，TurboEvolve在固定评估预算下始终获得更强性能，并在多个任务上改进了已知最佳解决方案

Conclusion: TurboEvolve通过多岛屿进化框架有效解决了LLM驱动程序进化的效率和鲁棒性问题，为程序优化提供了更可靠的进化方法

Abstract: LLM-driven program evolution can discover high-quality programs, but its cost and run-to-run variance hinder reliable progress. We propose TurboEvolve, a multi-island evolutionary framework that improves sample efficiency and robustness under fixed evaluation budgets. Inspired by the multiple-offspring strategy in evolutionary algorithms, TurboEvolve introduces verbalized Sampling, prompting the LLM to emit K diverse candidates with explicit self-assigned sampling weights, and an online scheduler that adapts K to expand exploration under stagnation and reduce overhead during steady progress. To exploit existing solution pools, we further propose "seed-pool injection," which clusters seeds and assigns them across islands with controlled perturbations and elitist preservation to balance diversity and refinement. Across multiple program-optimization benchmarks, TurboEvolve consistently achieves stronger performance at lower budgets and improves best-known solutions on several tasks.

</details>


### [2] [Optimising Urban Flood Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18620)
*James Mckenna,Christos Iliadis,Vassilis Glenis*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 开发了一个耦合先进水动力模型与定制进化算法的多目标优化工具，用于优化蓝绿基础设施配置以管理洪水风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于气候变化和城市扩张导致风暴事件频率和严重性增加，需要更高效的洪水风险管理策略。蓝绿基础设施虽提供可持续解决方案，但优化实施具有挑战性

Method: 开发了新型多目标优化工具，将完全动态水动力模型与定制进化算法耦合。水动力模型能准确评估BGI效果，进化算法专门设计以最小化所需模拟次数，确保计算可行性

Result: 工具通过可处理搜索空间的精确收敛度量和不可处理搜索空间的基准算法比较验证了有效性。相比传统设计实践，能自动高效探索广泛解决方案，为决策者提供最优解集

Conclusion: 该工具为复杂城市环境中优化各类蓝绿基础设施提供了稳健框架，使决策者能够基于最优解集做出明智投资决策

Abstract: Due to the increasing frequency and severity of storm events, driven by the escalation of anthropogenic climate change and urban expansion, there is a requirement for increasingly efficient flood risk management strategies. While Blue-Green Infrastructure (BGI) offers a sustainable solution for managing flood risk, optimal implementation is challenging. To help overcome this challenge, this study presents a novel multi-objective optimisation tool that couples a state-of-the-art hydrodynamic model with a bespoke evolutionary algorithm.
  The use of a fully dynamic hydrodynamic model enables the tool to accurately evaluate the effectiveness of proposed BGI features with respect to property scale flood vulnerability and hazard analysis. This contrasts with alternative approaches which utilise simplified models, which can only reliably predict inundation extents, thus the proposed optimisation tool provides greater certainty regarding the optimality of the solutions. As a hydrodynamic simulation is required to evaluate each candidate solution, the bespoke evolutionary algorithm is specifically designed to minimise the number of simulations required, ensuring the tool is computationally practical. The effectiveness of the tool in this regard is validated via the derivation of exact convergence measures, for a tractable search space, and via comparisons with benchmark algorithms, for an intractable search space.
  Compared with traditional design practices, the proposed tool offers an automated approach capable of efficiently exploring a wide range of solutions, providing decision-makers with a set of optimal solutions from which they can make informed investment decisions. The presented methods provide a robust framework for optimising a variety of BGI features in complex urban environments.

</details>

### [3] [HALO: Hybrid Auto-encoded Locomotion with Learned Latent Dynamics, Poincaré Maps, and Regions of Attraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18887)
*Blake Werner,Sergio A. Esteban,Massimiliano De Sa,Max H. Cohen,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO框架通过自编码器学习周期性混合动力系统的低维潜空间模型，结合潜空间Poincaré映射实现Lyapunov分析和吸引域估计，并将稳定性保证提升到全阶系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模板模型（如LIP、SLIP）只能近似复杂混合系统（如腿式机器人）的动力学，而数据驱动方法虽然能提取更准确的低维表示，但难以保证潜空间观测到的稳定性与安全性能够有意义地传递回全阶系统。

Method: HALO使用自编码器从轨迹数据中学习低维潜状态和捕捉步进运动动力学的潜空间Poincaré映射，通过解码器将潜空间的Lyapunov分析和吸引域构造提升到全阶状态空间。

Result: 在模拟跳跃机器人和全身人形机器人运动实验中，HALO生成的模型能够保留有意义的稳定性结构，并准确预测全阶系统的吸引域边界。

Conclusion: HALO框架成功桥接了数据驱动降阶建模与稳定性保证之间的鸿沟，为复杂混合动力系统提供了既准确又具有可解释稳定性保证的降阶模型。

Abstract: Reduced-order models are powerful for analyzing and controlling high-dimensional dynamical systems. Yet constructing these models for complex hybrid systems such as legged robots remains challenging. Classical approaches rely on hand-designed template models (e.g., LIP, SLIP), which, though insightful, only approximate the underlying dynamics. In contrast, data-driven methods can extract more accurate low-dimensional representations, but it remains unclear when stability and safety properties observed in the latent space meaningfully transfer back to the full-order system. To bridge this gap, we introduce HALO (Hybrid Auto-encoded Locomotion), a framework for learning latent reduced-order models of periodic hybrid dynamics directly from trajectory data. HALO employs an autoencoder to identify a low-dimensional latent state together with a learned latent Poincaré map that captures step-to-step locomotion dynamics. This enables Lyapunov analysis and the construction of an associated region of attraction in the latent space, both of which can be lifted back to the full-order state space through the decoder. Experiments on a simulated hopping robot and full-body humanoid locomotion demonstrate that HALO yields low-dimensional models that retain meaningful stability structure and predict full-order region-of-attraction boundaries.

</details>


### [4] [Thrust Regulation Through Wing Linkage Modulation on the Aerobat Platform: Piezoelectric Slip-Stick Actuated Regulator Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18900)
*Luca Ciampaglia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究通过调节Aerobat蝙蝠机器人计算结构中第一半径连杆R1的有效长度来实现推力控制，探索了多种执行方法并最终采用压电滑移执行器实现连杆长度调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aerobat蝙蝠机器人采用单电机驱动的计算结构设计，虽然重量轻但两翼共享输入电机，无法独立控制推力，限制了不对称机动能力。需要开发一种方法来调节单个机翼的推力。

Method: 通过修改计算结构中第一半径连杆R1的有效长度来调节推力。首先进行静态实验，使用FDM打印的三种长度R1连杆在不同扑翼频率下测试。然后评估多种执行方法：线缆张力调节、微型伺服电机和压电滑移执行器。最终选择TULA-50压电滑移执行器，开发了直接驱动的可变长度机构。

Result: 静态实验显示R1长度增加1.5mm可使峰值升力提高37%，并改变峰值力在向下行程中的时间。线缆张力调节因结构件柔顺性失败，微型伺服电机因脆弱性失败。压电滑移执行器成功实现连杆长度调制，最终机制在初步台架测试中展示，但输出力不足无法在扑翼过程中进行动态测试。

Conclusion: 通过嵌入式压电滑移执行器实现连杆长度调制是独立机翼推力控制的有效方法，为未来Aerobat机器人的不对称机动能力奠定了基础。

Abstract: Aerobat is a bat-inspired flapping-wing robot with a wing gait generate by the computational structure, a planar linkage of carbon fiber links driven by a single motor. This design minimizes weight but couples both wings to a shared input motor, eliminating independent thrust control and preventing asymmetric maneuvers. This thesis investigates thrust regulation by modifying the effective length of the first radius link $R_1$ in the computational structure. Static experiments using FDM-printed $R_1$ links at three lengths (28.58, 29.33, and 30.08 mm) across 3,4, and 5 Hz flapping frequencies demonstrated that a 1.5 mm length increase produced a 37% increase in peak lift force and shifted peak force timing within the downstroke. An additional experiment using a string-actuated regulator mechanism was performed. Further actuation methods were evaluated: sub-gram micro-servo and piezoelectric slip-stick. After both the string-tension and micro-servo actuation methods failed due to structural member compliance and motor fragility respectively, a TULA-50 piezoelectric slip-stick actuator was selected. Multiple force-amplifying mechanisms were prototyped, resulting in a direct-drive variable-length mechanism. This final mechanism was demonstrated in a preliminary bench-top test, though insufficient force output prevented dynamic testing during flapping. This work establishes linkage-length modulation via embedded slip-stick actuation as a viable approach to independent wing thrust control.

</details>


### [5] [Task-Adaptive Admittance Control for Human-Quadrotor Cooperative Load Transportation with Dynamic Cable-Length Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18905)
*Shuai Li,Ton T. H. Duong,Damiano Zanotto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于人-四旋翼协同负载运输的新型导纳控制器，通过主动控制绞盘实现安全高效的物理人机交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理人机交互研究主要集中于机械臂，采用阻抗或导纳控制保证安全，而人-四旋翼协同负载运输研究尚处于起步阶段，需要开发专门的控制方法

Method: 设计新型导纳控制器，考虑系统耦合动力学，使四旋翼及其缆绳能动态适应接触力；使用配备主动控制绞盘的四旋翼系统；控制器在整个CLT过程中具备任务自适应能力

Result: 在低刚度和高刚度条件下，与传统方法相比，所提方法在系统响应性和运动平滑性方面表现更优，提升了CLT能力；实验验证了控制器在包括原地装卸和负载运输在内的整个CLT过程中的任务自适应能力

Conclusion: 提出的新型导纳控制器能有效处理人-四旋翼协同负载运输中的耦合动力学问题，通过动态适应接触力提高了系统响应性和运动平滑性，为人-四旋翼物理交互提供了安全高效的解决方案

Abstract: The collaboration between humans and robots is critical in many robotic applications, especially in those requiring physical human-robot interaction (pHRI). Previous research in pHRI has largely focused on robotic manipulators, employing impedance or admittance control to maintain operational safety. Conversely, research in human-quadrotor cooperative load transportation (CLT) is still in its infancy. This letter introduces a novel admittance controller designed for safe and effective human-quadrotor CLT using a quadrotor equipped with an actively-controlled winch. The proposed method accounts for the system's coupled dynamics, allowing the quadrotor and its cable to dynamically adapt to contact forces during CLT tasks, thereby enhancing responsiveness. We experimentally validated the task-adaptive capability of the controller across the entire CLT process, including in-place loading/unloading and load transporting tasks. To this end, we compared the system performances against a conventional approach, using both variable and fixed cable lengths under low- and high-stiffness conditions. Results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms the conventional approach in terms of system responsiveness and motion smoothness, leading to improved CLT capabilities.

</details>


### [6] [Gated Memory Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18933)
*Yihuai Gao,Jinyun Liu,Shuang Li,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GMP是一种视觉运动策略，通过门控记忆机制选择性地激活历史上下文，使用交叉注意力构建潜在记忆表示，并通过注入扩散噪声增强鲁棒性，在非马尔可夫任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务具有不同的记忆需求，从无需记忆的马尔可夫任务到依赖单次或多次交互历史信息的非马尔可夫任务。简单地扩展视觉运动策略的观测历史通常会导致性能显著下降，主要原因是分布偏移和过拟合问题。

Method: 提出门控记忆策略（GMP），包含三个核心组件：1）学习记忆门机制，选择性激活历史上下文；2）轻量级交叉注意力模块，构建有效的潜在记忆表示；3）向历史动作注入扩散噪声，减轻训练和推理中对噪声或不准确历史的敏感性。

Result: 在提出的非马尔可夫基准MemMimic上，GMP相比长历史基线实现了30.1%的平均成功率提升，同时在RoboMimic的马尔可夫任务中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: GMP通过选择性记忆激活和鲁棒性增强机制，有效解决了非马尔可夫任务中的记忆需求问题，在保持马尔可夫任务性能的同时显著提升了非马尔可夫任务的性能。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation tasks exhibit varying memory requirements, ranging from Markovian tasks that require no memory to non-Markovian tasks that depend on historical information spanning single or multiple interaction trials. Surprisingly, simply extending observation histories of a visuomotor policy often leads to a significant performance drop due to distribution shift and overfitting. To address these issues, we propose Gated Memory Policy (GMP), a visuomotor policy that learns both when to recall memory and what to recall. To learn when to recall memory, GMP employs a learned memory gate mechanism that selectively activates history context only when necessary, improving robustness and reactivity. To learn what to recall efficiently, GMP introduces a lightweight cross-attention module that constructs effective latent memory representations. To further enhance robustness, GMP injects diffusion noise into historical actions, mitigating sensitivity to noisy or inaccurate histories during both training and inference. On our proposed non-Markovian benchmark MemMimic, GMP achieves a 30.1% average success rate improvement over long-history baselines, while maintaining competitive performance on Markovian tasks in RoboMimic. All code, data and in-the-wild deployment instructions are available on our project website https://gated-memory-policy.github.io/.

</details>


### [7] [Wrench-Aware Admittance Control for Unknown-Payload Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19469)
*Hossein Gholampour,Logan E. Beaver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种针对未知负载的力矩感知导纳控制框架，通过力/力矩测量补偿负载质心偏移对机器人柔顺操作的影响，提升搬运和放置精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知负载（特别是质心与工具中心点不对齐时）会在机器人手腕产生偏移力矩，柔顺控制器会将其误判为外部交互力矩，导致非预期的柔顺运动、更大的跟踪误差和降低的搬运精度

Method: 使用UR5e机器人实现力矩感知导纳控制框架：1）采用三轴平移激励项减少搬运过程中的负载诱导力效应；2）抓取后先估计负载质量，然后通过平移运动中的手腕力/力矩测量估计负载质心相对于TCP的偏移

Result: 实验结果显示，与未校正的放置相比，该方法在保持柔顺运动的同时，显著改善了搬运和放置性能

Conclusion: 提出的力矩感知导纳控制框架能有效补偿未知负载的质心偏移影响，提高柔顺操作的搬运和放置精度，同时保持系统的柔顺性

Abstract: Unknown payloads can strongly affect compliant robotic manipulation, especially when the payload center of mass is not aligned with the tool center point. In this case, the payload generates an offset wrench at the robot wrist. During motion, this wrench is not only related to payload weight, but also to payload inertia. If it is not modeled, the compliant controller can interpret it as an external interaction wrench, which causes unintended compliant motion, larger tracking error, and reduced transport accuracy. This paper presents a wrench-aware admittance control framework for unknown-payload pick-and-place using a UR5e robot. The method uses force-torque measurements in two different roles. First, a three-axis translational excitation term is used to reduce payload-induced force effects during transport without making the robot excessively stiff. Second, after grasping, the controller first estimates payload mass for transport compensation and then estimates the payload CoM offset relative to the TCP using wrist force-torque measurements collected during the subsequent translational motion. This helps improve object placement and stacking behavior. Experimental results show improved transport and placement performance compared with uncorrected placement while preserving compliant motion.

</details>


### [8] [RoomRecon: High-Quality Textured Room Layout Reconstruction on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19025)
*Seok Joon Kim,Dinh Duc Cao,Federica Spinola,Se Jin Lee,Kyu Sung Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoomRecon：用于3D房间模型的交互式实时扫描和纹理化管道，通过AR引导图像捕获和生成式AI提高纹理质量，专注于永久性房间元素以实现可定制性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RGB-D传感器和3D重建技术存在局限性：无法反映细微场景变化而无需完全重新捕获、缺乏语义场景理解、纹理化挑战影响3D模型视觉质量，这些问题影响了VR体验、室内设计和房地产等应用所需的真实感。

Method: 提出RoomRecon交互式实时扫描和纹理化管道，采用两阶段纹理化流程：1）AR引导图像捕获进行纹理化；2）集成生成式AI模型提高纹理质量；专注于永久性房间元素（墙壁、地板、天花板）以实现可定制3D模型。

Result: 在多种室内空间进行实验评估纹理质量和速度，定量结果和用户研究表明，RoomRecon在纹理质量和设备计算时间方面优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: RoomRecon通过AR引导捕获和生成式AI集成，解决了当前3D房间重建的纹理化挑战，提供了高质量的交互式实时扫描和纹理化解决方案，适用于AR/VR/XR应用。

Abstract: Widespread RGB-Depth (RGB-D) sensors and advanced 3D reconstruction technologies facilitate the capture of indoor spaces, improving the fields of augmented reality (AR), virtual reality (VR), and extended reality (XR). Nevertheless, current technologies still face limitations, such as the inability to reflect minor scene changes without a complete recapture, the lack of semantic scene understanding, and various texturing challenges that affect the 3D model's visual quality. These issues affect the realism required for VR experiences and other applications such as in interior design and real estate. To address these challenges, we introduce RoomRecon, an interactive, real-time scanning and texturing pipeline for 3D room models. We propose a two-phase texturing pipeline that integrates AR-guided image capturing for texturing and generative AI models to improve texturing quality and provide better replicas of indoor spaces. Moreover, we suggest focusing only on permanent room elements such as walls, floors, and ceilings, to allow for easily customizable 3D models. We conduct experiments in a variety of indoor spaces to assess the texturing quality and speed of our method. The quantitative results and user study demonstrate that RoomRecon surpasses state-of-the-art methods in terms of texturing quality and on-device computation time.

</details>


### [9] [AeroBridge-TTA: Test-Time Adaptive Language-Conditioned Control for UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19059)
*Lingxue Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroBridge-TTA通过测试时自适应解决语言引导无人机执行不匹配问题，在13种不匹配条件下显著提升OOD性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导无人机经常因执行不匹配而失败，即规划轨迹与控制器在真实动态变化（质量变化、阻力变化、执行器延迟、风等）时的跟踪能力之间存在差距

Method: 提出AeroBridge-TTA语言条件控制流程，包含三个部分：语言编码器将命令映射为子目标；基于子目标和学习潜在变量的自适应策略；测试时自适应模块从观测到的转移中在线更新潜在变量

Result: 在5个语言条件无人机任务和13种不匹配条件下，AeroBridge-TTA在分布内与PPO-MLP基线持平，在5种分布外条件下全部获胜，平均提升22.0个百分点（62.7% vs. 40.7%）；总体8.5个百分点的提升完全来自OOD区域。仅改变步长α的消融实验显示潜在变量更新本身带来了4.6倍的OOD提升

Conclusion: 测试时自适应能有效解决语言引导无人机中的执行不匹配问题，通过在线更新潜在变量显著提升在动态变化条件下的性能

Abstract: Language-guided unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) often fail not from bad reasoning or perception, but from execution
  mismatch: the gap between a planned trajectory and the controller's ability to track it when the real dynamics differ
  from training (mass changes, drag shifts, actuator delay, wind). We propose AeroBridge-TTA, a language-conditioned
  control pipeline that targets this gap with test-time adaptation. It has three parts: a language encoder that maps the
  command into a subgoal, an adaptive policy conditioned on the subgoal and a learned latent, and a test-time
  adaptation (TTA) module that updates the latent online from observed transitions. On five language-conditioned UAV
  tasks under 13 mismatch conditions with the same domain randomization, AeroBridge-TTA ties a strong PPO-MLP baseline
  in-distribution and wins all 5 out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions, +22.0 pts on average (62.7% vs. 40.7%); the +8.5
  pt overall gain comes entirely from the OOD regime. A same-weights ablation that only changes the step size $α$
  shows the latent update itself is responsible for a $4.6\times$ OOD lift.

</details>


### [10] [Assessing VLM-Driven Semantic-Affordance Inference for Non-Humanoid Robot Morphologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19509)
*Jess Jones,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Sabine Hauert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLMs在非人形机器人形态的具身化推理中存在显著差异，表现出低假阳性但高假阴性的保守预测模式，需要互补方法平衡安全性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在理解人-物交互方面表现出色，但其在非人形机器人形态上的应用尚未充分探索。本研究旨在解决VLMs在多样化机器人应用部署中的关键空白，即能否有效推断与人类形态根本不同的机器人的可供性。

Method: 引入新颖的混合数据集，结合标注的真实世界机器人可供性-物体关系与VLM生成的合成场景。对多个物体类别和机器人形态进行VLM性能的实证分析，评估其可供性推理能力。

Result: VLMs对非人形机器人形态显示出有希望的泛化能力，但在不同物体领域表现不一致。所有形态和物体类别均呈现低假阳性率但高假阴性率的一致模式，表明VLMs倾向于保守的可供性预测。这种模式在新工具使用场景和非传统物体操作中尤为明显。

Conclusion: 将VLMs有效集成到机器人系统中需要互补方法，以减轻过度保守行为，同时保持低假阳性率带来的固有安全优势。需要平衡安全性与实用性，特别是在新颖工具使用和非传统操作场景中。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in understanding human-object interactions, but their application to robotic systems with non-humanoid morphologies remains largely unexplored. This work investigates whether VLMs can effectively infer affordances for robots with fundamentally different embodiments than humans, addressing a critical gap in the deployment of these models for diverse robotic applications. We introduce a novel hybrid dataset that combines annotated real-world robotic affordance-object relations with VLM-generated synthetic scenarios, and perform an empirical analysis of VLM performance across multiple object categories and robot morphologies, revealing significant variations in affordance inference capabilities. Our experiments demonstrate that while VLMs show promising generalisation to non-humanoid robot forms, their performance is notably inconsistent across different object domains. Critically, we identify a consistent pattern of low false positive rates but high false negative rates across all morphologies and object categories, indicating that VLMs tend toward conservative affordance predictions. Our analysis reveals that this pattern is particularly pronounced for novel tool use scenarios and unconventional object manipulations, suggesting that effective integration of VLMs in robotic systems requires complementary approaches to mitigate over-conservative behaviour while preserving the inherent safety benefits of low false positive rates.

</details>


### [11] [Differentiable Satellite Constellation Configuration via Relaxed Coverage and Revisit Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19062)
*Shreeyam Kacker,Kerri Cahoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于梯度优化的卫星星座设计方法，通过四种连续松弛技术使覆盖和重访指标可微，结合可微轨道传播器实现端到端梯度优化，相比传统方法显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星星座设计方法存在局限性：对称参数化星座（如Walker星座）设计空间受限；元启发式方法（如模拟退火、遗传算法）计算成本高、迭代次数多。梯度优化因覆盖和重访指标的非可微性（涉及二元可见性指示器和离散最大操作）而被认为不可行。

Method: 提出四种连续松弛技术：1）软Sigmoid可见性函数；2）Noisy-OR多卫星聚合；3）泄漏积分器重访间隔跟踪；4）LogSumExp软最大值函数。这些技术与∂SGP4可微轨道传播器结合，构建从轨道要素到任务级目标的完全可微管道。

Result: 该方法能够从不规则初始化中恢复Walker-Delta几何结构，仅通过梯度就能发现具有远地点驻留的椭圆Molniya型轨道。与模拟退火、遗传算法和差分进化相比，梯度方法仅需约750次评估即可恢复Walker等效几何，而三种黑盒基线即使使用约四倍的评估预算，其重访性能仍显著较差。

Conclusion: 通过连续松弛技术使星座设计指标可微化，实现了高效的梯度优化方法，显著优于传统元启发式算法，为卫星星座设计提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Satellite constellation design requires optimizing orbital parameters across multiple satellites to maximize mission specific metrics. For many types of mission, it is desirable to maximize coverage and minimize revisit gaps over ground targets. Existing approaches to constellation design either restrict the design space to symmetric parametric families such as Walker constellations, or rely on metaheuristic methods that require significant compute and many iterations. Gradient-based optimization has been considered intractable due to the non-differentiability of coverage and revisit metrics, which involve binary visibility indicators and discrete max operations. We introduce four continuous relaxations: soft sigmoid visibility, noisy-OR multi-satellite aggregation, leaky integrator revisit gap tracking, and LogSumExp soft-maximum, which when composed with the $\partial$SGP4 differentiable orbit propagator, yield a fully differentiable pipeline from orbital elements to mission-level objectives. We show that this scheme can recover Walker-Delta geometry from irregular initializations, and discovers elliptical Molniya-like orbits with apogee dwell over extreme latitudes from only gradients. Compared to simulated annealing (SA), genetic algorithm (GA), and differential evolution (DE) baselines, our gradient-based method recovers Walker-equivalent geometry within ${\sim}750$ evaluations, whereas the three black-box baselines plateau at with significantly worse revisit even with roughly four times the evaluation budget.

</details>


### [12] [RoboWM-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating World Models in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19092)
*Feng Jiang,Yang Chen,Kyle Xu,Yuchen Liu,Haifeng Wang,Zhenhao Shen,Jasper Lu,Shengze Huang,Yuanfei Wang,Chen Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWM-Bench：首个面向机器人操作的视频世界模型基准，通过将生成的视频行为转换为可执行动作序列并进行机器人执行验证，系统评估视频世界模型的物理可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视频世界模型能生成逼真视频，但视觉真实不代表物理合理，从生成视频推断的行为可能违反物理动力学，无法在实体机器人上执行。现有基准主要关注感知或诊断，缺乏对行为可执行性的系统评估。

Method: 提出RoboWM-Bench基准，将生成的人类手部操作和机器人操作视频行为转换为具体的机器人动作序列，并通过实际机器人执行验证。基准涵盖多样化操作场景，建立统一评估协议。

Result: 评估当前最先进的视频世界模型发现，可靠生成物理可执行行为仍是开放挑战。常见失败模式包括空间推理错误、接触预测不稳定和非物理形变。虽然在操作数据上微调有改进，但物理不一致性仍然存在。

Conclusion: RoboWM-Bench填补了视频世界模型在物理可执行性评估方面的空白，揭示了当前模型在生成物理合理行为方面的局限性，为开发更物理基础的视频生成方法提供了方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large-scale video world models have enabled increasingly realistic future prediction, raising the prospect of leveraging imagined videos for robot learning. However, visual realism does not imply physical plausibility, and behaviors inferred from generated videos may violate dynamics and fail when executed by embodied agents. Existing benchmarks begin to incorporate notions of physical plausibility, but they largely remain perception- or diagnostic-oriented and do not systematically evaluate whether predicted behaviors can be translated into executable actions that complete the intended task. To address this gap, we introduce RoboWM-Bench, a manipulation-centric benchmark for embodiment-grounded evaluation of video world models. RoboWM-Bench converts generated behaviors from both human-hand and robotic manipulation videos into embodied action sequences and validates them through robotic execution. The benchmark spans diverse manipulation scenarios and establishes a unified protocol for consistent and reproducible evaluation. Using RoboWM-Bench, we evaluate state-of-the-art video world models and find that reliably generating physically executable behaviors remains an open challenge. Common failure modes include errors in spatial reasoning, unstable contact prediction, and non-physical deformations. While finetuning on manipulation data yields improvements, physical inconsistencies still persist, suggesting opportunities for more physically grounded video generation for robots.

</details>


### [13] [Multi-Gait Learning for Humanoid Robots Using Reinforcement Learning with Selective Adversarial Motion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19102)
*Yuanye Wu,Keyi Wang,Linqi Ye,Boyang Xing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种选择性对抗运动先验策略，使仿人机器人能够在统一强化学习框架中学习多种步态，包括行走、正步走、跑步、爬楼梯和跳跃，通过选择性应用AMP来平衡稳定性和动态性需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统一强化学习框架中学习仿人机器人的多样化步态面临挑战，因为不同步态对稳定性和动态表现力的要求存在冲突。传统方法难以同时满足周期性稳定步态和高动态步态的不同需求。

Method: 采用选择性对抗运动先验策略：对周期性、稳定性关键的步态（行走、正步走、爬楼梯）应用AMP以加速收敛并抑制异常行为；对高动态步态（跑步、跳跃）则故意省略AMP以避免过度约束运动。使用PPO算法结合域随机化在仿真中训练策略，并通过零样本仿真到现实迁移部署到12自由度物理机器人上。

Result: 定量比较表明，选择性AMP策略在全部五种步态上优于统一AMP策略，实现了更快的收敛速度、更低的跟踪误差，在稳定性关键步态上获得更高的成功率，同时不牺牲动态步态所需的敏捷性。

Conclusion: 选择性应用AMP的策略能够有效解决仿人机器人多步态学习中的稳定性和动态性冲突问题，为统一框架下的多样化运动技能学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Learning diverse locomotion skills for humanoid robots in a unified reinforcement learning framework remains challenging due to the conflicting requirements of stability and dynamic expressiveness across different gaits. We present a multi-gait learning approach that enables a humanoid robot to master five distinct gaits -- walking, goose-stepping, running, stair climbing, and jumping -- using a consistent policy structure, action space, and reward formulation. The key contribution is a selective Adversarial Motion Prior (AMP) strategy: AMP is applied to periodic, stability-critical gaits (walking, goose-stepping, stair climbing) where it accelerates convergence and suppresses erratic behavior, while being deliberately omitted for highly dynamic gaits (running, jumping) where its regularization would over-constrain the motion. Policies are trained via PPO with domain randomization in simulation and deployed on a physical 12-DOF humanoid robot through zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. Quantitative comparisons demonstrate that selective AMP outperforms a uniform AMP policy across all five gaits, achieving faster convergence, lower tracking error, and higher success rates on stability-focused gaits without sacrificing the agility required for dynamic ones.

</details>


### [14] [Multi-Step Gaussian Process Propagation for Adaptive Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19148)
*Alex Beaudin,Bjørn Andreas Kristiansen,Kristoffer Gryte,Corrado Chiatante,Morten Omholt Alver,Murat Arcak,Tor Arne Johansen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高斯过程的路径规划方法OLAhGP，用于机器人环境探索与监测，能够适应多模态环境传感数据并处理状态和输入约束，在藻华监测场景中相比现有方法显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效鲁棒的路径规划需要整合所有可用信息源。机器人环境探索与监测的路径规划任务高度依赖于对世界的当前信念，需要捕捉信念中的不确定性，特别是在处理多模态环境传感数据时。

Method: 提出OLAhGP方法：基于高斯过程的路径规划方法，适应多模态环境传感数据并纳入状态和输入约束。采用滚动时域优化未来航点，成本函数基于这些航点上的高斯过程后验分布。

Result: 在自主水面舰艇上使用高保真模型和现场传感数据的海洋藻华监测场景中验证。模拟和实验结果显示相比现有方法有显著改进：相同采样数量下生成更具信息量的路径，在叶绿素a富集水域识别藻华时获得更高准确度，通过总误分类概率和感兴趣区域二元误分类率衡量。

Conclusion: OLAhGP方法能够有效整合多模态传感数据并处理约束，在环境监测任务中实现更高效的路径规划和更准确的环境特征识别，为机器人自主探索提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient and robust path planning hinges on combining all accessible information sources. In particular, the task of path planning for robotic environmental exploration and monitoring depends highly on the current belief of the world. To capture the uncertainty in the belief, we present a Gaussian process based path planning method that adapts to multi-modal environmental sensing data and incorporates state and input constraints. To solve the path planning problem, we optimize over future waypoints in a receding horizon fashion, and our cost is thus a function of the Gaussian process posterior over all these waypoints. We demonstrate this method, dubbed OLAhGP, on an autonomous surface vessel using oceanic algal bloom data from both a high-fidelity model and in-situ sensing data in a monitoring scenario. Our simulated and experimental results demonstrate significant improvement over existing methods. With the same number of samples, our method generates more informative paths and achieves greater accuracy in identifying algal blooms in chlorophyll a rich waters, measured with respect to total misclassification probability and binary misclassification rate over the domain of interest.

</details>


### [15] [Multimodal embodiment-aware navigation transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19267)
*Louis Dezons,Quentin Picard,Rémi Marsal,François Goulette,David Filliat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViLiNT：基于多模态注意力的目标导航策略，通过Transformer融合RGB、LiDAR、目标嵌入和机器人本体描述符，结合扩散模型生成轨迹，并利用路径通过性预测头进行轨迹评分，显著提升零样本转移和碰撞避免能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于监督学习的地面机器人目标导航模型在零样本转移方面表现良好，但在环境、机器人或传感器配置变化等分布偏移情况下，碰撞避免能力会显著下降。需要开发更鲁棒的导航策略来应对这些挑战。

Method: 1. 使用Transformer架构融合RGB图像、3D LiDAR点云、目标嵌入和机器人本体描述符，捕捉互补的几何和外观信息；2. Transformer输出用于条件化扩散模型，生成可导航轨迹；3. 利用自动生成的离线标签训练路径通过性预测头，对扩散模型生成的轨迹进行评分和排序；4. 扩散条件化和轨迹排序都依赖于机器人本体标记，使模型能根据机器人尺寸生成和选择轨迹。

Result: 在三个模拟环境中，ViLiNT相比最先进的纯视觉基线（NoMaD）平均提升166%的成功率。在真实世界部署中，机器人在障碍物场地中的导航性能也得到验证，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合多模态融合与碰撞预测机制能显著提升越野导航的鲁棒性。ViLiNT通过融合多传感器数据、利用机器人本体信息和轨迹评分机制，有效应对分布偏移问题，实现了更可靠的零样本转移导航性能。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned navigation models for ground robots trained using supervised learning show promising zero-shot transfer, but their collision-avoidance capability nevertheless degrades under distribution shift, i.e. environmental, robot or sensor configuration changes. We propose ViLiNT a multimodal, attention-based policy for goal navigation, trained on heterogeneous data from multiple platforms and environments, which improves robustness with two key features. First, we fuse RGB images, 3D LiDAR point clouds, a goal embedding and a robot's embodiment descriptor with a transformer architecture to capture complementary geometry and appearance cues. The transformer's output is used to condition a diffusion model that generates navigable trajectories. Second, using automatically generated offline labels, we train a path clearance prediction head for scoring and ranking trajectories produced by the diffusion model. The diffusion conditioning as well as the trajectory ranking head depend on a robot's embodiment token that allows our model to generate and select trajectories with respect to the robot's dimensions. Across three simulated environments, ViLiNT improves Success Rate on average by 166\% over equivalent state-of-the-art vision-only baseline (NoMaD). This increase in performance is confirmed through real-world deployments of a rover navigating in obstacle fields. These results highlight that combining multimodal fusion with our collision prediction mechanism leads to improved off-road navigation robustness.

</details>


### [16] [Warmth and Competence in the Swarm: Designing Effective Human-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19270)
*Genki Miyauchi,Roderich Groß,Chaona Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明机器人群体行为影响人类对其温暖度和能力的感知，这些社会感知比任务表现更能预测团队偏好


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人群体与人类协作日益增多，理解人类如何感知机器人群体对于设计有效的人机团队至关重要。现有研究主要关注个体机器人的能力与意图感知，而机器人团队的社会感知相对未被充分探索。

Method: 采用能力-温暖度理论框架，通过两个实验研究：研究1让参与者作为观察者，研究2让参与者作为操作者。实验操纵群体机器人在集体搜索任务中的行为（广播时长、分离距离、个体速度），测量参与者对群体行为的社会感知。

Result: 群体行为变化一致影响温暖度和能力感知：较长广播时长增加温暖度感知；较大分离距离增加能力感知；个体速度对两种感知均无影响。社会感知比任务表现更能预测团队偏好，参与者偏好既温暖又有能力的机器人团队，而非任务完成最快的团队。

Conclusion: 人机交互动态塑造社会感知，设计机器人群体时需同时考虑技术和社会因素，以实现有效的人机协作。温暖度和能力感知是影响人类对机器人团队偏好的关键因素。

Abstract: As groups of robots increasingly collaborate with humans, understanding how humans perceive them is critical for designing effective human-robot teams. While prior research examined how humans interpret and evaluate the abilities and intentions of individual agents, social perception of robot teams remains relatively underexplored. Drawing on the competence-warmth framework, we conducted two studies manipulating swarm behaviors in completing a collective search task and measured the social perception of swarm behaviors when human participants are either observers (Study 1) and operators (Study 2). Across both studies, our results show that variations in swarm behaviors consistently influenced participants' perceptions of warmth and competence. Notably, longer broadcast durations increased perceived warmth; larger separation distances increased perceived competence. Interestingly, individual robot speed had no effect on either of the perceptions. Furthermore, our results show that these social perceptions predicted participants' team preferences more strongly than task performance. Participants preferred robot teams that were both warm and competent, not those that completed tasks most quickly. These findings demonstrate that human-robot interaction dynamically shapes social perception, underscoring the importance of integrating both technical and social considerations when designing robot swarms for effective human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [17] [Quadruped Parkour Learning: Sparsely Gated Mixture of Experts with Visual Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19344)
*Michael Ziegltrum,Jianhao Jiao,Tianhu Peng,Chengxu Zhou,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将稀疏门控混合专家架构应用于视觉机器人跑酷，相比传统MLP架构在相同激活参数下性能显著提升，成功通过障碍的试验次数翻倍，且计算效率更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人跑酷是极具挑战性的运动控制任务，传统方法通常使用密集激活的MLP架构。混合专家架构在大型语言模型中已证明能有效提升可扩展性和性能，但尚未在机器人跑酷领域得到充分探索。本研究旨在验证稀疏门控MoE架构在视觉机器人跑酷中的有效性。

Method: 采用稀疏门控混合专家架构替代传统的顺序多层感知机，在推理时仅激活参数子集。在真实Unitree Go2四足机器人上进行实验，在控制激活参数数量相同的条件下，比较MLP和MoE架构的控制策略性能。

Result: MoE策略在通过大型障碍物方面表现显著优于标准MLP基线，成功试验次数达到两倍。要达到可比性能，标准MLP需要将参数规模扩展到与MoE模型总参数相当，但这会导致计算时间增加14.3%。

Conclusion: 稀疏门控MoE架构在视觉机器人跑酷控制策略中提供了性能与计算效率之间的有利权衡，能够实现更好的可扩展性，为高性能机器人运动控制提供了有前景的架构选择。

Abstract: Robotic parkour provides a compelling benchmark for advancing locomotion over highly challenging terrain, including large discontinuities such as elevated steps. Recent approaches have demonstrated impressive capabilities, including dynamic climbing and jumping, but typically rely on sequential multilayer perceptron (MLP) architectures with densely activated layers. In contrast, sparsely gated mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures have emerged in the large language model domain as an effective paradigm for improving scalability and performance by activating only a subset of parameters at inference time. In this work, we investigate the application of sparsely gated MoE architectures to vision-based robotic parkour. We compare control policies based on standard MLPs and MoE architectures under a controlled setting where the number of active parameters at inference time is matched. Experimental results on a real Unitree Go2 quadruped robot demonstrate clear performance gains, with the MoE policy achieving double the number of successful trials in traversing large obstacles compared to a standard MLP baseline. We further show that achieving comparable performance with a standard MLP requires scaling its parameter count to match that of the total MoE model, resulting in a 14.3\% increase in computation time. These results highlight that sparsely gated MoE architectures provide a favorable trade-off between performance and computational efficiency, enabling improved scaling of control policies for vision-based robotic parkour. An anonymized link to the codebase is https://osf.io/v2kqj/files/github?view_only=7977dee10c0a44769184498eaba72e44.

</details>


### [18] [Achieving Interaction Fluidity in a Wizard-of-Oz Robotic System: A Prototype for Fluid Error-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19374)
*Carlos Baptista De Lima,Julian Hough,Frank Förster,Patrick Holthaus,Yongjun Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于VR的HRI仿真环境，满足可中断与修正、可轮询、延迟测量优化和时间精确动作重现等关键标准，以支持流畅的人机交互错误修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于语音界面的人机交互体验仍然生硬且令人沮丧，缺乏合适的开发平台来改进交互，即使在用于数据收集和原型设计的Wizard-of-Oz操作模式中也是如此。

Method: 基于先前系统，提出可中断与修正、可轮询、延迟测量优化和时间精确动作重现等关键标准，并开发了一个满足这些标准的虚拟现实HRI仿真环境，用于移动机械臂。

Result: 开发了一个满足所提关键标准的虚拟现实HRI仿真环境，能够支持流畅的错误修正和更自然的人机交互。

Conclusion: 通过满足可中断与修正、可轮询、延迟测量优化和时间精确动作重现等标准，VR HRI仿真环境能够显著改善Wizard-of-Oz系统的流畅性，从而提升人机交互体验。

Abstract: Achieving truly fluid interaction with robots with speech interfaces remains a hard problem, and the experience of current Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) remains laboured and frustrating. Some of the barriers to fluid interaction stem from a lack of a suitable development platform for HRI for improving interaction, even in robotic Wizard-of-Oz (WoZ) modes of operation used for data collection and prototyping. Based on previous systems, we propose the properties of interruptibility and correction (IaC), pollability, latency measurement and optimisation and time-accurate reproducibility of actions from logging data as key criteria for a fluid WoZ system to support fluid error correction. We finish by presenting a Virtual Reality (VR) HRI simulation environment for mobile manipulators which meets these criteria.

</details>


### [19] [M$^{2}$GRPO: Mamba-based Multi-Agent Group Relative Policy Optimization for Biomimetic Underwater Robots Pursuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19404)
*Yukai Feng,Zhiheng Wu,Zhengxing Wu,Junwen Gu,Junzhi Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出M²GRPO框架，结合选择性状态空间Mamba策略与组相对策略优化，解决仿生水下机器人协同追捕中的长时决策、部分可观测性和机器人间协调问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协同追捕策略学习方法在仿生水下机器人中面临挑战：长时决策、部分可观测性和机器人间协调需要表达能力和稳定性。现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 提出M²GRPO框架，集成选择性状态空间Mamba策略与组相对策略优化。Mamba策略利用观测历史捕捉长时依赖，基于注意力的关系特征编码智能体间交互，通过归一化高斯采样产生有界连续动作。组相对优势通过归一化每回合内智能体奖励获得，通过多智能体GRPO扩展优化，减少训练资源需求。

Result: 在团队规模和逃避者策略不同的广泛仿真和真实水池实验中，M²GRPO在追捕成功率和捕获效率上持续优于MAPPO和循环基线方法。

Conclusion: M²GRPO为仿生机器人系统协同水下追捕提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，平衡了表达能力和稳定性要求。

Abstract: Traditional policy learning methods in cooperative pursuit face fundamental challenges in biomimetic underwater robots, where long-horizon decision making, partial observability, and inter-robot coordination require both expressiveness and stability. To address these issues, a novel framework called Mamba-based multi-agent group relative policy optimization (M$^{2}$GRPO) is proposed, which integrates a selective state-space Mamba policy with group-relative policy optimization under the centralized-training and decentralized-execution (CTDE) paradigm. Specifically, the Mamba-based policy leverages observation history to capture long-horizon temporal dependencies and exploits attention-based relational features to encode inter-agent interactions, producing bounded continuous actions through normalized Gaussian sampling. To further improve credit assignment without sacrificing stability, the group-relative advantages are obtained by normalizing rewards across agents within each episode and optimized through a multi-agent extension of GRPO, significantly reducing the demand for training resources while enabling stable and scalable policy updates. Extensive simulations and real-world pool experiments across team scales and evader strategies demonstrate that M$^{2}$GRPO consistently outperforms MAPPO and recurrent baselines in both pursuit success rate and capture efficiency. Overall, the proposed framework provides a practical and scalable solution for cooperative underwater pursuit with biomimetic robot systems.

</details>


### [20] [Forward Dynamics of Variable Topology Mechanisms - The Case of Constraint Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19419)
*Andreas Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两种处理可变拓扑机构动力学的方法：基于冗余坐标的投影运动方程和基于最小坐标的Voronets方程，用于解决机构拓扑变化时的非光滑动力学问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械系统在运动过程中会因理想接触、静摩擦或部件锁定等原因发生运动学拓扑变化，这种变化在人与机器交互中日益重要。预测可变拓扑机构的动态行为需要解决非光滑动力学问题，核心挑战在于拓扑切换事件时的物理意义明确的过渡条件。

Method: 提出了两种处理拓扑切换的过渡条件：1）使用冗余坐标的投影运动方程方法；2）使用最小坐标的Voronets方程方法。讨论了两种方法的计算特性，并在平面3R机构和6自由度工业机械臂的关节锁定问题上进行了验证。

Result: 成功开发了两种处理机构拓扑变化的过渡条件方法，能够物理意义明确地描述拓扑切换事件。在平面3R机构和6自由度工业机械臂的关节锁定问题上展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的两种过渡条件方法为解决可变拓扑机构的非光滑动力学问题提供了有效工具，特别适用于处理因接触、静摩擦或部件锁定引起的拓扑变化，在人机交互等领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Many mechanical systems exhibit changes in their kinematic topology altering the mobility. Ideal contact is the best known cause, but also stiction and controlled locking of parts of a mechanism lead to topology changes. The latter is becoming an important issue in human-machine interaction. Anticipating the dynamic behavior of variable topology mechanisms requires solving a non-smooth dynamic problem. The core challenge is a physically meaningful transition condition at the topology switching events. Such a condition is presented in this paper. Two versions are reported, one using projected motion equations in terms of redundant coordinates, and another one using the Voronets equations in terms of minimal coordinates. Their computational properties are discussed. Results are shown for joint locking of a planar 3R mechanisms and a 6DOF industrial manipulator.

</details>


### [21] [LiveVLN: Breaking the Stop-and-Go Loop in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19536)
*Xiangchen Wang,Weiye Zhu,Teng Wang,TianTian Geng,Zekai Zhang,Zhiyuan Qi,Jinyu Yang,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiveVLN是一个无需训练的框架，通过多步动作延续增强预训练的VLM导航器，实现更连续的具身导航，减少等待时间并提高动作可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前导航系统虽然基准测试结果良好，但在实际部署中经常出现明显的"走走停停"现象。这是因为感知-推理-执行循环仍然是阻塞式的：每次新观察后，控制器必须等待感知、传输和推理完成后才能继续运动。仅减少动作生成成本无法消除冗余等待。

Method: LiveVLN采用多步动作延续方法，在执行过程中重叠处理新到达的观察结果。框架在运行时操作，与兼容的预训练VLM导航器集成，允许在执行当前可执行前缀耗尽之前传递刷新的未来动作，从而在运动期间保持动作的持续可用性。

Result: 在R2R和RxR数据集上，LiveVLN保持了基准性能，同时减少了等待时间并提高了动作可用性。在实际部署中，平均等待时间减少高达77.7%，StreamVLN的墙钟时间缩短12.6%，NaVIDA缩短19.6%，产生更连贯的执行效果。

Conclusion: LiveVLN通过多步动作延续解决了具身导航中的阻塞等待问题，实现了更连续的导航执行，显著减少了实际部署中的等待时间，同时保持了导航性能。

Abstract: Recent navigation systems achieve strong benchmark results, yet real-world deployment often remains visibly stop-and-go. This bottleneck arises because the sense-inference-execution loop is still blocking: after each new observation, the controller must wait for sensing, transmission, and inference before motion can continue. Reducing action-generation cost alone therefore does not remove redundant waiting. To address this issue, we present LiveVLN, a training-free framework for more continuous embodied navigation by augmenting pretrained VLM navigators with multi-step action continuation. Instead of pausing for each full sense-and-inference round, LiveVLN overlaps execution with the processing of newly arrived observations, allowing refreshed future actions to be handed off before the current executable prefix is exhausted. This design keeps actions continuously available during motion, reducing idle waiting and enabling smoother online execution. The framework operates at runtime and can be integrated with compatible pretrained VLM navigators. Across R2R and RxR, LiveVLN preserves benchmark performance while reducing waiting time and improving action availability. In real-world deployments, it cuts average episode waiting time by up to $77.7\%$ and shortens wall-clock episode time by $12.6\%$ on StreamVLN and $19.6\%$ on NaVIDA, yielding more coherent execution during deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/NIneeeeeem/LiveVLN.

</details>


### [22] [Autonomous UAV Pipeline Near-proximity Inspection via Disturbance-Aware Predictive Visual Servoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19618)
*Wen Li,Hui Wang,Jinya Su,Cunjia Liu,Wen-Hua Chen,Shihua Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉模型预测控制的自主四旋翼无人机近距管道巡检框架，通过扩展状态卡尔曼滤波和地形自适应速度设计，在三维场景中实现可靠巡检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管道巡检对能源运输安全至关重要，但面临距离长、地形复杂、人工巡检风险高等挑战。无人机提供灵活传感平台，但可靠的自主巡检仍具挑战性，特别是在三维场景中需要应对视觉更新率低、测量噪声和环境不确定性等问题。

Method: 1. 基于图像视觉伺服的模型预测控制(VMPC)框架，将四旋翼动力学与图像特征运动学耦合；2. 扩展状态卡尔曼滤波与图像特征预测(ESKF-PRE)方案，处理低速率视觉更新、噪声和不确定性；3. 地形自适应速度设计，在未知地形坡度下维持期望巡航速度；4. 将估计的总扰动纳入VMPC预测模型，形成ESKF-PRE-VMPC框架。

Result: 1. 高保真Gazebo仿真和真实世界实验验证；2. 无风直线管道巡检中，管道方向RMSE降低52.63%，图像横向偏差RMSE降低75.04%；3. 成功完成基线方法失败的风扰动和弯管任务；4. 改装开源纳米四旋翼进行室内实验。

Conclusion: 提出的ESKF-PRE-VMPC框架在三维管道巡检中表现出优越性能，能够有效处理视觉延迟、环境扰动和未知地形，为自主无人机管道巡检提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable pipeline inspection is critical to safe energy transportation, but is constrained by long distances, complex terrain, and risks to human inspectors. Unmanned aerial vehicles provide a flexible sensing platform, yet reliable autonomous inspection remains challenging. This paper presents an autonomous quadrotor near-proximity pipeline inspection framework for three-dimensional scenarios based on image-based visual servoing model predictive control (VMPC). A unified predictive model couples quadrotor dynamics with image feature kinematics, enabling direct image-space prediction within the control loop. To address low-rate visual updates, measurement noise, and environmental uncertainties, an extended-state Kalman filtering scheme with image feature prediction (ESKF-PRE) is developed, and the estimated lumped disturbances are incorporated into the VMPC prediction model, yielding the ESKF-PRE-VMPC framework. A terrain-adaptive velocity design is introduced to maintain the desired cruising speed while generating vertical velocity references over unknown terrain slopes without prior terrain information. The framework is validated in high-fidelity Gazebo simulations and real-world experiments. In real-world tests, the proposed method reduces RMSE by 52.63% and 75.04% in pipeline orientation and lateral deviation in the image, respectively, for straight-pipeline inspection without wind, and successfully completes both wind-disturbance and bend-pipeline tasks where baseline method fails. An open-source nano quadrotor is modified for indoor experimentation.

</details>


### [23] [A Gesture-Based Visual Learning Model for Acoustophoretic Interactions using a Swarm of AcoustoBots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19643)
*Alex Lin,Lei Gao,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Sriram Subramanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于手势的视觉学习框架，用于与多模态声学机器人平台进行无接触人机交互，通过视觉语言模型识别手势并映射到触觉、音频和悬浮三种模态，实现了87.8%的切换准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的声学机器人（AcoustoBots）虽然能够提供空中触觉、定向音频和声悬浮等多模态功能，但主要依赖脚本命令，缺乏直观的实时人机交互界面。需要开发一种自然、无接触的控制方式来增强人机交互体验。

Method: 结合ESP32-CAM手势捕捉、PhaseSpace运动追踪、集中式处理和基于OpenCLIP的视觉学习模型（VLM）与线性探测，构建手势识别系统。系统将三种手势分类并映射到触觉、音频和悬浮三种模态，实现多模态声学机器人平台的无接触控制。

Result: 手势识别准确率从约67%（小数据集）提升至近98%（大数据集）。在两个声学机器人的集成实验中，系统在90次试验中实现了87.8%的整体手势到模态切换准确率，平均端到端延迟为3.95秒。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于视觉语言模型的手势界面在多模态人机交互中的可行性。虽然当前系统存在集中式处理、静态手势集和受控环境评估等限制，但为更富表现力、可扩展和易访问的群体机器人界面奠定了基础。

Abstract: AcoustoBots are mobile acoustophoretic robots capable of delivering mid-air haptics, directional audio, and acoustic levitation, but existing implementations rely on scripted commands and lack an intuitive interface for real-time human control. This work presents a gesture-based visual learning framework for contactless human-swarm interaction with a multimodal AcoustoBot platform. The system combines ESP32-CAM gesture capture, PhaseSpace motion tracking, centralized processing, and an OpenCLIP-based visual learning model (VLM) with linear probing to classify three hand gestures and map them to haptics, audio, and levitation modalities. Validation accuracy improved from about 67% with a small dataset to nearly 98% with the largest dataset. In integrated experiments with two AcoustoBots, the system achieved an overall gesture-to-modality switching accuracy of 87.8% across 90 trials, with an average end-to-end latency of 3.95 seconds. These results demonstrate the feasibility of using a vision-language-model-based gesture interface for multimodal human-swarm interaction. While the current system is limited by centralized processing, a static gesture set, and controlled-environment evaluation, it establishes a foundation for more expressive, scalable, and accessible swarm robotic interfaces.

</details>


### [24] [Multi-Cycle Spatio-Temporal Adaptation in Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19670)
*Alex Cuellar,Michael Hagenow,Julie Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPIDDS框架通过建模个体空间行为（运动路径）和时间行为（任务完成时间），在任务级和运动级联合适应，优化人机协作计划，提高效率和减少近距离干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机协作优化面临挑战：现有方法要么在任务级优化分配和调度但忽略空间干扰，要么在运动级关注碰撞避免但忽视任务上下文，缺乏对个体化人类能力和偏好的统一建模。

Method: 提出RAPIDDS框架：在多周期交互中建模个体的空间行为（运动路径）和时间行为（任务完成时间），然后联合适应任务调度并引导机器人运动扩散模型，以最大化效率并最小化近距离干扰。

Result: 通过仿真消融研究和7自由度机械臂物理场景验证了双重适应的重要性；用户研究（n=32）显示，相比非自适应系统，在客观指标（效率、接近度）和主观指标（流畅性、用户偏好）上均有显著改进。

Conclusion: RAPIDDS通过统一任务级和运动级适应，有效解决了人机协作中的个体化建模问题，显著提升了团队效率和用户体验，为人机协作的实际部署提供了重要框架。

Abstract: Effective human-robot teaming is crucial for the practical deployment of robots in human workspaces. However, optimizing joint human-robot plans remains a challenge due to the difficulty of modeling individualized human capabilities and preferences. While prior research has leveraged the multi-cycle structure of domains like manufacturing to learn an individual's tendencies and adapt plans over repeated interactions, these techniques typically consider task-level and motion-level adaptation in isolation. Task-level methods optimize allocation and scheduling but often ignore spatial interference in close-proximity scenarios; conversely, motion-level methods focus on collision avoidance while ignoring the broader task context. This paper introduces RAPIDDS, a framework that unifies these approaches by modeling an individual's spatial behavior (motion paths) and temporal behavior (time required to complete tasks) over multiple cycles. RAPIDDS then jointly adapts task schedules and steers diffusion models of robot motions to maximize efficiency and minimize proximity accounting for these individualized models. We demonstrate the importance of this dual adaptation through an ablation study in simulation and a physical robot scenario using a 7-DOF robot arm. Finally, we present a user study (n=32) showing significant plan improvement compared to non-adaptive systems across both objective metrics, such as efficiency and proximity, and subjective measures, including fluency and user preference. See this paper's companion video at: https://youtu.be/55Q3lq1fINs.

</details>


### [25] [Learning Hybrid-Control Policies for High-Precision In-Contact Manipulation Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19677)
*Hunter L. Brown,Geoffrey Hollinger,Stefan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MATCH方法，通过混合位置-力控制策略动态选择控制模式，显著提升精密装配任务的成功率并减少损坏


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于姿态的强化学习控制策略在需要力约束的精密装配任务（如脆弱连接器插入）中存在局限性，缺乏对力的显式控制，依赖精心调校的低级控制器来避免损坏操作

Method: 提出混合位置-力控制策略，学习在每个控制维度上动态选择使用力控制或位置控制；引入MATCH训练方法，调整策略动作概率以显式反映混合控制中的模式选择行为

Result: MATCH在极端定位不确定性下的脆弱孔轴装配任务中显著优于姿态控制策略：成功率提高10%，损坏减少5倍；在1600多次仿真到真实实验中，高噪声环境下成功率是姿态策略的2倍（68% vs 33%），平均施力比变阻抗策略减少30%

Conclusion: MATCH方法通过混合位置-力控制策略有效解决了精密装配任务中的力约束问题，在保持数据效率的同时显著提升了任务成功率和安全性，为复杂操作任务提供了更可靠的控制方案

Abstract: Reinforcement learning-based control policies have been frequently demonstrated to be more effective than analytical techniques for many manipulation tasks. Commonly, these methods learn neural control policies that predict end-effector pose changes directly from observed state information. For tasks like inserting delicate connectors which induce force constraints, pose-based policies have limited explicit control over force and rely on carefully tuned low-level controllers to avoid executing damaging actions. In this work, we present hybrid position-force control policies that learn to dynamically select when to use force or position control in each control dimension. To improve learning efficiency of these policies, we introduce Mode-Aware Training for Contact Handling (MATCH) which adjusts policy action probabilities to explicitly mirror the mode selection behavior in hybrid control. We validate MATCH's learned policy effectiveness using fragile peg-in-hole tasks under extreme localization uncertainty. We find MATCH substantially outperforms pose-control policies -- solving these tasks with up to 10% higher success rates and 5x fewer peg breaks than pose-only policies under common types of state estimation error. MATCH also demonstrates data efficiency equal to pose-control policies, despite learning in a larger and more complex action space. In over 1600 sim-to-real experiments, we find MATCH succeeds twice as often as pose policies in high noise settings (33% vs.~68%) and applies ~30% less force on average compared to variable impedance policies on a Franka FR3 in laboratory conditions.

</details>


### [26] [Mask World Model: Predicting What Matters for Robust Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19683)
*Yunfan Lou,Xiaowei Chi,Xiaojie Zhang,Zezhong Qian,Chengxuan Li,Rongyu Zhang,Yaoxu Lyu,Guoyu Song,Chuyao Fu,Haoxuan Xu,Pengwei Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MWM使用语义掩码而非RGB视频预测的视觉世界模型，通过几何信息瓶颈过滤视觉噪声，提升机器人策略的泛化能力和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于RGB视频预测的世界模型容易过拟合于动态背景、光照变化等无关因素，导致泛化能力差和控制策略脆弱。需要一种能捕捉核心物理动态而过滤视觉噪声的方法。

Method: 提出掩码世界模型（MWM），利用视频扩散架构预测语义掩码的演化而非像素。通过几何信息瓶颈强制模型学习关键物理动态和接触关系。将掩码动态骨干与基于扩散的策略头无缝集成，实现端到端控制。

Result: 在LIBERO和RLBench仿真基准测试中显著优于最先进的RGB世界模型。真实世界实验和鲁棒性评估（通过随机令牌剪枝）显示MWM具有优越的泛化能力和对纹理信息损失的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MWM通过语义掩码预测的几何信息瓶颈有效过滤视觉噪声，专注于核心物理动态，从而实现了更可靠、泛化能力更强的机器人控制策略。

Abstract: World models derived from large-scale video generative pre-training have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist robot policy learning. However, standard approaches often focus on high-fidelity RGB video prediction, this can result in overfitting to irrelevant factors, such as dynamic backgrounds and illumination changes. These distractions reduce the model's ability to generalize, ultimately leading to unreliable and fragile control policies. To address this, we introduce the Mask World Model (MWM), which leverages video diffusion architectures to predict the evolution of semantic masks instead of pixels. This shift imposes a geometric information bottleneck, forcing the model to capture essential physical dynamics and contact relations while filtering out visual noise. We seamlessly integrate this mask dynamics backbone with a diffusion-based policy head to enable robust end-to-end control. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the superiority of MWM on the LIBERO and RLBench simulation benchmarks, significantly outperforming the state-of-the-art RGB-based world models. Furthermore, real-world experiments and robustness evaluation (via random token pruning) reveal that MWM exhibits superior generalization capabilities and robust resilience to texture information loss.

</details>


### [27] [UniT: Toward a Unified Physical Language for Human-to-Humanoid Policy Learning and World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19734)
*Boyu Chen,Yi Chen,Lu Qiu,Jerry Bai,Yuying Ge,Yixiao Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniT框架通过视觉锚定建立统一物理语言，解决人形机器人数据稀缺问题，实现人类到人形机器人的跨具身知识迁移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人基础模型扩展受限于机器人数据稀缺，而海量人类自我中心数据提供了可扩展替代方案，但跨具身（人类与人形机器人）的动力学不匹配是主要挑战

Method: 提出UniT框架，基于"异构运动学共享通用视觉结果"的理念，采用三分支交叉重建机制：动作预测视觉以将运动学锚定到物理结果，视觉重建动作以过滤无关视觉干扰，融合分支将净化后的模态协同到共享的离散潜在空间

Result: 在两种范式下验证：1) VLA-UniT在策略学习中实现最先进的数据效率和OOD泛化，展示零样本任务迁移；2) WM-UniT在世界建模中实现直接的人类到人形机器人动作迁移，增强动作可控性。t-SNE可视化显示人类和人形机器人特征收敛到共享流形

Conclusion: UniT通过诱导高度对齐的跨具身表示，为将海量人类知识提炼为通用人形机器人能力提供了可扩展路径

Abstract: Scaling humanoid foundation models is bottlenecked by the scarcity of robotic data. While massive egocentric human data offers a scalable alternative, bridging the cross-embodiment chasm remains a fundamental challenge due to kinematic mismatches. We introduce UniT (Unified Latent Action Tokenizer via Visual Anchoring), a framework that establishes a unified physical language for human-to-humanoid transfer. Grounded in the philosophy that heterogeneous kinematics share universal visual consequences, UniT employs a tri-branch cross-reconstruction mechanism: actions predict vision to anchor kinematics to physical outcomes, while vision reconstructs actions to filter out irrelevant visual confounders. Concurrently, a fusion branch synergies these purified modalities into a shared discrete latent space of embodiment-agnostic physical intents. We validate UniT across two paradigms: 1) Policy Learning (VLA-UniT): By predicting these unified tokens, it effectively leverages diverse human data to achieve state-of-the-art data efficiency and robust out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization on both humanoid simulation benchmark and real-world deployments, notably demonstrating zero-shot task transfer. 2) World Modeling (WM-UniT): By aligning cross-embodiment dynamics via unified tokens as conditions, it realizes direct human-to-humanoid action transfer. This alignment ensures that human data seamlessly translates into enhanced action controllability for humanoid video generation. Ultimately, by inducing a highly aligned cross-embodiment representation (empirically verified by t-SNE visualizations revealing the convergence of human and humanoid features into a shared manifold), UniT offers a scalable path to distill vast human knowledge into general-purpose humanoid capabilities.

</details>

### [28] [A taxonomy for controlling (in)consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18766)
*Marcelo E. Coniglio,Rafael Ongaratto*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LFI层次结构L_n^k（一致性控制逻辑LCC），这是一个受da Costa C_n系统启发的二维逻辑层次，用于表示不同程度的次协调承诺和相关的一致性、不一致性及否定概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是建立一个能够表示不同次协调承诺程度的逻辑层次结构，从怀疑论到教条主义，为不同哲学立场提供逻辑表达工具，同时扩展多值次协调逻辑的语义框架。

Method: 采用交换结构语义学建立L_n^k层次，结合Karnaugh图和扭曲结构技术为5值LFI3提供公理化，使用RN矩阵为整个L_n^k家族提供一般语义学描述，并证明其可靠性和完备性。

Result: 成功构建了L_n^k逻辑层次，证明了一些已知LFI是该层次的特例，为LFI3提供了完整的公理化和语义描述，为整个L_n^k家族建立了基于RN矩阵的一般语义框架并证明了可靠完备性。

Conclusion: L_n^k层次为次协调逻辑提供了灵活的框架，能够表示不同程度的次协调承诺，支持实用主义选择，扩展了多值次协调逻辑的语义方法，为哲学立场的形式化提供了逻辑工具。

Abstract: In this article, the hierarchy of LFIs L$_n^k$, Logics of Controlled Consistency (LCC), is introduced. Inspired by da Costa's original C$_n$ systems, this hierarchy can represent different degrees of paraconsistent commitment and different related notions of consistency, inconsistency, and negation associated with each two-dimensional level of these logics. In one dimension, the logics become increasingly more paraconsistent by allowing the consistency operator to behave inconsistently up to a fixed iteration. In another dimension, the negation is increasingly strengthened. Initially, we present these logics with a swap structure semantics, showing their soundness and completeness. Some well-known LFIs are shown to be particular cases of LCCs. With some examples, we show how these different logics represent different types of paraconsistent commitment: from skepticism to dogmastism, these logics have the multiplicity to represent these different philosophical positions. Furthermore, the development of the hierarchy in a general manner allows pragmatism to take place when considering the different types of paraconsistent commitment. Each level we go up in this direction we get a stronger family of logics. Furthermore, we also present an extension of an LCC, a 5-valued LFI called LFI3, a sublogic of LFI1. LFI3 presents a paradigmatic case for the development of many-valued LFIs that have more than three values. Using a technique that combines Karnaugh Maps and Twist Structures, we give an axiomatization and a semantical account of LFI3. Finally, using RNmatrices, we give a general semantical account of the L$_n^k$ family of logics, and we also prove its soundness and completeness.

</details>


### [29] [Streamliners for Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19251)
*Florentina Voboril,Martin Gebser,Stefan Szeider,Alice Tarzariol*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 将StreamLLM方法从约束规划扩展到答案集编程，使用大语言模型为ASP问题生成流线型约束，在三个基准问题上实现4-5倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流线型约束通过排除部分解空间来减少组合问题的搜索空间。现有StreamLLM方法使用LLM为约束规划生成流线型约束，但尚未应用于答案集编程领域。本文旨在将这一方法扩展到ASP，以利用LLM的语义理解能力自动生成有效的约束简化

Method: 给定ASP编码和少量训练实例，提示多个LLM生成候选约束。通过三层筛选：1) 排除语法错误；2) 排除使可满足实例不可满足的约束；3) 排除在所有训练实例上降低性能的约束。剩余流线型约束与原始编码一起评估，采用虚拟最佳编码策略为每个实例选择最快版本

Result: 在三个ASP竞赛基准问题（Partner Units Problem、Sokoban、Towers of Hanoi）上，虚拟最佳编码相比原始编码实现了4-5倍的加速。不同LLM生成的约束在语义上具有多样性，而非仅仅是语法变体，表明该方法能够捕捉真正的问题结构

Conclusion: 将StreamLLM方法成功扩展到ASP领域，LLM能够生成语义多样且有效的流线型约束，显著提升求解性能。该方法展示了LLM在理解问题结构和自动生成简化约束方面的潜力，为ASP求解优化提供了新途径

Abstract: Streamliner constraints reduce the search space of combinatorial problems by ruling out portions of the solution space. We adapt the StreamLLM approach, which uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate streamliners for Constraint Programming, to Answer Set Programming (ASP). Given an ASP encoding and a few small training instances, we prompt multiple LLMs to propose candidate constraints. Candidates that cause syntax errors, render satisfiable instances unsatisfiable, or degrade performance on all training instances are discarded. The surviving streamliners are evaluated together with the original encoding, and we report results for a virtual best encoding (VBE) that, for each instance, selects the fastest among the original encoding and its streamlined variants. On three ASP Competition benchmarks (Partner Units Problem, Sokoban, Towers of Hanoi), the VBE achieves speedups of up to 4--5x over the original encoding. Different LLMs produce semantically diverse constraints, not mere syntactic variations, indicating that the approach captures genuine problem structure.

</details>


### [30] [Automatic constraint satisfaction problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19266)
*Andrei Bulatov,Xiaoyang Gong,Bakh Khoussainov,Xinyao Wang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于有限自动机的约束满足问题（AutCSP），建立了自动约束满足问题的复杂性分类理论，证明了布尔域上的Schaefer二分定理可扩展到AutCSP，并提供了通过自动多态性判断任意有限域上AutCSP可处理性的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索由有限自动机定义的约束语言所构成的约束满足问题（CSPs）。传统CSP研究有限约束语言，而AutCSP通过有限自动机描述无限但有限可描述的约束关系集合，能够简洁表示复杂约束。研究AutCSP的复杂性可以揭示经典CSP、自动机和逻辑之间的相互作用，并精确界定可处理与不可处理约束之间的边界。

Method: 方法的核心是定义自动约束满足问题（AutCSP），其中约束语言和实例都由有限自动机指定。首先证明检查操作是否为这种语言的多元态可以在多项式时间内完成。基于此，建立了AutCSP的复杂性分类结果。特别地，证明了Schaefer二分定理可以扩展到布尔域上的AutCSP，并通过自动多态性为任意有限域上的AutCSP类提供了可处理性判定算法。

Result: 主要结果包括：1）证明了检查操作是否为自动约束语言的多元态可以在多项式时间内完成；2）建立了Schaefer二分定理在布尔域AutCSP上的扩展；3）提供了通过自动多态性判定任意有限域上AutCSP类可处理性的算法；4）特别重要的是，这些多项式时间算法可以在比标准CSP对应实例指数级更简洁的AutCSP实例上运行。

Conclusion: 结论表明，基于有限自动机的约束满足问题（AutCSP）提供了一个强大的框架，能够简洁表示复杂约束，同时保持可计算性。研究建立了AutCSP的复杂性分类理论，将经典CSP的二分定理扩展到自动机表示的场景，并为实际应用提供了高效算法，这些算法能够处理指数级更简洁的问题表示。

Abstract: We study constraint satisfaction problems (CSPs) where the constraint languages are defined by finite automata, giving rise to automata-based CSPs. The key notion is the concept of Automatic Constraint Satisfaction Problem ($AutCSP$), where constraint languages and instances are specified by finite automata. The $AutCSP$ captures infinite yet finitely describable sets of relations, enabling concise representations of complex constraints. Studying the complexity of the $AutCSP$s illustrates the interplay between classical CSPs, automata, and logic, sharpening the boundary between tractable and intractable constraints. We show that checking whether an operation is a polymorphism of such a language can be done in polynomial time. Building on this, we establish several complexity classification results for the $AutCSP$. In particular, we prove that Schaefer's Dichotomy Theorem extends to the $AutCSP$ over the Boolean domain, and we provide algorithms that decide tractability of some classes of $AutCSP$s over arbitrary finite domains via automatic polymorphisms. An important part of our work is that our polynomial-time algorithms run on $AutCSP$ instances that can be exponentially more succinct than their standard CSP counterparts.

</details>


### [31] [A Sequent Calculus for General Inductive Definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19382)
*Robbe Van den Eede,Marc Denecker*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 扩展Brotherston和Simpson的LKID演算，提出SCFO(ID)作为FO(ID)的矢列演算，支持非单调归纳定义，基于稳定语义解决非单调性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归纳定义证明系统大多对定义施加语法限制，排除了许多有用且自然的定义。FO(ID)作为经典一阶逻辑的扩展，支持一般非单调归纳定义，但缺乏合适的证明演算。

Method: 扩展Brotherston和Simpson的LKID矢列演算，基于数学归纳原理，构建SCFO(ID)演算。主要挑战是容纳非单调归纳定义，通过借鉴逻辑编程中与FO(ID)的良基语义密切相关的稳定语义来解决。

Result: 建立了SCFO(ID)演算，并通过多种示例演示其应用。证明了该演算的若干证明论性质，为涉及一般归纳定义的定理提供了形式化证明基础。

Conclusion: SCFO(ID)是一个理论上有充分依据的FO(ID)矢列演算，能够对涉及一般归纳定义的定理进行形式化证明，克服了现有系统对归纳定义施加语法限制的问题。

Abstract: Inductive definitions are an important form of knowledge. The logic FO(ID) is an extension of classical first-order logic FO with general non-monotone inductive definitions. Most existing proof systems for inductive definitions impose syntactic constraints on their definitions, thereby excluding many useful and natural definitions. We extend an existing sequent calculus LKID by Brotherston and Simpson, founded on the principle of mathematical induction, to a sequent calculus SCFO(ID) for FO(ID). The main challenge in this extension is the accommodation of non-monotone inductive definitions. To overcome this challenge, we draw inspiration from the stable semantics, which is a commonly used semantics in logic programming that is closely related to the well-founded semantics behind FO(ID). We corroborate SCFO(ID) by establishing several proof-theoretical properties and through demonstration on various examples. In conclusion, SCFO(ID) is a theoretically substantiated sequent calculus for FO(ID), enabling formal proofs of theorems involving general inductive definitions.

</details>


### [32] [Counting Worlds Branching Time Semantics for post-hoc Bias Mitigation in generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19431)
*Alessandro G. Buda,Giuseppe Primiero,Leonardo Ceragioli,Melissa Antonelli*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出CTLF逻辑框架，用于形式化分析生成式AI输出序列中的偏见问题，提供概率分布验证、边界预测和修复措施的形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI偏见缓解策略多为经验性方法，缺乏形式化保证。需要一种形式化逻辑框架来分析和验证生成过程中偏见问题的动态演变。

Method: 提出CTLF分支时序逻辑，采用计数世界语义（每个世界代表生成过程中特定步骤的可能输出），引入模态运算符来验证输出序列是否符合受保护属性的预期概率分布、预测保持可接受边界的可能性，以及确定需要移除多少输出才能恢复公平性。

Result: 在偏见的图像生成示例中展示了CTLF公式如何在不同时间点表达具体的公平性属性，验证了框架的实际应用能力。

Conclusion: CTLF为生成式AI偏见分析提供了形式化逻辑框架，能够对输出序列中的偏见问题进行动态验证和预测，弥补了现有经验性方法缺乏形式化保证的不足。

Abstract: Generative AI systems are known to amplify biases present in their training data. While several inference-time mitigation strategies have been proposed, they remain largely empirical and lack formal guarantees. In this paper we introduce CTLF, a branching-time logic designed to reason about bias in series of generative AI outputs. CTLF adopts a counting worlds semantics where each world represents a possible output at a given step in the generation process and introduces modal operators that allow us to verify whether the current output series respects an intended probability distribution over a protected attribute, to predict the likelihood of remaining within acceptable bounds as new outputs are generated, and to determine how many outputs are needed to remove in order to restore fairness. We illustrate the framework on a toy example of biased image generation, showing how CTLF formulas can express concrete fairness properties at different points in the output series.

</details>


### [33] [Equational and Inductive Reasoning for Maude in Athena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19475)
*Mateo Sanabria,Carlos Varela,Camilo Rocha,Nicolas Cardozo*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: maude2athena框架将Maude的等式理论系统化地转换为Athena定理证明语言，支持归纳推理、等式链、基于案例的推理和反证法，弥合了模型检测与定理证明之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等式逻辑框架虽然为确定性功能行为、抽象数据类型和数据规范表示提供了强大基础，但缺乏对归纳或演绎推理的原生支持。需要将Maude的等式理论与支持自然演绎证明的定理证明语言连接起来，以支持更丰富的推理能力。

Method: 开发maude2athena框架，系统地将Maude的等式理论翻译为Athena定理证明语言。翻译支持带参数归纳规则的模结构公理归纳推理，在保持原始规范语义的同时确保翻译紧凑且适合演绎推理。

Result: 该框架能够忠实编码成员等式逻辑到多类设置中，在合理条件下避免指数爆炸。翻译保持了原始规范的语义，同时确保翻译结果紧凑且适合演绎推理。

Conclusion: maude2athena框架弥合了模型检测与定理证明之间的差距，使形式化验证工作能够同时受益于这两种方法，为等式规范提供了强大的推理支持。

Abstract: In the rewriting logic framework, equational-based specifications are used to define deterministic functional behavior, abstract data types, and canonical representations of data. These specifications include a (possibly order-sorted) signature and equations interpreted modulo structural axioms, such as associativity, commutativity, and identity. While equational rewriting provides a powerful basis for execution and symbolic reasoning, it does not by itself offer native support for inductive or deductive reasoning. This paper presents maude2athena, a framework that systematically translates Maude's equational theories into Athena, a theorem proving language designed to support natural deduction proofs over many-sorted first-order logic specifications, including inductive reasoning, equational chaining, case-based reasoning, and proofs by contradiction. The translation supports induction-based reasoning modulo structural axioms with parametric induction rules; it faithfully encodes membership equational logic in a many-sorted setting without exponential blowup under reasonable conditions. This approach preserves the semantics of the original specification, while ensuring that the translation remains compact and amenable to deductive reasoning. This work helps bridge the gap between model checking and theorem proving, enabling formal verification efforts that can benefit from both of these approaches.

</details>

### [34] [Compile to Compress: Boosting Formal Theorem Provers by Compiler Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18587)
*Guchan Li,Rui Tian,Hongning Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习-精炼框架的定理证明方法，利用编译器将多样证明尝试映射到结构化失败模式的压缩特性，通过局部错误修正避免长历史积累，在有限计算资源下实现高效证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在形式定理证明中表现出潜力，但最佳性能通常需要大量测试时计算（通过大规模展开或长上下文窗口），这存在可扩展性瓶颈。需要解决这一效率问题。

Method: 引入学习-精炼框架，利用编译器将多样证明尝试映射到结构化失败模式的压缩特性。执行树搜索，基于显式验证器反馈进行局部错误修正，避免积累长证明历史的高成本。

Result: 该方法在不同规模上持续增强基础证明器的推理能力。在PutnamBench上，在可比测试时预算下，使用约80亿和约320亿参数模型取得了公开报告中的最佳性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为下一代验证器引导推理提供了可扩展范式，通过利用形式验证中的信息结构（证明尝试到失败模式的压缩），有效解决了定理证明中的可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in formal theorem proving, yet state-of-the-art performance often necessitates prohibitive test-time compute via massive roll-outs or extended context windows. In this work, we address this scalability bottleneck by exploiting an informative structure in formal verification: the observation that compilers map a vast space of diverse proof attempts to a compact set of structured failure modes. We introduce a learning-to-refine framework that leverages this compression to perform efficient learning and proof exploration. We perform tree search that corrects errors locally conditioned on explicit verifier feedback, thereby circumventing the costs associated with accumulating a long history of proof attempts. Extensive evaluations show that our method consistently amplifies the reasoning capabilities of base provers across varying scales. Notably, our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on PutnamBench among publicly reported $\sim$8B and $\sim$32B parameter models under comparable test-time budgets, offering a scalable paradigm for next-generation verifier-guided reasoning.

</details>


### [35] [Easy Samples Are All You Need: Self-Evolving LLMs via Data-Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18639)
*Zhiyin Yu,Bo Zhang,Qibin Hou,Zhonghai Wu,Xiao Luo,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EasyRL：一种基于认知学习理论的新型RL方法，通过整合简单标注数据的可靠知识转移和渐进式分治策略，仅需10%简单标注数据即可超越现有SOTA方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的RL研究存在标注成本高、模型崩溃或奖励破解等问题，性能远未达到满意水平。需要一种既能降低标注成本又能避免现有问题的新方法。

Method: 1. 使用少量标注数据进行监督RL初始化预热模型；2. 对困难未标注数据采用分治伪标注策略：低不确定性案例使用一致性选择，中等不确定性案例使用反思解析；3. 难度渐进的自训练结合迭代伪标注和RL进一步增强模型推理能力

Result: 在数学和科学基准测试中，EasyRL仅使用10%简单标注数据，在数据效率方面持续超越最先进的基线方法

Conclusion: EasyRL提供了一个统一的自进化框架，通过模拟人类认知获取曲线，实现了LLM的高效后训练，为解决标注成本高和模型性能不足问题提供了有效方案

Abstract: Previous LLMs-based RL studies typically follow either supervised learning with high annotation costs, or unsupervised paradigms using voting or entropy-based rewards. However, their performance remains far from satisfactory due to the substantial annotation cost and issues such as model collapse or reward hacking. To address these issues, we introduce a new perspective inspired by cognitive learning theory and propose a novel approach called EasyRL. The core of EasyRL is to simulate the human cognitive acquisition curve by integrating reliable knowledge transfer from easy labeled data with a progressive divide-and-conquer strategy that tackles increasingly difficult unlabeled data. Specifically, we initialize a warm-up model using supervised RL with few-shot labeled data. This is followed by a divide-and-conquer pseudo-labeling strategy on difficult unlabeled data, combining consistency-based selection for low-uncertainty cases and reflection-based resolution for medium-uncertainty cases. Finally, difficulty-progressive self-training with iterative pseudo-labeling and RL further strengthens the model's reasoning capability. EasyRL provides a unified self-evolving framework that facilitates data-efficient post-training of LLMs. Experimental results on mathematical and scientific benchmarks demonstrate that EasyRL, using only 10% of easy labeled data, consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [36] [Local Linearity of LLMs Enables Activation Steering via Model-Based Linear Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19018)
*Julian Skifstad,Xinyue Annie Yang,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于线性二次调节器（LQR）的闭环激活引导方法，将LLM推理建模为线性时变动力系统，通过层间雅可比矩阵计算反馈控制器，实现语义设定点的精确跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时LLM对齐方法（特别是激活引导）存在两个主要问题：1）非预期性干预忽略了扰动在Transformer层间的传播；2）缺乏在线误差反馈，导致次优的开环控制。需要一种能够考虑动态传播并提供反馈的闭环控制方法。

Method: 基于Transformer层间动态在局部线性近似的经验发现，将LLM推理建模为线性时变动力系统。采用经典线性二次调节器（LQR）框架，利用层间雅可比矩阵计算反馈控制器。提出自适应语义特征设定点信号，在闭环中引导激活向量，无需离线训练且计算开销最小。

Result: 方法在多个LLM架构和规模上验证有效，实现了对毒性、真实性、拒绝行为和任意概念的精细行为控制，性能超过基线引导方法。理论推导了设定点跟踪误差的边界，为引导性能提供了形式化保证。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM推理建模为线性动力系统并应用LQR控制理论，实现了高效、鲁棒的闭环激活引导。该方法为推理时对齐提供了理论保证和实际性能提升，超越了现有开环方法。

Abstract: Inference-time LLM alignment methods, particularly activation steering, offer an alternative to fine-tuning by directly modifying activations during generation. Existing methods, however, often rely on non-anticipative interventions that ignore how perturbations propagate through transformer layers and lack online error feedback, resulting in suboptimal, open-loop control. To address this, we show empirically that, despite the nonlinear structure of transformer blocks, layer-wise dynamics across multiple LLM architectures and scales are well-approximated by locally-linear models. Exploiting this property, we model LLM inference as a linear time-varying dynamical system and adapt the classical linear quadratic regulator to compute feedback controllers using layer-wise Jacobians, steering activations toward desired semantic setpoints in closed-loop with minimal computational overhead and no offline training. We also derive theoretical bounds on setpoint tracking error, enabling formal guarantees on steering performance. Using a novel adaptive semantic feature setpoint signal, our method yields robust, fine-grained behavior control across models, scales, and tasks, including state-of-the-art modulation of toxicity, truthfulness, refusal, and arbitrary concepts, surpassing baseline steering methods. Our code is available at: https://github.com/trustworthyrobotics/lqr-activation-steering

</details>


### [37] [Curiosity-Critic: Cumulative Prediction Error Improvement as a Tractable Intrinsic Reward for World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18701)
*Vin Bhaskara,Haicheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Curiosity-Critic方法，将内在奖励基于世界模型在所有访问转移上的累积预测误差改进，简化为可处理的每步形式：当前预测误差与当前状态转移的渐近误差基线之差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于局部预测误差的好奇心奖励只关注当前转移，未考虑世界模型在所有访问转移上的累积预测误差。需要一种能在线分离认知（可减少）和偶然（不可减少）预测误差的方法。

Method: 引入Curiosity-Critic，其内在奖励基于累积目标改进，简化为当前预测误差与当前状态转移渐近误差基线之差。通过在线学习的critic与world model协同训练来估计基线，critic回归单个标量，在世界模型饱和前收敛，将探索导向可学习的转移。

Result: 在随机网格世界实验中，Curiosity-Critic在收敛速度和最终世界模型准确性方面优于预测误差和访问计数基线方法。奖励对可学习转移更高，对随机转移则收敛到基线，有效在线分离认知和偶然预测误差。

Conclusion: Curiosity-Critic通过基于累积预测误差改进的内在奖励，提供了一种可处理的每步形式，能有效分离认知和偶然预测误差。先前从Schmidhuber到学习特征空间变体的预测误差好奇心公式，都作为特定基线近似的特例出现。

Abstract: Local prediction-error-based curiosity rewards focus on the current transition without considering the world model's cumulative prediction error across all visited transitions. We introduce Curiosity-Critic, which grounds its intrinsic reward in the improvement of this cumulative objective, and show that it reduces to a tractable per-step form: the difference between the current prediction error and the asymptotic error baseline of the current state transition. We estimate this baseline online with a learned critic co-trained alongside the world model; regressing a single scalar, the critic converges well before the world model saturates, redirecting exploration toward learnable transitions without oracle knowledge of the noise floor. The reward is higher for learnable transitions and collapses toward the baseline for stochastic ones, effectively separating epistemic (reducible) from aleatoric (irreducible) prediction error online. Prior prediction-error curiosity formulations, from Schmidhuber (1991) to learned-feature-space variants, emerge as special cases corresponding to specific approximations of this baseline. Experiments on a stochastic grid world show that Curiosity-Critic outperforms prediction-error and visitation-count baselines in convergence speed and final world model accuracy.

</details>


### [38] [Revisiting RaBitQ and TurboQuant: A Symmetric Comparison of Methods, Theory, and Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19528)
*Jianyang Gao,Yutong Gou,Yuexuan Xu,Jifan Shi,Yongyi Yang,Shuolin Li,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong,Cheng Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该技术笔记在统一比较框架下重新审视RaBitQ和TurboQuant的关系，发现TurboQuant并未在可比设置中持续优于RaBitQ，且存在实验结果不可复现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清RaBitQ和TurboQuant两种量化方法之间的真实关系，解决文献中关于TurboQuant优势的争议，并通过可复现、透明、对称的实验设置进行客观比较。

Method: 建立统一的比较框架，从方法论、理论保证和实证性能三个维度对比两种方法。采用可复现、透明、对称的实验设置，对两种方法进行系统性评估。

Result: TurboQuant并未在直接可比设置中提供对RaBitQ的一致改进；在许多测试配置中，其表现甚至不如RaBitQ。同时发现TurboQuant论文中报告的多个运行时和召回率结果无法从其发布的实现中复现。

Conclusion: 该研究澄清了两种方法之间的共享结构和真实差异，同时记录了TurboQuant论文中实验结果的复现性问题。结果表明TurboQuant的优势被夸大，RaBitQ在某些情况下表现更优。

Abstract: This technical note revisits the relationship between RaBitQ and TurboQuant under a unified comparison framework. We compare the two methods in terms of methodology, theoretical guarantees, and empirical performance, using a reproducible, transparent, and symmetric setup. Our results show that, despite the claimed advantage of TurboQuant, TurboQuant does not provide a consistent improvement over RaBitQ in directly comparable settings; in many tested configurations, it performs worse than RaBitQ.
