This inconsistency is widely acknowledged but poorly understood. Practitioners note that NPs work well despite the violation, without quantifying what this means. We address this gap by defining the conditioning consistency gap, a KL divergence measuring how much a conditional neural process's (CNP) predictions change when a point is added to the context versus conditioned upon. Our main results show that for CNPs with bounded encoders and Lipschitz decoders, the consistency gap is $O(1/n^2)$ in context size $n$, and that this rate is tight. These bounds establish the precise sense in which CNPs approximate valid stochastic processes. The inconsistency is negligible for moderate context sizes but can be significant in the few-shot regime.

</details>


### [77] [RDP LoRA: Geometry-Driven Identification for Parameter-Efficient Adaptation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19321)
*Yusuf Çelebi,Yağız Asker,Özay Ezerceli,Mahmoud ElHussieni,Selva Taş,Reyhan Bayraktar,Fatma Betül Terzioğlu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于几何轨迹分析的RDP算法，用于识别LLM隐藏状态演化中的关键转折点，并以此指导LoRA微调中的层选择，在Qwen3-8B-Base上仅微调13个RDP选定的层就在MMLU-Math上取得了优于全层微调的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在LoRA等参数高效微调方法，但LLM微调仍存在结构不确定性，因为内部表示的层特定作用理解不足，导致关于适应层选择的启发式决策。需要更系统的方法来理解表示演化并指导层选择。

Method: 将隐藏状态演化建模为高维几何轨迹，使用Ramer-Douglas-Peucker（RDP）算法（一种无需参数和训练的多边形简化方法）识别表示路径上的关键转折点。这些几何枢轴点作为直接决策信号，用于确定在参数高效微调中应适应哪些层。将该几何感知层选择策略集成到Qwen3-8B-Base的LoRA微调中。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B-Base上，仅微调13个RDP选定的层就在MMLU-Math上取得了81.67%的准确率，显著优于全36层适应（79.32%）、随机13层选择（75.56%）和基线模型（74.25%）。

Conclusion: 利用表示轨迹的内在几何特性为模型适应期间的层选择优化提供了鲁棒、可解释且无需训练的信号，证明了基于几何的层选择策略的有效性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) remains structurally uncertain despite parameter-efficient methods such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), as the layer-specific roles of internal representations are poorly understood, leading to heuristic decisions about where adaptation should be applied. We model the evolution of hidden states as a high-dimensional geometric trajectory and propose using the Ramer-Douglas-Peucker (RDP) algorithm, a parameter-free and training-free polygon simplification method that preserves global structural transitions while eliminating locally redundant changes, to identify critical breakpoints along the representation path. Crucially, we use these geometric pivots not merely for analysis, but as a direct decision signal for determining which layers should be adapted during parameter-efficient fine-tuning. By integrating this geometry-aware layer selection strategy into LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen3-8B-Base, we achieve superior performance on MMLU-Math using only 13 RDP-selected layers (81.67%), significantly outperforming both full 36-layer adaptation (79.32%) and random 13-layer selection (75.56%), as well as the baseline Qwen3-8B-Base model (74.25%). These results demonstrate that leveraging the intrinsic geometry of representation trajectories provides a robust, interpretable, and training-free signal for optimizing layer selection during model adaptation.

</details>


### [78] [Concept Inconsistency in Dermoscopic Concept Bottleneck Models: A Rough-Set Analysis of the Derm7pt Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19323)
*Gonzalo Nápoles,Isel Grau,Yamisleydi Salgueiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文应用粗糙集理论分析Derm7pt皮肤镜数据集，发现概念瓶颈模型因概念标签不一致性存在92.1%的理论精度上限，提出过滤策略创建完全一致的Derm7pt+子集，并在19个骨干架构上评估硬概念瓶颈模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型(CBMs)通过临床概念层进行预测，但当数据集中存在概念级不一致时（相同概念配置对应冲突的诊断标签），会形成无法解决的概念瓶颈，对可达到的精度设置硬性上限。本研究旨在量化这种不一致性的程度和临床结构。

Method: 1. 应用粗糙集理论分析Derm7pt皮肤镜基准数据集；2. 识别概念不一致性的完整范围和临床结构；3. 提出两种过滤策略（对称和不对称）处理边界区域图像；4. 在19个骨干架构（EfficientNet、DenseNet、ResNet、Wide ResNet）上评估硬概念瓶颈模型。

Result: 1. 在305个由7点黑色素瘤检查清单形成的独特概念配置中，50个（16.4%）存在不一致性，涉及306张图像（数据集的30.3%）；2. 理论精度上限为92.1%；3. 对称过滤创建了Derm7pt+子集（705张图像），完全一致；4. 在对称过滤下，EfficientNet-B5获得最佳标签F1分数（0.85）和标签准确率（0.90）；5. 在不对称过滤下，EfficientNet-B7在所有四个指标上领先，标签F1分数为0.82。

Conclusion: 概念标签不一致性对概念瓶颈模型设置了硬性精度上限，通过粗糙集分析和过滤策略可以创建完全一致的数据集子集。研究为皮肤镜数据上的概念一致CBM评估建立了可复现的基线，强调了数据质量对模型解释性和性能的重要性。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) route predictions exclusively through a clinically grounded concept layer, binding interpretability to concept-label consistency. When a dataset contains concept-level inconsistencies, identical concept profiles mapped to conflicting diagnosis labels create an unresolvable bottleneck that imposes a hard ceiling on achievable accuracy. In this paper, we apply rough set theory to the Derm7pt dermoscopy benchmark and characterize the full extent and clinical structure of this inconsistency. Among 305 unique concept profiles formed by the 7 dermoscopic criteria of the 7-point melanoma checklist, 50 (16.4%) are inconsistent, spanning 306 images (30.3% of the dataset). This yields a theoretical accuracy ceiling of 92.1%, independent of backbone architecture or training strategy for CBMs that exclusively operate with hard concepts. In addition, we characterize the conflict-severity distribution, identify the clinical features most responsible for boundary ambiguity, and evaluate two filtering strategies with quantified effects on dataset composition and CBM interpretability. Symmetric removal of all boundary-region images yields Derm7pt+, a fully consistent benchmark subset of 705 images with perfect quality of classification and no hard accuracy ceiling. Building on this filtered dataset, we present a hard CBM evaluated across 19 backbone architectures from the EfficientNet, DenseNet, ResNet, and Wide ResNet families. Under symmetric filtering, explored for completeness, EfficientNet-B5 achieves the best label F1 score (0.85) and label accuracy (0.90) on the held-out test set, with a concept accuracy of 0.70. Under asymmetric filtering, EfficientNet-B7 leads across all four metrics, reaching a label F1 score of 0.82 and concept accuracy of 0.70. These results establish reproducible baselines for concept-consistent CBM evaluation on dermoscopic data.

</details>


### [79] [When Active Learning Falls Short: An Empirical Study on Chemical Reaction Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19335)
*Simin Yu,Sufia Fathima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统研究化学文献中反应提取的主动学习方法，评估六种基于不确定性和多样性的策略与预训练transformer-CRF架构的结合效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学文献快速增长产生大量非结构化数据，其中反应信息对反应预测和药物设计等应用价值重大，但专家标注成本高昂导致训练数据稀缺，严重阻碍自动反应提取性能

Method: 整合六种基于不确定性和多样性的主动学习策略与预训练transformer-CRF架构，在产物提取和角色标注任务上进行评估

Result: 多种方法能用较少标注实例接近全数据性能，但学习曲线常呈现非单调性和任务依赖性；强预训练、结构化CRF解码和标签稀疏性限制了传统主动学习策略的稳定性

Conclusion: 研究结果为化学信息提取中有效使用主动学习提供了实用见解，揭示了现有方法在特定条件下的局限性

Abstract: The rapid growth of chemical literature has generated vast amounts of unstructured data, where reaction information is particularly valuable for applications such as reaction predictions and drug design. However, the prohibitive cost of expert annotation has led to a scarcity of training data, severely hindering the performance of automatic reaction extraction. In this work, we conduct a systematic study of active learning for chemical reaction extraction. We integrate six uncertainty- and diversity-based strategies with pretrained transformer-CRF architectures, and evaluate them on product extraction and role labeling task. While several methods approach full-data performance with fewer labeled instances, learning curves are often non-monotonic and task-dependent. Our analysis shows that strong pretraining, structured CRF decoding, and label sparsity limit the stability of conventional active learning strategies. These findings provide practical insights for the effective use of active learning in chemical information extraction.

</details>


### [80] [Evaluation-driven Scaling for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19341)
*Haotian Ye,Haowei Lin,Jingyi Tang,Yizhen Luo,Caiyin Yang,Chang Su,Rahul Thapa,Rui Yang,Ruihua Liu,Zeyu Li,Chong Gao,Dachao Ding,Guangrong He,Miaolei Zhang,Lina Sun,Wenyang Wang,Yuchen Zhong,Zhuohao Shen,Di He,Jianzhu Ma,Stefano Ermon,Tongyang Li,Xiaowen Chu,James Zou,Yuzhi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SimpleTES框架通过规模化评估驱动发现循环，在21个科学问题上实现SOTA解决方案，显著提升LASSO算法速度2倍以上，量子电路门开销降低24.5%，并发现新的Erdos最小重叠构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽强调评估在科学发现中的重要性，但未系统解决如何规模化评估驱动发现循环以推动科学边界的问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供原则性、有效的规模化框架。

Method: 提出SimpleTES框架，战略性地结合并行探索、反馈驱动精炼和局部选择，通过沿正确维度规模化评估驱动发现循环来解锁显著增益。

Result: 在6个领域的21个科学问题上，SimpleTES使用gpt-oss模型发现SOTA解决方案，持续优于前沿模型基线和复杂优化流程。具体成果包括：LASSO算法加速2倍以上，量子电路路由策略减少24.5%门开销，发现超越最佳已知结果的Erdos最小重叠构造。

Conclusion: 有效的评估驱动循环规模化是推进LLM驱动科学发现的核心轴，SimpleTES为实现这些增益提供了简单实用的框架。轨迹级历史记录可监督反馈驱动学习，后训练模型不仅能提高已见问题效率，还能泛化到未见问题。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used in scientific discovery to generate hypotheses, propose candidate solutions, implement systems, and iteratively refine them. At the core of these trial-and-error loops lies evaluation: the process of obtaining feedback on candidate solutions via verifiers, simulators, or task-specific scoring functions. While prior work has highlighted the importance of evaluation, it has not explicitly formulated the problem of how evaluation-driven discovery loops can be scaled up in a principled and effective manner to push the boundaries of scientific discovery, a problem this paper seeks to address. We introduce Simple Test-time Evaluation-driven Scaling (SimpleTES), a general framework that strategically combines parallel exploration, feedback-driven refinement, and local selection, revealing substantial gains unlocked by scaling evaluation-driven discovery loops along the right dimensions. Across 21 scientific problems spanning six domains, SimpleTES discovers state-of-the-art solutions using gpt-oss models, consistently outperforming both frontier-model baselines and sophisticated optimization pipelines. Particularly, we sped up the widely used LASSO algorithm by over 2x, designed quantum circuit routing policies that reduce gate overhead by 24.5%, and discovered new Erdos minimum overlap constructions that surpass the best-known results. Beyond novel discoveries, SimpleTES produces trajectory-level histories that naturally supervise feedback-driven learning. When post-trained on successful trajectories, models not only improve efficiency on seen problems but also generalize to unseen problems, discovering solutions that base models fail to uncover. Together, our results establish effective evaluation-driven loop scaling as a central axis for advancing LLM-driven scientific discovery, and provide a simple yet practical framework for realizing these gains.

</details>


### [81] [LASER: Learning Active Sensing for Continuum Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19355)
*Huayu Deng,Jinghui Zhong,Xiangming Zhu,Yunbo Wang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LASER是一个闭环主动感知框架，将连续物理场重建建模为POMDP问题，使用潜在世界模型和强化学习策略动态优化传感器布局


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续物理场重建方法依赖固定传感器布局，无法适应动态物理状态变化，在稀疏和受限感知条件下难以实现高保真度测量

Method: 提出LASER框架：1) 将主动感知建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)；2) 使用连续物理场潜在世界模型捕捉底层物理动力学；3) 通过强化学习策略在潜在想象空间中模拟"假设"感知场景；4) 基于预测潜在状态条件化传感器移动，导航到潜在高信息区域

Result: 实验表明LASER在多种连续物理场中一致优于静态和离线优化策略，在稀疏感知条件下实现高保真度重建

Conclusion: LASER提供了一个统一的闭环主动感知框架，能够自适应地优化传感器布局，显著提高稀疏感知条件下的连续物理场重建质量

Abstract: High-fidelity measurements of continuum physical fields are essential for scientific discovery and engineering design but remain challenging under sparse and constrained sensing. Conventional reconstruction methods typically rely on fixed sensor layouts, which cannot adapt to evolving physical states. We propose LASER, a unified, closed-loop framework that formulates active sensing as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). At its core, LASER employs a continuum field latent world model that captures the underlying physical dynamics and provides intrinsic reward feedback. This enables a reinforcement learning policy to simulate ''what-if'' sensing scenarios within a latent imagination space. By conditioning sensor movements on predicted latent states, LASER navigates toward potentially high-information regions beyond current observations. Our experiments demonstrate that LASER consistently outperforms static and offline-optimized strategies, achieving high-fidelity reconstruction under sparsity across diverse continuum fields.

</details>


### [82] [FairTree: Subgroup Fairness Auditing of Machine Learning Models with Bias-Variance Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19357)
*Rudolf Debelak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairTree：一种基于心理测量不变性测试的新算法，用于检测机器学习模型在不同子组中的性能差异，可直接处理连续、分类和有序特征，无需离散化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习模型评估主要依赖基于损失函数的性能指标，容易忽略相关子组中的性能变化。现有审计工具如SliceFinder和SliceLine存在概念缺陷，特别是无法直接处理连续协变量。

Method: 提出FairTree算法，基于心理测量不变性测试。算法直接处理连续、分类和有序特征，无需离散化，并将性能差异分解为系统性偏差和方差。提出两种变体：基于置换的方法（概念上更接近SliceFinder）和波动测试。

Result: 通过模拟研究（包括与SliceLine的直接比较）表明，两种方法都有令人满意的假阳性率，但波动测试方法具有相对更高的检验力。在UCI成人人口普查数据集上进行了方法演示。

Conclusion: FairTree算法为机器学习模型的性能和公平性方面的统计评估提供了一个灵活框架，即使在相对较小的数据集中也能在广泛的应用中使用。

Abstract: The evaluation of machine learning models typically relies mainly on performance metrics based on loss functions, which risk to overlook changes in performance in relevant subgroups. Auditing tools such as SliceFinder and SliceLine were proposed to detect such groups, but usually have conceptual disadvantages, such as the inability to directly address continuous covariates. In this paper, we introduce FairTree, a novel algorithm adapted from psychometric invariance testing. Unlike SliceFinder and related algorithms, FairTree directly handles continuous, categorical, and ordinal features without discretization. It further decomposes performance disparities into systematic bias and variance, allowing a categorization of changes in algorithm performance. We propose and evaluate two variations of the algorithm: a permutation-based approach, which is conceptually closer to SliceFinder, and a fluctuation test. Through simulation studies that include a direct comparison with SliceLine, we demonstrate that both approaches have a satisfactory rate of false-positive results, but that the fluctuation approach has relatively higher power. We further illustrate the method on the UCI Adult Census dataset. The proposed algorithms provide a flexible framework for the statistical evaluation of the performance and aspects of fairness of machine learning models in a wide range of applications even in relatively small data.

</details>


### [83] [TACENR: Task-Agnostic Contrastive Explanations for Node Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19372)
*Vasiliki Papanikou,Evaggelia Pitoura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACENR：一种任务无关的对比解释方法，用于识别节点表示中最重要的属性、邻近和结构特征


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表示学习方法生成的节点表示缺乏可解释性，现有解释方法主要关注监督设置或单个维度解释，缺乏对节点表示整体结构的解释能力

Method: 基于对比学习学习表示空间中的相似性函数，识别对节点表示最重要的属性特征、邻近特征和结构特征，支持任务无关解释，也可应用于监督场景

Result: 实验表明邻近和结构特征在塑造节点表示中起重要作用，监督变体在识别最有影响特征方面与现有任务特定方法表现相当

Conclusion: TACENR填补了节点表示整体结构解释的空白，为图表示学习提供了更全面的可解释性工具

Abstract: Graph representation learning has achieved notable success in encoding graph-structured data into latent vector spaces, enabling a wide range of downstream tasks. However, these node representations remain opaque and difficult to interpret. Existing explainability methods primarily focus on supervised settings or on explaining individual representation dimensions, leaving a critical gap in explaining the overall structure of node representations. In this paper, we propose TACENR (Task-Agnostic Contrastive Explanations for Node Representations), a local explanation method that identifies not only attribute features but also proximity and structural ones that contribute the most in the representation space. TACENR builds on contrastive learning, through which we learn a similarity function in the representation space, revealing which are the features that play an important role in the representation of a node. While our focus is on task-agnostic explanations, TACENR can be applied to supervised scenarios as well. Experimental results demonstrate that proximity and structural features play a significant role in shaping node representations and that our supervised variant performs comparably to existing task-specific approaches in identifying the most impactful features.

</details>


### [84] [Revisiting Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19401)
*Gerard Pons,Carlos Escolano,Besim Bilalli,Anna Queralt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出当前连续知识图谱嵌入方法忽略了实体干扰问题，导致对灾难性遗忘的评估存在偏差，提出了修正的评估协议和专用度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前连续知识图谱嵌入方法主要关注限制现有嵌入的变化来缓解灾难性遗忘，但忽略了新实体引入时产生的实体干扰问题，导致评估不准确且性能被高估。

Method: 提出了修正的连续知识图谱嵌入评估协议，考虑实体干扰效应；分析了不同CKGE方法和KGE模型受各种遗忘源的影响；引入了专门针对CKGE的灾难性遗忘度量指标。

Result: 实验表明忽略实体干扰会导致性能被高估达25%，特别是在实体显著增长的情况下；不同CKGE方法和模型受遗忘影响程度不同。

Conclusion: 实体干扰是连续知识图谱嵌入中一个被忽视的重要问题，需要专门的评估协议和度量指标来准确评估灾难性遗忘，这对CKGE方法的开发和评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Embeddings (KGEs) support a wide range of downstream tasks over Knowledge Graphs (KGs). In practice, KGs evolve as new entities and facts are added, motivating Continual Knowledge Graph Embedding (CKGE) methods that update embeddings over time. Current CKGE approaches address catastrophic forgetting (i.e., the performance degradation on previously learned tasks) primarily by limiting changes to existing embeddings.
  However, we show that this view is incomplete. When new entities are introduced, their embeddings can interfere with previously learned ones, causing the model to predict them in place of previously correct answers. This phenomenon, which we call entity interference, has been largely overlooked and is not accounted for in current CKGE evaluation protocols. As a result, the assessment of catastrophic forgetting becomes misleading, and CKGE methods performance is systematically overestimated.
  To address this issue, we introduce a corrected CKGE evaluation protocol that accounts for entity interference. Through experiments on multiple benchmarks, we show that ignoring this effect can lead to performance overestimation of up to 25%, particularly in scenarios with significant entity growth. We further analyze how different CKGE methods and KGE models are affected by the different sources of forgetting, and introduce a catastrophic forgetting metric tailored to CKGE.

</details>


### [85] [Heterogeneity-Aware Personalized Federated Learning for Industrial Predictive Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19451)
*Yuhan Hu,Xiaolei Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出个性化联邦预测模型，解决传统联邦学习中退化过程同质性假设不成立的问题，通过相似退化模式客户间的成对协作实现个性化模型构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦预测模型假设各客户（公司、工厂、生产线）的退化过程具有同质性，但在许多工业场景中这一假设不成立。需要一种能够适应异构退化过程的个性化联邦预测模型，使客户能够构建定制化的预测模型。

Method: 提出个性化联邦预测模型，通过迭代促进具有相似退化模式的客户之间的成对协作来增强个性化联邦学习性能。开发基于近端梯度下降的联邦参数估计算法，使用分散数据集联合估计参数。

Result: 通过广泛的仿真研究和NASA存储库中涡扇发动机退化数据集的案例研究验证了所提模型的优越性。该模型能够同时实现模型个性化、保护数据隐私并提供全面的故障时间分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的个性化联邦预测模型克服了现有联邦预测模型的局限性，能够适应异构退化过程，在保持数据隐私的同时实现个性化建模，为工业应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated prognostics enable clients (e.g., companies, factories, and production lines) to collaboratively develop a failure time prediction model while keeping each client's data local and confidential. However, traditional federated models often assume homogeneity in the degradation processes across clients, an assumption that may not hold in many industrial settings. To overcome this, this paper proposes a personalized federated prognostic model designed to accommodate clients with heterogeneous degradation processes, allowing them to build tailored prognostic models. The prognostic model iteratively facilitates the underlying pairwise collaborations between clients with similar degradation patterns, which enhances the performance of personalized federated learning. To estimate parameters jointly using decentralized datasets, we develop a federated parameter estimation algorithm based on proximal gradient descent. The proposed approach addresses the limitations of existing federated prognostic models by simultaneously achieving model personalization, preserving data privacy, and providing comprehensive failure time distributions. The superiority of the proposed model is validated through extensive simulation studies and a case study using the turbofan engine degradation dataset from the NASA repository.

</details>


### [86] [EVPO: Explained Variance Policy Optimization for Adaptive Critic Utilization in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19485)
*Chengjun Pan,Shichun Liu,Jiahang Lin,Dingwei Zhu,Jiazheng Zhang,Shihan Dou,Songyang Gao,Zhenhua Han,Binghai Wang,Rui Zheng,Xuanjing Huang,Tao Gui,Yansong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVPO：基于解释方差的自适应基线选择方法，通过监控批处理级别的解释方差，在稀疏奖励场景中动态切换基于评论家的优势估计和批均值优势估计，实现比PPO和GRPO更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM后训练的强化学习中，基于评论家的方法（如PPO）和免评论家方法（如GRPO）各有优劣。传统理论认为评论家能减少方差，但在稀疏奖励场景中，评论家的估计噪声可能超过其捕获的状态信号，反而增加优势方差。需要一种理论指导的方法来自适应选择最优基线策略。

Method: 将基线选择建模为卡尔曼滤波问题，将PPO和GRPO统一为卡尔曼增益的两个极端。提出解释方差（EV）作为判断标准：正EV表示评论家减少方差，零或负EV表示评论家增加方差。在此基础上提出EVPO方法，在每个训练步骤监控批处理级别的EV，自适应地在基于评论家的优势估计和批均值优势估计之间切换。

Result: 在四个任务（经典控制、智能体交互、数学推理）上的实验表明，EVPO始终优于PPO和GRPO，无论哪种固定基线在特定任务上更强。进一步分析证实自适应门控能跟踪评论家在训练过程中的成熟度，且理论推导的零阈值在经验上是最优的。

Conclusion: EVPO通过理论推导的解释方差指标，实现了在稀疏奖励场景中自适应选择最优基线策略，解决了评论家方法在稀疏奖励下可能增加方差的问题，为LLM后训练的强化学习提供了更鲁棒的优化框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for LLM post-training faces a fundamental design choice: whether to use a learned critic as a baseline for policy optimization. Classical theory favors critic-based methods such as PPO for variance reduction, yet critic-free alternatives like GRPO have gained widespread adoption due to their simplicity and competitive performance. We show that in sparse-reward settings, a learned critic can inject estimation noise that exceeds the state signal it captures, increasing rather than reducing advantage variance. By casting baseline selection as a Kalman filtering problem, we unify PPO and GRPO as two extremes of the Kalman gain and prove that explained variance (EV), computable from a single training batch, identifies the exact boundary: positive EV indicates the critic reduces variance, while zero or negative EV signals that it inflates variance. Building on this insight, we propose Explained Variance Policy Optimization (EVPO), which monitors batch-level EV at each training step and adaptively switches between critic-based and batch-mean advantage estimation, provably achieving no greater variance than the better of the two at every step. Across four tasks spanning classical control, agentic interaction, and mathematical reasoning, EVPO consistently outperforms both PPO and GRPO regardless of which fixed baseline is stronger on a given task. Further analysis confirms that the adaptive gating tracks critic maturation over training and that the theoretically derived zero threshold is empirically optimal.

</details>


### [87] [Calibrating Scientific Foundation Models with Inference-Time Stochastic Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19530)
*Akash Yadav,Taiwo A. Adebiyi,Ruda Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Stochastic Attention方法，通过随机化注意力权重实现预测不确定性校准，无需重新训练即可生成预测集成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer基础模型在科学应用中缺乏校准的预测不确定性支持，当前架构输出确定性结果且不确定性校准有限

Method: 提出随机注意力机制，用归一化多项分布样本替换softmax权重，通过单一浓度参数控制随机性；引入校准目标匹配随机注意力输出与目标，形成高效单变量后调优问题

Result: 在天气和时间序列预测等科学基础模型上评估，相比不确定性感知基线，随机注意力实现最强原生校准和最尖锐预测区间，覆盖度相当，后调优仅需分钟而非基线所需数天重新训练

Conclusion: 随机注意力是轻量级推理时修改，能有效为Transformer基础模型提供校准的预测不确定性，在科学应用中具有实用价值

Abstract: Transformer-based scientific foundation models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, but current architectures give deterministic outputs and provide limited support for calibrated predictive uncertainty. We propose Stochastic Attention, a lightweight inference-time modification that randomizes attention by replacing softmax weights with normalized multinomial samples controlled by a single concentration parameter, and produces predictive ensembles without retraining. To set this parameter, we introduce a calibration objective that matches the stochastic attention output with the target, yielding an efficient univariate post-hoc tuning problem. We evaluate this mechanism on two scientific foundation models for weather and timeseries forecasting along with an additional regression task. Across benchmarks against uncertainty-aware baselines, we find that Stochastic Attention achieves the strongest native calibration and the sharpest prediction intervals at comparable coverage, while requiring only minutes of post-hoc tuning versus days of retraining for competitive baselines.

</details>


### [88] [Separating Geometry from Probability in the Analysis of Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19560)
*Maxim Raginsky,Benjamin Recht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于优化问题敏感性分析的确定性泛化分析框架，替代传统的概率统计方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习泛化分析依赖于无法验证的i.i.d.概率假设，需要一种更基础的确定性分析框架

Method: 通过优化问题解对数据扰动的敏感性分析，建立变分原理形式的确定性泛化界

Result: 获得不依赖概率假设的泛化界，将泛化误差与样本外数据接近样本内数据的程度联系起来

Conclusion: 提供了一种纯确定性的泛化分析视角，统计假设可作为后验工具用于分析误差项的大小

Abstract: The goal of machine learning is to find models that minimize prediction error on data that has not yet been seen. Its operational paradigm assumes access to a dataset $S$ and articulates a scheme for evaluating how well a given model performs on an arbitrary sample. The sample can be $S$ (in which case we speak of ``in-sample'' performance) or some entirely new $S'$ (in which case we speak of ``out-of-sample'' performance). Traditional analysis of generalization assumes that both in- and out-of-sample data are i.i.d.\ draws from an infinite population. However, these probabilistic assumptions cannot be verified even in principle. This paper presents an alternative view of generalization through the lens of sensitivity analysis of solutions of optimization problems to perturbations in the problem data. Under this framework, generalization bounds are obtained by purely deterministic means and take the form of variational principles that relate in-sample and out-of-sample evaluations through an error term that quantifies how close out-of-sample data are to in-sample data. Statistical assumptions can then be used \textit{ex post} to characterize the situations when this error term is small (either on average or with high probability).

</details>


### [89] [Structure-guided molecular design with contrastive 3D protein-ligand learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19562)
*Carles Navarro,Philipp Tholke,Gianni de Fabritiis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一框架结合对比3D结构编码与基于商业化合物空间的自回归分子生成，解决基于结构药物发现中准确捕获3D蛋白-配体相互作用与探索超大化学空间的双重挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结构的药物发现面临两个核心挑战：1）准确捕获3D蛋白-配体相互作用；2）在超大化学空间中导航以识别可合成候选分子。现有方法通常单独处理这两个问题，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 1）引入SE(3)-等变Transformer，通过对比学习将配体和口袋结构编码到共享嵌入空间；2）将这些嵌入集成到多模态化学语言模型（MCLM）中，该模型基于口袋或配体结构生成靶点特异性分子；3）使用学习的数据集令牌引导输出朝向目标化学空间。

Result: 1）对比学习编码在零样本虚拟筛选中取得竞争性结果；2）MCLM生成具有有利预测结合特性的候选分子，适用于多种靶点；3）框架能够生成针对特定靶点的分子，同时引导输出朝向目标化学空间。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功整合了3D结构编码与分子生成，通过结合SE(3)-等变对比编码和多模态条件生成，为基于结构的药物发现提供了同时处理结构准确性和化学空间探索的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Structure-based drug discovery faces the dual challenge of accurately capturing 3D protein-ligand interactions while navigating ultra-large chemical spaces to identify synthetically accessible candidates. In this work, we present a unified framework that addresses these challenges by combining contrastive 3D structure encoding with autoregressive molecular generation conditioned on commercial compound spaces. First, we introduce an SE(3)-equivariant transformer that encodes ligand and pocket structures into a shared embedding space via contrastive learning, achieving competitive results in zero-shot virtual screening. Second, we integrate these embeddings into a multimodal Chemical Language Model (MCLM). The model generates target-specific molecules conditioned on either pocket or ligand structures, with a learned dataset token that steers the output toward targeted chemical spaces, yielding candidates with favorable predicted binding properties across diverse targets.

</details>


### [90] [An Efficient Black-Box Reduction from Online Learning to Multicalibration, and a New Route to $Φ$-Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19592)
*Gabriele Farina,Juan Carlos Perdomo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了从在线学习到在线多校准的GGM风格黑盒约简，建立了多校准与期望变分不等式求解的等价关系，并提供了从外部遗憾到Φ-遗憾的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Garg等人(SODA '24)提出的主要开放问题：证明具有√T型保证的预言机高效在线多校准在完全一般性下是可能的。统一现有在线多校准算法的分析，并为具有延迟观测或审查结果的挑战性环境提供新算法。

Method: 1. 提出Gordon-Greenwald-Marks风格的从在线学习到在线多校准的黑盒约简，将任何无遗憾学习器与期望变分不等式求解器结合。2. 证明高效多校准意味着高效EVI求解，建立了EVI在多校准中的作用与GGM结果中固定点的类比。3. 提出从高维在线多校准到(上下文)Φ-遗憾最小化的细粒度约简。

Result: 1. 完全解决了Garg等人(SODA '24)的主要开放问题，证明了具有√T型保证的预言机高效在线多校准在完全一般性下是可能的。2. 统一了现有在线多校准算法的分析，为延迟观测或审查结果环境提供了新算法。3. 建立了从外部遗憾到Φ-遗憾的新路径，简化了Daskalakis等人(STOC '25)的结果并改进了速率。4. 产生了对更丰富偏差类(如再生核希尔伯特空间)具有鲁棒性的新算法。

Conclusion: 该论文通过建立在线学习与在线多校准之间的GGM风格约简，解决了该领域的关键开放问题，统一了现有分析框架，并为更广泛的偏差类和挑战性环境提供了新的算法工具。期望变分不等式在多校准中的作用被证明与GGM结果中固定点的作用类似，这一发现为理解多校准与遗憾最小化之间的关系提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We give a Gordon-Greenwald-Marks (GGM) style black-box reduction from online learning to online multicalibration. Concretely, we show that to achieve high-dimensional multicalibration with respect to a class of functions H, it suffices to combine any no-regret learner over H with an expected variational inequality (EVI) solver. We also prove a converse statement showing that efficient multicalibration implies efficient EVI solving, highlighting how EVIs in multicalibration mirror the role of fixed points in the GGM result for $Φ$-regret.
  This first set of results resolves the main open question in Garg, Jung, Reingold, and Roth (SODA '24), showing that oracle-efficient online multicalibration with $\sqrt{T}$-type guarantees is possible in full generality. Furthermore, our GGM-style reduction unifies the analyses of existing online multicalibration algorithms, enables new algorithms for challenging environments with delayed observations or censored outcomes, and yields the first efficient black-box reduction between online learning and multiclass omniprediction.
  Our second main result is a fine-grained reduction from high-dimensional online multicalibration to (contextual) $Φ$-regret minimization. Together with our first result, this establishes a new route from external regret to Phi-regret that bypasses sophisticated fixed-point or semi-separation machinery, dramatically simplifies a result of Daskalakis, Farina, Fishelson, Pipis, and Schneider (STOC '25) while improving rates, and yields new algorithms that are robust to richer deviation classes, such as those belonging to any reproducing kernel Hilbert space.

</details>


### [91] [SAGE: Training-Free Semantic Evidence Composition for Edge-Cloud Inference under Hard Uplink Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19623)
*Inhyeok Choi,Hyuncheol Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SAGE方法，在边缘-云混合推理中通过结合重要性过滤和嵌入多样性采样，在硬比特预算约束下优化传输内容选择，显著优于仅基于注意力的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘-云混合推理中，上行信道对传输比特数有硬约束。传统基于注意力重要性的内容选择方法在硬预算下存在固有局限性，需要更优的传输内容选择策略。

Method: 提出SAGE（语义注意力引导证据）方法，这是一种无需训练的方法，结合重要性过滤和嵌入多样性采样。首先基于注意力重要性筛选候选单元，然后通过嵌入空间的多样性采样选择最终传输集，确保覆盖输入的不同语义方面。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K数据集上，SAGE仅传输不到一半可用证据单元就达到服务器上限精度的93%，显著优于仅基于重要性的组合方法。实验表明空间覆盖本身具有独立价值，均匀选择在中等预算下也能达到竞争性精度。

Conclusion: 在硬比特预算约束下，传输内容选择不应仅基于个体重要性，而应注重集合对输入不同方面的覆盖度。SAGE通过结合重要性过滤和多样性采样，实现了更有效的边缘-云推理卸载。

Abstract: Edge-cloud hybrid inference offloads difficult inputs to a powerful remote model, but the uplink channel imposes hard per-request constraints on the number of bits that can be transmitted. We show that selecting transmitted content based solely on attention-based importance, the standard approach in collaborative inference, is inherently limited under hard budgets. Two findings support this claim. First, replacing high-importance units with low-importance but complementary ones improves server accuracy. This shows that what matters is not individual importance but how well the transmitted set covers diverse aspects of the input. Second, spatially uniform selection without any content information achieves competitive accuracy at moderate budgets. This confirms that spatial coverage alone carries independent value. Based on this analysis, we propose SAGE (Semantic Attention-Guided Evidence), a principled, training-free method that combines importance filtering with embedding-diversity sampling. SAGE achieves 93% of the server ceiling in offloaded accuracy while transmitting fewer than half of the available evidence units on ImageNet-1K, substantially outperforming importance-only composition.

</details>


### [92] [Disentangling Damage from Operational Variability: A Label-Free Self-Supervised Representation Learning Framework for Output-Only Structural Damage Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19658)
*Xudong Jian,Charikleia Stoura,Simon Scandella,Eleni Chatzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督无标签解耦表示学习框架，用于基于振动的结构损伤识别，通过解耦损伤相关特征与干扰变量，提高在实际变化条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构健康监测中的损伤识别在实际应用中常受到非损伤因素（如激励和环境条件变化）的干扰，这些变化可能比损伤引起的变化更大，从而降低识别可靠性。需要一种能够区分损伤相关特征与干扰变量的鲁棒方法。

Method: 采用自编码器架构，学习两个潜在表示。通过VICReg自监督不变性正则化，使一个潜在表示对基线数据中的操作和环境变化保持不变。引入频域约束，确保潜在表示重建的功率谱密度与输入时间序列一致。这些机制共同促进表示解耦，使学习到的表示对损伤敏感而对干扰变量不变。框架以端到端、无标签方式训练，无需损伤、激励或环境条件的先验信息。

Result: 在两个真实振动数据集（桥梁和齿轮箱）上验证了有效性。结果表明：对操作变化具有鲁棒性，强泛化能力，在损伤检测和量化方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 提出的自监督无标签解耦表示学习框架能够有效分离损伤相关特征与干扰变量，在实际变化条件下实现鲁棒的振动基结构损伤识别，适用于实际应用场景。

Abstract: Damage identification is a core task in structural health monitoring. In practice, however, its reliability is often compromised by confounding non-damage effects, such as variations in excitation and environmental conditions, which can induce changes comparable to or larger than those caused by structural damage. To address this challenge, this study proposes a self-supervised label-free disentangled representation learning framework for robust vibration-based structural damage identification. The proposed framework employs an autoencoder with two latent representations to learn directly from raw vibration acceleration signals. A self-supervised invariance regularization, implemented via Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization (VICReg), is imposed on one latent representation using baseline data where structural damage is assumed constant but operational and environmental conditions vary. In addition, a frequency-domain constraint is introduced to enforce agreement between the power spectral density reconstructed from the latent representation and that computed from the corresponding input time series. Together, these mechanisms promote disentanglement, enabling the learned representation to be sensitive to damage-related characteristics while remaining invariant to nuisance variability. The framework is trained in a fully end-to-end and label-free manner, requiring no prior information on damage, excitation, or environmental conditions, making it well-suited for real-world applications. Its effectiveness is validated on two distinct real-world vibration datasets, including a bridge and a gearbox. The results demonstrate robustness to operational variability, strong generalization capability, and good performance in both damage detection and quantification.

</details>


### [93] [Budgeted Online Influence Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19672)
*Pierre Perrault,Jennifer Healey,Zheng Wen,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预算约束下的在线影响力最大化新框架，用总成本替代传统基数约束，更贴近真实广告投放场景，在独立级联模型和边级半赌博反馈下提供算法与理论分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线影响力最大化通常采用基数约束（选择固定数量的影响者），但实际广告活动中影响者成本各异，广告主更关注整体预算的最优价值分配，需要更贴近现实的预算约束框架

Method: 提出新算法，基于独立级联扩散模型和边级半赌博反馈，考虑影响者成本差异，以总成本为约束条件进行在线影响力最大化

Result: 提供理论分析和实验结果，证明算法有效性；该分析同样适用于基数约束场景，并在该场景下改进了现有最优遗憾界

Conclusion: 预算约束框架更贴近实际广告投放需求，提出的算法在理论和实验上均表现良好，同时为传统基数约束问题提供了改进的遗憾界

Abstract: We introduce a new budgeted framework for online influence maximization, considering the total cost of an advertising campaign instead of the common cardinality constraint on a chosen influencer set. Our approach better models the real-world setting where the cost of influencers varies and advertisers want to find the best value for their overall social advertising budget. We propose an algorithm assuming an independent cascade diffusion model and edge level semi-bandit feedback, and provide both theoretical and experimental results. Our analysis is also valid for the cardinality constraint setting and improves the state of the art regret bound in this case.

</details>


### [94] [PREF-XAI: Preference-Based Personalized Rule Explanations of Black-Box Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19684)
*Salvatore Greco,Jacek Karolczak,Roman Słowiński,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PREF-XAI：将可解释人工智能重新定义为基于偏好的决策问题，通过偏好学习生成个性化解释


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法主要生成模型中心的解释，忽略了不同用户因目标、偏好和认知约束需要不同解释这一基本事实。虽然近期研究探索了用户中心和个性化解释，但大多依赖启发式调整或隐式用户建模，缺乏表示和学习个体偏好的原则性框架。

Method: 提出PREF-XAI框架，将解释视为基于偏好的决策问题。结合基于规则的解释和形式化偏好学习，通过用户对少量候选解释的排序来获取偏好，使用鲁棒序数回归推断加性效用函数来建模用户偏好。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，PREF-XAI能够：1）从有限反馈中准确重建用户偏好；2）识别高度相关的解释；3）发现用户最初未考虑的新解释规则。

Conclusion: PREF-XAI建立了XAI与偏好学习之间的连接，为交互式和自适应解释系统开辟了新方向。该方法将解释视为需要根据用户特定标准评估和选择的备选方案，而非固定输出。

Abstract: Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) has predominantly focused on generating model-centric explanations that approximate the behavior of black-box models. However, such explanations often overlook a fundamental aspect of interpretability: different users require different explanations depending on their goals, preferences, and cognitive constraints. Although recent work has explored user-centric and personalized explanations, most existing approaches rely on heuristic adaptations or implicit user modeling, lacking a principled framework for representing and learning individual preferences. In this paper, we consider Preference-Based Explainable Artificial Intelligence (PREF-XAI), a novel perspective that reframes explanation as a preference-driven decision problem. Within PREF-XAI, explanations are not treated as fixed outputs, but as alternatives to be evaluated and selected according to user-specific criteria. In the PREF-XAI perspective, here we propose a methodology that combines rule-based explanations with formal preference learning. User preferences are elicited through a ranking of a small set of candidate explanations and modeled via an additive utility function inferred using robust ordinal regression. Experimental results on real-world datasets show that PREF-XAI can accurately reconstruct user preferences from limited feedback, identify highly relevant explanations, and discover novel explanatory rules not initially considered by the user. Beyond the proposed methodology, this work establishes a connection between XAI and preference learning, opening new directions for interactive and adaptive explanation systems.

</details>


### [95] [Planning in entropy-regularized Markov decision processes and games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19695)
*Jean-Bastien Grill,Omar Darwiche Domingues,Pierre Ménard,Rémi Munos,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SmoothCruiser是一种用于估计熵正则化MDP和双人博弈值函数的规划算法，在环境生成模型下实现O~(1/ε⁴)的样本复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非正则化设置中，没有已知算法能在最坏情况下保证多项式样本复杂度，而正则化带来的Bellman算子平滑性为解决这一问题提供了机会

Method: 利用熵正则化促进的Bellman算子平滑性，设计新的规划算法SmoothCruiser，在环境生成模型下进行值函数估计

Result: SmoothCruiser实现了问题无关的样本复杂度O~(1/ε⁴)，其中ε为期望精度，这是非正则化设置中无法达到的性能

Conclusion: 熵正则化通过使Bellman算子平滑，为设计具有多项式样本复杂度保证的规划算法提供了关键优势，SmoothCruiser证明了这一理论优势的实际可行性

Abstract: We propose SmoothCruiser, a new planning algorithm for estimating the value function in entropy-regularized Markov decision processes and two-player games, given a generative model of the environment. SmoothCruiser makes use of the smoothness of the Bellman operator promoted by the regularization to achieve problem-independent sample complexity of order O~(1/epsilon^4) for a desired accuracy epsilon, whereas for non-regularized settings there are no known algorithms with guaranteed polynomial sample complexity in the worst case.

</details>


### [96] [On two ways to use determinantal point processes for Monte Carlo integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19698)
*Guillaume Gautier,Rémi Bardenet,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了基于行列式点过程(DPP)的蒙特卡洛积分估计器，比较了两种现有方法：Bardenet & Hardy (2020)的固定DPP方法和Ermakov & Zolotukhin (1960)的自适应DPP方法，并将它们推广到连续设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准蒙特卡洛估计器使用独立样本，方差为O(1/N)。使用具有排斥性质的行列式点过程(DPP)作为样本可以改进估计器性能，但现有DPP方法各有局限：一种使用固定DPP但收敛速度有限，另一种自适应DPP但缺乏连续设置下的实现。

Method: 重新审视两种DPP基估计器：1) Bardenet & Hardy (2020)方法使用固定DPP，收敛速度为O(N^{-(1+1/d)})；2) Ermakov & Zolotukhin (1960)方法使用针对f定制的DPP，无偏且方差为O(1/N)。论文将这两种方法推广到连续设置，并提供了相应的采样算法。

Result: 论文系统分析了两种DPP基估计器的理论性质，建立了它们在连续设置下的推广形式，并开发了相应的采样算法实现。这为在不同应用场景中选择合适的DPP估计器提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Conclusion: DPP基估计器为蒙特卡洛积分提供了有吸引力的替代方案，通过利用样本间的排斥性可以改进方差特性。两种方法各有优势：固定DPP方法实现简单但收敛速度受维度影响，自适应DPP方法方差特性更好但需要针对特定函数定制。论文的统一框架为实际应用提供了指导。

Abstract: The standard Monte Carlo estimator $\widehat{I}_N^{\mathrm{MC}}$ of $\int fdω$ relies on independent samples from $ω$ and has variance of order $1/N$. Replacing the samples with a determinantal point process (DPP), a repulsive distribution, makes the estimator consistent, with variance rates that depend on how the DPP is adapted to $f$ and $ω$. We examine two existing DPP-based estimators: one by Bardenet & Hardy (2020) with a rate of $\mathcal{O}(N^{-(1+1/d)})$ for smooth $f$, but relying on a fixed DPP. The other, by Ermakov & Zolotukhin (1960), is unbiased with rate of order $1/N$, like Monte Carlo, but its DPP is tailored to $f$. We revisit these estimators, generalize them to continuous settings, and provide sampling algorithms.

</details>


### [97] [Ultrametric OGP - parametric RDT \emph{symmetric} binary perceptron connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19712)
*Mihailo Stojnic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将参数化RDT与重叠间隙特性（OGPs）联系起来，研究了对称二元感知器的统计计算间隙，通过分析联合边界程序获得了超度量OGPs的密度上限，并提出了连接ult-OGP与参数化RDT的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究引入了fl-RDT框架来表征统计计算间隙（SCGs），并获得了对称二元感知器的算法阈值估计。本文旨在进一步连接参数化RDT与重叠间隙特性（OGPs），这是解空间的另一个关键几何特征，以深入理解统计计算间隙的本质。

Method: 考虑s级超度量OGPs（ult_s-OGPs），开发了一个包含组合和概率分量的分析联合边界程序。将组合部分转化为凸问题，概率部分转化为嵌套积分，通过数值评估获得约束密度的严格上限。

Result: 获得了前两个级别的紧致边界：¯α_ult_1 ≈ 1.6578和¯α_ult_2 ≈ 1.6219，分别接近第3和第4提升级别的参数化RDT估计（α_c^(3) ≈ 1.6576和α_c^(4) ≈ 1.6218）。在其他关键参数（包括重叠值和超度量簇的相对大小）上也观察到良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 基于观察结果，提出了连接ult-OGP与参数化RDT的几个猜想：算法阈值α_a等于两个极限的极限值，且α_ult_s ≤ α_c^(s+2)（可能对某些或所有s相等）。讨论了连接ult-OGP与参数化RDT所有关键参数的完全同构存在的可能性。

Abstract: In [97,99,100], an fl-RDT framework is introduced to characterize \emph{statistical computational gaps} (SCGs). Studying \emph{symmetric binary perceptrons} (SBPs), [100] obtained an \emph{algorithmic} threshold estimate $α_a\approx α_c^{(7)}\approx 1.6093$ at the 7th lifting level (for $κ=1$ margin), closely approaching $1.58$ local entropy (LE) prediction [18].
  In this paper, we further connect parametric RDT to overlap gap properties (OGPs), another key geometric feature of the solution space. Specifically, for any positive integer $s$, we consider $s$-level ultrametric OGPs ($ult_s$-OGPs) and rigorously upper-bound the associated constraint densities $α_{ult_s}$. To achieve this, we develop an analytical union-bounding program consisting of combinatorial and probabilistic components. By casting the combinatorial part as a convex problem and the probabilistic part as a nested integration, we conduct numerical evaluations and obtain that the tightest bounds at the first two levels, $\barα_{ult_1} \approx 1.6578$ and $\barα_{ult_2} \approx 1.6219$, closely approach the 3rd and 4th lifting level parametric RDT estimates, $α_c^{(3)} \approx 1.6576$ and $α_c^{(4)} \approx 1.6218$. We also observe excellent agreement across other key parameters, including overlap values and the relative sizes of ultrametric clusters.
  Based on these observations, we propose several conjectures linking $ult$-OGP and parametric RDT. Specifically, we conjecture that algorithmic threshold $α_a=\lim_{s\rightarrow\infty} α_{ult_s} = \lim_{s\rightarrow\infty} \barα{ult_s} = \lim_{r\rightarrow\infty} α_{c}^{(r)}$, and $α_{ult_s} \leq α_{c}^{(s+2)}$ (with possible equality for some (maybe even all) $s$). Finally, we discuss the potential existence of a full isomorphism connecting all key parameters of $ult$-OGP and parametric RDT.

</details>


### [98] [Adaptive MSD-Splitting: Enhancing C4.5 and Random Forests for Skewed Continuous Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19722)
*Jake Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMSD（自适应MSD分割）通过动态调整标准差乘数来解决MSD分割在偏态分布数据中的信息丢失问题，并将其集成到随机森林中，在保持O(N)时间复杂度的同时获得2-4%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续数值属性的离散化是决策树归纳中的计算瓶颈。虽然MSD分割技术通过均值和标准差分箱提高了C4.5算法的效率和准确性，但其固定的一个标准差截断在高度偏态分布数据（如生物医学和金融数据集）中会导致灾难性的信息丢失。

Method: 提出自适应MSD分割（AMSD），根据特征偏度动态调整标准差乘数，在密集区域缩小间隔以保持判别分辨率。将AMSD集成到集成方法中，特别是提出了随机森林-AMSD（RF-AMSD）框架。

Result: 在Census Income、Heart Disease、Breast Cancer和Forest Covertype数据集上的实证评估显示，AMSD相比标准MSD分割获得了2-4%的准确率提升，同时保持了与O(N log N)穷举搜索相比近乎相同的O(N)时间复杂度降低。随机森林扩展以标准计算成本的一小部分实现了最先进的准确率。

Conclusion: 自适应统计分箱在大规模集成学习架构中具有可行性，AMSD通过动态调整偏度敏感的分割策略，在保持计算效率的同时显著提高了在偏态分布数据上的性能。

Abstract: The discretization of continuous numerical attributes remains a persistent computational bottleneck in the induction of decision trees, particularly as dataset dimensions scale. Building upon the recently proposed MSD-Splitting technique -- which bins continuous data using the empirical mean and standard deviation to dramatically improve the efficiency and accuracy of the C4.5 algorithm -- we introduce Adaptive MSD-Splitting (AMSD). While standard MSD-Splitting is highly effective for approximately symmetric distributions, its rigid adherence to fixed one-standard-deviation cutoffs can lead to catastrophic information loss in highly skewed data, a common artifact in real-world biomedical and financial datasets. AMSD addresses this by dynamically adjusting the standard deviation multiplier based on feature skewness, narrowing intervals in dense regions to preserve discriminative resolution. Furthermore, we integrate AMSD into ensemble methods, specifically presenting the Random Forest-AMSD (RF-AMSD) framework. Empirical evaluations on the Census Income, Heart Disease, Breast Cancer, and Forest Covertype datasets demonstrate that AMSD yields a 2-4% accuracy improvement over standard MSD-Splitting, while maintaining near-identical O(N) time complexity reductions compared to the O(N log N) exhaustive search. Our Random Forest extension achieves state-of-the-art accuracy at a fraction of standard computational costs, confirming the viability of adaptive statistical binning in large-scale ensemble learning architectures.

</details>


### [99] [Benign Overfitting in Adversarial Training for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19724)
*Jiaming Zhang,Meng Ding,Shaopeng Fu,Jingfeng Zhang,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次对简化ViT架构进行对抗训练的理论分析，证明在一定条件下对抗训练可使ViT实现近乎零的鲁棒训练损失和泛化误差，甚至在过拟合时也能保持强泛化能力（良性过拟合）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Vision Transformers在各种视觉任务中取得了显著成功，但研究表明它们与CNN一样容易受到对抗样本的攻击。对抗训练是常用的经验性防御策略，但其在ViT中的鲁棒性理论基础尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 对简化的Vision Transformer架构进行理论分析，研究在满足特定信噪比条件和适度扰动预算下，对抗训练如何影响ViT的性能。通过理论推导和实验验证（合成数据集和真实数据集）相结合的方法。

Result: 理论分析表明：当满足特定信噪比条件且在适度扰动预算内，对抗训练能使ViT实现近乎零的鲁棒训练损失和鲁棒泛化误差。即使在过拟合情况下也能保持强泛化能力，这种现象被称为"良性过拟合"，此前仅在CNN（经对抗训练）中观察到。

Conclusion: 本研究首次为ViT对抗训练提供了理论分析框架，证明了在一定条件下对抗训练可使ViT实现鲁棒性和泛化能力的平衡，甚至在过拟合时也能保持强泛化性能（良性过拟合现象）。实验验证了理论发现。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of Vision Transformers (ViTs) across a wide range of vision tasks, recent studies have revealed that they remain vulnerable to adversarial examples, much like Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). A common empirical defense strategy is adversarial training, yet the theoretical underpinnings of its robustness in ViTs remain largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first theoretical analysis of adversarial training under simplified ViT architectures. We show that, when trained under a signal-to-noise ratio that satisfies a certain condition and within a moderate perturbation budget, adversarial training enables ViTs to achieve nearly zero robust training loss and robust generalization error under certain regimes. Remarkably, this leads to strong generalization even in the presence of overfitting, a phenomenon known as \emph{benign overfitting}, previously only observed in CNNs (with adversarial training). Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets further validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [100] [FB-NLL: A Feature-Based Approach to Tackle Noisy Labels in Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19729)
*Abdulmoneam Ali,Ahmed Arafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FB-NLL：一种特征中心的个性化联邦学习框架，通过谱结构分析实现一次性用户聚类，并采用特征一致性策略处理噪声标签，显著降低通信开销并提升性能稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习动态的PFL方法容易受到低质量数据和噪声标签的影响，因为损坏的更新会扭曲聚类决策并降低个性化性能。需要一种能够解耦用户聚类与迭代训练动态、对噪声鲁棒的方法。

Method: FB-NLL采用特征中心框架：1) 通过特征表示协方差的谱结构分析用户特征空间异质性，利用子空间相似性一次性识别任务一致的用户分组；2) 基于特征一致性的检测和校正策略，利用学习特征空间中的方向对齐和类特定特征子空间分配标签，缓解损坏的监督信号。

Result: 在多种数据集和噪声机制下的广泛实验表明，FB-NLL在平均准确率和性能稳定性方面持续优于最先进的基线方法，同时显著降低了通信开销和计算成本。

Conclusion: FB-NLL通过特征中心方法有效解决了PFL中的噪声鲁棒性问题，提供了一种模型无关、通信高效的解决方案，能够与现有噪声鲁棒训练技术无缝集成。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) aims to learn multiple task-specific models rather than a single global model across heterogeneous data distributions. Existing PFL approaches typically rely on iterative optimization-such as model update trajectories-to cluster users that need to accomplish the same tasks together. However, these learning-dynamics-based methods are inherently vulnerable to low-quality data and noisy labels, as corrupted updates distort clustering decisions and degrade personalization performance. To tackle this, we propose FB-NLL, a feature-centric framework that decouples user clustering from iterative training dynamics. By exploiting the intrinsic heterogeneity of local feature spaces, FB-NLL characterizes each user through the spectral structure of the covariances of their feature representations and leverages subspace similarity to identify task-consistent user groupings. This geometry-aware clustering is label-agnostic and is performed in a one-shot manner prior to training, significantly reducing communication overhead and computational costs compared to iterative baselines.
  Complementing this, we introduce a feature-consistency-based detection and correction strategy to address noisy labels within clusters. By leveraging directional alignment in the learned feature space and assigning labels based on class-specific feature subspaces, our method mitigates corrupted supervision without requiring estimation of stochastic noise transition matrices. In addition, FB-NLL is model-independent and integrates seamlessly with existing noise-robust training techniques. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and noise regimes demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of average accuracy and performance stability.

</details>


### [101] [FASTER: Value-Guided Sampling for Fast RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19730)
*Perry Dong,Alexander Swerdlow,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FASTER是一种用于扩散策略的轻量级测试时扩展方法，通过将多动作候选采样建模为MDP，在去噪过程中早期预测并过滤候选，从而获得采样优势而无需高计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前性能最好的强化学习算法使用测试时扩展方法（如采样多个动作候选并选择最佳）可能计算成本过高。需要一种方法既能获得扩散策略采样优势，又避免高计算开销。

Method: 将多动作候选去噪和选择过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），目标是在去噪完成前逐步过滤动作候选。学习去噪空间中的策略和价值函数，预测去噪过程中动作候选的下游价值并进行过滤以最大化回报。

Result: 在具有挑战性的长时程操作任务中，FASTER持续改进底层策略，在在线和批量在线强化学习中表现最佳。应用于预训练的VLA时，FASTER实现相同性能同时显著降低训练和推理计算需求。

Conclusion: FASTER提供了一种轻量级方法，可插入现有生成式强化学习算法，在不增加计算成本的情况下获得采样优势，为扩散策略的测试时扩展提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Some of the most performant reinforcement learning algorithms today can be prohibitively expensive as they use test-time scaling methods such as sampling multiple action candidates and selecting the best one. In this work, we propose FASTER, a method for getting the benefits of sampling-based test-time scaling of diffusion-based policies without the computational cost by tracing the performance gain of action samples back to earlier in the denoising process. Our key insight is that we can model the denoising of multiple action candidates and selecting the best one as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) where the goal is to progressively filter action candidates before denoising is complete. With this MDP, we can learn a policy and value function in the denoising space that predicts the downstream value of action candidates in the denoising process and filters them while maximizing returns. The result is a method that is lightweight and can be plugged into existing generative RL algorithms. Across challenging long-horizon manipulation tasks in online and batch-online RL, FASTER consistently improves the underlying policies and achieves the best overall performance among the compared methods. Applied to a pretrained VLA, FASTER achieves the same performance while substantially reducing training and inference compute requirements. Code is available at https://github.com/alexanderswerdlow/faster .

</details>


### [102] [Safe Continual Reinforcement Learning in Non-stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19737)
*Austin Coursey,Abel Diaz-Gonzalez,Marcos Quinones-Grueiro,Gautam Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究安全持续强化学习，探讨在非平稳环境中同时满足安全约束和防止灾难性遗忘的挑战，提出了三个基准环境并评估现有方法，揭示了安全与适应之间的基本张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习控制器假设环境平稳，但在实际非平稳部署中系统动态和操作条件会意外变化。同时，物理环境中的RL控制器必须在学习和执行阶段始终满足安全约束。尽管持续RL和安全RL分别解决了非平稳性和安全性问题，但两者的交叉研究相对不足，因此需要研究能够在系统生命周期中适应变化同时保持安全的安全持续RL算法。

Method: 系统研究安全持续强化学习：1）引入三个捕捉安全关键持续适应的基准环境；2）评估来自安全RL、持续RL及其组合的代表性方法；3）分析现有方法在同时满足安全约束和防止灾难性遗忘方面的表现；4）研究基于正则化的策略以部分缓解这种权衡。

Result: 实证结果揭示了在非平稳动态下维持安全约束和防止灾难性遗忘之间的基本张力，现有方法通常无法同时实现这两个目标。基于正则化的策略部分缓解了这种权衡，但仍有局限性。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够在变化环境中持续自主运行的安全、有弹性的基于学习的控制器。论文概述了关键开放挑战和研究方向，包括如何更好地平衡安全约束与适应能力，以及开发更有效的算法来解决安全持续RL中的基本张力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a compelling data-driven paradigm for synthesizing controllers for complex systems when accurate physical models are unavailable; however, most existing control-oriented RL methods assume stationarity and, therefore, struggle in real-world non-stationary deployments where system dynamics and operating conditions can change unexpectedly. Moreover, RL controllers acting in physical environments must satisfy safety constraints throughout their learning and execution phases, rendering transient violations during adaptation unacceptable. Although continual RL and safe RL have each addressed non-stationarity and safety, respectively, their intersection remains comparatively unexplored, motivating the study of safe continual RL algorithms that can adapt over the system's lifetime while preserving safety. In this work, we systematically investigate safe continual reinforcement learning by introducing three benchmark environments that capture safety-critical continual adaptation and by evaluating representative approaches from safe RL, continual RL, and their combinations. Our empirical results reveal a fundamental tension between maintaining safety constraints and preventing catastrophic forgetting under non-stationary dynamics, with existing methods generally failing to achieve both objectives simultaneously. To address this shortcoming, we examine regularization-based strategies that partially mitigate this trade-off and characterize their benefits and limitations. Finally, we outline key open challenges and research directions toward developing safe, resilient learning-based controllers capable of sustained autonomous operation in changing environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [CentaurTA Studio: A Self-Improving Human-Agent Collaboration System for Thematic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18589)
*Lei Wang,Min Huang,Eduard Dragut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CentaurTA Studio是一个基于网页的自改进人-智能体协作系统，用于主题分析中的开放编码和主题构建，通过两阶段反馈流程、持续提示优化和基于量规的评估来提高可扩展性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主题分析难以规模化：手动工作流程劳动密集，而完全自动化的流程往往缺乏可控性和透明评估。需要一种能够结合人类专业知识和AI效率的协作系统。

Method: 系统包含三个核心组件：(1) 两阶段人类反馈流程，分离模拟器草拟和专家验证；(2) 持续提示优化，将验证过的反馈提炼为可重用的对齐原则；(3) 基于量规的评估与早期停止机制，用于过程控制。

Result: 在三个领域中，CentaurTA在开放编码和主题构建方面均表现最佳，准确率高达92.12%，始终优于基线系统。基于量规的LLM评估者与人类标注者之间达到实质性可靠性（平均κ=0.68）。消融研究表明，移除反馈循环会使性能从90%降至81%，而移除批评者或早期停止会降低准确性或增加交互成本。完整系统在10轮迭代内（约25分钟）达到峰值性能，比纯专家精炼效率更高。

Conclusion: CentaurTA Studio通过人-智能体协作框架有效解决了主题分析的可扩展性问题，结合了人类专业知识的可控性和AI的效率，在保持高质量的同时显著减少了时间和劳动成本。

Abstract: Thematic analysis is difficult to scale: manual workflows are labor-intensive, while fully automated pipelines often lack controllability and transparent evaluation. We present \textbf{CentaurTA Studio}, a web-based system for self-improving human--agent collaboration in open coding and theme construction. The system integrates (1) a two-stage human feedback pipeline separating simulator drafting and expert validation, (2) persistent prompt optimization that distills validated feedback into reusable alignment principles, and (3) rubric-based evaluation with early stopping for process control.
  Across three domains, CentaurTA achieves the strongest performance in both Open Coding and Theme Construction, reaching up to 92.12\% accuracy and consistently outperforming baseline systems. Agreement between the rubric-based LLM judge and human annotators reaches substantial reliability (average $κ= 0.68$). Ablation studies show that removing the feedback loop reduces performance from 90\% to 81\%, while eliminating the Critic or early stopping degrades accuracy or increases interaction cost. The full system reaches peak performance within 10 iterative rounds (about 25 minutes), demonstrating improved efficiency over expert-only refinement.

</details>


### [104] [Critical Thinking in the Age of Artificial Intelligence: A Survey-Based Study with Machine Learning Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18590)
*M Murshidul Bari,Akif Islam,Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Abu Saleh Musa Miah,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI在教育、工作和日常问题解决中的使用对批判性思维有复杂影响，研究发现AI使用行为与批判性思维表现之间存在混合关系，影响取决于使用方式而非单一的正负效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI在教育和专业领域日益增长的使用对人类推理能力的影响，特别是关注AI可能导致的认知卸载、减少生产性挣扎和削弱独立批判性思维的问题。

Method: 采用访谈式调查结合简短逻辑推理任务的方法，研究AI使用行为与批判性思维表现之间的关系，并进行探索性聚类分析识别用户行为模式。

Result: 发现混合模式：参与者普遍将AI视为提高速度和便利性的工具，但许多人报告耐心减少；客观推理表现个体差异大，耐心减少和依赖倾向与较低推理表现更相关；AI用户可分为过度依赖型、混合策略型和平衡支持寻求型等行为模式。

Conclusion: AI对批判性思维的影响不是单一的正向或负向，而是取决于使用方式；有效的人机协作应支持反思、验证和持续认知努力，而不是替代它们。

Abstract: The growing use of artificial intelligence (AI) in education, professional work, and everyday problem-solving has raised important questions about its effect on human reasoning. While AI can improve efficiency, save time, and support learning, repeated dependence on it may also encourage cognitive offloading, reduce productive struggle, and weaken independent critical thinking. This paper investigates the relationship between AI-use behavior and critical-thinking performance through an interview-based survey combined with short logic and reasoning tasks. The findings reveal a mixed pattern: participants largely viewed AI as a tool for speed, convenience, and learning support, yet many also reported reduced patience for sustained effort. Objective reasoning performance varied considerably across individuals, and the analyses suggest that reduced patience and stronger dependence-related tendencies are more closely associated with lower reasoning performance than background characteristics alone. Exploratory clustering further indicates that AI users do not form a single homogeneous group, but instead reflect tentative behavioral profiles, including over-reliant users, mixed-strategy users, and balanced support-seekers. Although the findings are exploratory, they indicate that AI does not affect critical thinking in a uniformly negative or positive way. Instead, its influence appears to depend on the manner in which it is used. The paper therefore argues that effective human-AI collaboration should support reflection, verification, and sustained cognitive effort rather than substitute for them.

</details>


### [105] [SPRITE: From Static Mockups to Engine-Ready Game UI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18591)
*Yunshu Bai,RuiHao Li,Hao Zhang,Chien Her Lim,Ming Yan,Mengtian Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SPRITE是一个将游戏UI截图转换为可编辑引擎资产的自动化管道，通过结合视觉语言模型和结构化YAML中间表示来解决游戏界面不规则几何和深层视觉层次的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前"截图转代码"工具在处理游戏界面时存在困难，因为游戏UI通常具有不规则的几何形状和深层的视觉层次结构。需要一种能够准确捕捉复杂容器关系和非矩形布局的解决方案，以弥合艺术设计与技术实现之间的鸿沟。

Method: SPRITE通过整合视觉语言模型(VLMs)和结构化YAML中间表示来构建管道。该方法明确捕捉复杂的容器关系和非矩形布局，将静态截图转换为可编辑的引擎资产。

Result: 在精心策划的游戏UI基准测试中评估了SPRITE，并进行了专业开发者的专家评审，评估重建保真度和原型制作效率。结果表明SPRITE通过自动化繁琐的编码和解决复杂嵌套问题来简化开发流程。

Conclusion: SPRITE通过促进快速的引擎内迭代，有效地模糊了游戏开发中艺术设计与技术实现之间的界限，为游戏UI实现提供了一种高效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Game UI implementation requires translating stylized mockups into interactive engine entities. However, current "Screenshot-to-Code" tools often struggle with the irregular geometries and deep visual hierarchies typical of game interfaces. To bridge this gap, we introduce SPRITE, a pipeline that transforms static screenshots into editable engine assets. By integrating Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with a structured YAML intermediate representation, SPRITE explicitly captures complex container relationships and non-rectangular layouts. We evaluated SPRITE against a curated Game UI benchmark and conducted expert reviews with professional developers to assess reconstruction fidelity and prototyping efficiency. Our findings demonstrate that SPRITE streamlines development by automating tedious coding and resolving complex nesting. By facilitating rapid in-engine iteration, SPRITE effectively blurs the boundaries between artistic design and technical implementation in game development. Project page: https://baiyunshu.github.io/sprite.github.io/

</details>


### [106] [Input Visualizations to Track Health Data by Older Adults with Multiple Chronic Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18741)
*Shri Harini Ramesh,Foroozan Daneshzand,Matteo Sotelo,Mahsa Sinaei,Fateme Rajabiyazdi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨了老年多重慢性病患者如何使用物理代币进行健康数据输入可视化，以支持数据收集过程中的即时反思和洞察发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年多重慢性病患者需要收集健康数据，但传统方法（数字工具或手写记录）中数据收集与反思分离，收集过程本身缺乏主动参与和意义感。物理代币输入可视化有望帮助患者在收集数据时即时识别模式，以更有意义和愉悦的方式发现洞察。

Method: 采用访谈和日记研究方法，让老年多重慢性病患者使用物理代币输入健康数据两周，观察他们如何采用、适应和个性化这种数据收集方式。

Result: 研究发现老年患者具有多样化的个人健康数据追踪需求，他们会调整策略并个性化物理输入可视化以满足个体需求。参与者将输入可视化融入日常习惯，利用有形标记反思模式和行为，享受追踪过程并注重个人表达和有意义反思。

Conclusion: 研究为支持老年多重慢性病患者通过物理代币输入健康数据提供了设计考虑，强调了物理输入可视化在促进主动参与、个人表达和有意义反思方面的潜力。

Abstract: Older adults living with multiple chronic conditions (MCC) can considerably benefit from collecting and reflecting on their health data. Many older adults collect their health data using various approaches, such as digital tools or handwritten notebooks. However, in these approaches, the act of collecting data does not itself yield insights; sensemaking and reflection happen only if individuals later review their accumulated records. The daily process of data collection thus offers limited opportunity for individuals to actively engage with their data or find the process personally meaningful and enjoyable. Personal data input visualizations using physical tokens offer a promising solution that can help individuals recognize evolving patterns while collecting data and discover meaningful insights more serendipitously and engagingly. Yet, there is a limited understanding of whether and how older adults living with MCC might adopt physical input visualizations to collect data and reflect on their health, and how the tangible, expressive, and personalizable nature of this process supports their sensemaking and reflection. In this paper, we present the results of our interview and diary studies in which older adults living with MCC inputted health data using physical tokens over two weeks. Our findings highlight the diverse and unique needs of older adults for tracking personal health data, illustrating how they adapt strategies and personalize physical input visualizations to align with their individual needs. We demonstrate how older adults integrated input visualizations into daily routines and leveraged tangible markers to reflect on patterns and behaviors, while enjoying the process of tracking and focusing on personal expression and meaningful reflection. Finally, we provide design considerations for supporting older adults with MCC when inputting health data through physical tokens.

</details>


### [107] [AffectCity: An Empirical Investigation of Complexity, Transparency, and Materiality in Shaping Affective Perception of Building Facades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18768)
*Chenxi Wang,Haining Ding,Michal Gath-Morad*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过剑桥立面情感数据集(CFAD)揭示了建筑立面特性与人类情感反应之间的量化关系，发现感知复杂性是主要情感预测因子，而机器视觉指标需通过人类感知作为中介才能有效预测情感反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑立面如何影响人们的情感状态，其具体机制在实证研究中尚未明确。现有研究缺乏将可量化的立面属性与人类情感反应直接关联的验证方法，需要建立从建筑特征到情感体验的因果路径模型。

Method: 研究创建了剑桥立面情感数据集(CFAD)，包含86张正交校正的立面图像，由85名参与者进行连续唤醒度和愉悦度评分。采用机器视觉提取三个可量化属性：复杂性、透明度（窗墙比）和材质性（自然与人工表面比例），通过回归分析和中介分析探究这些属性与情感反应的关系，并进行跨情境验证。

Result: 感知复杂性是主要情感预测因子，与唤醒度(β=0.507)和愉悦度(β=0.376)均呈显著正相关，且在较高复杂性水平呈现曲线放大效应。透明度与愉悦度呈倒U型关系。表面人工化程度增加会抑制唤醒度并降低愉悦度。机器衍生指标对情感结果的直接预测能力有限，人类感知评价作为必要中介层，感知材质性显著中介了机器指标与愉悦度的关系。跨情境验证显示复杂性和材质性评分具有中等稳定性，而情感反应（特别是愉悦度）表现出显著的情境依赖性。

Conclusion: 研究将立面研究从描述性形态分析推进到基于感知的预测建模，为城市环境的情感导向设计提供了实证基础。关键发现是建筑立面通过人类感知中介影响情感，而非直接由物理特征决定，强调了在设计实践中考虑感知过程的重要性。

Abstract: Buildings shape how people feel, yet the mechanisms through which specific facade properties drive affective states remain empirically underspecified. Here we introduce the Cambridge Facade Affect Dataset (CFAD), 86 orthogonally rectified facade images annotated with continuous arousal and valence ratings from 85 participants, and establish a validated pipeline linking machine-vision-derived surface metrics to human affective responses. Focusing on three quantifiable attributes, complexity, transparency (window-to-wall ratio), and materiality (proportion of natural versus artificial surface composition), we show that perceived complexity is the dominant affective predictor, with significant positive associations for both arousal (beta = 0.507, p < 0.001) and valence (beta = 0.376, p < 0.001) and a curvilinear amplification at higher complexity levels. Transparency exhibits an inverted-U relationship with valence, while increasing surface artificiality suppresses arousal and reduces pleasantness consistent with biophilic response theory. Critically, machine-derived metrics show limited direct predictive power over affective outcomes; mediation analyses reveal that human perceptual evaluation functions as a necessary intermediate layer, with perceived materiality significantly mediating the machine-valence relationship (indirect effect = -0.205, p = 0.003). Cross-context validation demonstrates moderate stability of complexity and materiality ratings across image-based and in-situ conditions, while affective responses, particularly valence, exhibit significant context-dependence (ICC = 0.332). These findings advance facade research from descriptive morphological analysis toward predictive, perception-grounded modelling, and provide an empirically validated basis for affect-informed design of the urban environment.

</details>


### [108] [Choose Your Own Adventure: Non-Linear AI-Assisted Programming with EvoGraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18883)
*Vassilios Exarhakos,Jinghui Cheng,Jin L. C. Guo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: EvoGraph是一个IDE插件，通过交互式开发图记录和管理AI辅助编程的分支历史，帮助开发者探索替代方案、管理提示序列和追踪变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助编程工具主要是线性和聊天式的，这与编程本身的迭代和分支特性不符。初步研究发现开发者在使用AI助手时难以探索替代方案、管理提示序列和追踪变更。

Method: 创建EvoGraph IDE插件，将AI交互和代码变更集成为轻量级交互式开发图。自动记录分支的AI辅助编码历史，允许开发者操作图来比较、合并和重新访问先前的协作AI编程状态。

Result: 20名参与者的用户研究表明，EvoGraph解决了初步研究中发现的开发者挑战，同时降低了认知负荷。参与者认为基于图的表示支持安全探索、高效迭代和反思AI生成的变更。

Conclusion: 这项工作强调了在AI介导的编程背景下，工具设计的机会可以帮助开发者理解和处理他们的问题解决进展，支持安全探索、高效迭代和反思。

Abstract: Current AI-assisted programming tools are predominantly linear and chat-based, which deviates from the iterative and branching nature of programming itself. Our preliminary study with developers using AI assistants suggested that they often struggle to explore alternatives, manage prompting sequences, and trace changes. Informed by these insights, we created EvoGraph, an IDE plugin that integrates AI interactions and code changes as a lightweight and interactive development graph. EvoGraph automatically records a branching AI-assisted coding history and allows developers to manipulate the graph to compare, merge, and revisit prior collaborative AI programming states. Our user study with 20 participants revealed that EvoGraph addressed developers' challenges identified in our preliminary study while imposing lower cognitive load. Participants also found the graph-based representation supported safe exploration, efficient iteration, and reflection on AI-generated changes. Our work highlights design opportunities for tools to help developers make sense of and act on their problem-solving progress in the emerging AI-mediated programming context.

</details>


### [109] [Relationships Between Trust, Compliance, and Performance for Novice Programmers Using AI Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18948)
*Nicholas Gardella,Matthew L. Bolton,Sara L. Riggs*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索新手程序员对AI驱动开发环境的信任如何影响其编码表现和AI建议采纳，发现在时间压力下信任与AI合规性无直接关联，但强表现会提升后续信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI驱动开发环境在软件开发中日益普及，了解用户对这些工具的信任态度至关重要。虽然自动化领域对信任的研究已有数十年，但在AIDE环境中信任如何影响编程表现和AI建议采纳的研究仍很有限，特别是在时间压力情境下。

Method: 研究收集了来自两个研究地点的27名新手程序员的主观信任测量数据，以及客观的性能和AIDE合规性测量数据。通过实验设计，在时间压力下观察程序员与AIDE的交互过程。

Result: 结果证实了传统理解中信任如何通过经验变化，但未发现信任与后续编程任务中AI合规性之间存在关系。更强的合规性与更好的表现相关，而更好的表现会带来更高的后续信任。

Conclusion: 研究结果对AIDE和生成式AI交互中信任的效用提出了新问题。需要进一步研究信任对采纳不完美AI建议的影响。教学设计师应考虑AI误用和弃用的风险，专注于促进理想的交互结果，无论信任是否与之相关。

Abstract: Objective. To explore how novice programmers' trust in Artificial Intelligence-driven Development Environments (AIDEs) relates to their coding performance and AI compliance while programming under time pressure. Background. Computer programming has undergone rapid upheaval due to state-of-the-art AIDEs, which provide clever automation for many aspects of software development. A longstanding interest of researchers of automation more generally has been the attitude of trust. Decades of research seek to explain how influencing trust can help to achieve desirable outcomes in different domains, but very limited work has provided similar focus on trust in AIDEs. Method. We collected subjective measures of trust along with objective measures of performance and AIDE compliance from a diverse group of 27 novice programmers between two study locations. Results. Our results corroborated traditional understandings of how trust changes through experiences. However, we did not find a relationship between trust and subsequent compliance during programming tasks. Greater compliance was associated with strong performance, and strong performance led to greater subsequent trust. Conclusion. Our findings raise new questions about the utility of trust in the context of interacting with AIDEs and generative AI. We call for further research into the effect of trust on compliance to recommendations from imperfect AI. Application. This work can inform the design of training and educational content for generative AI use within and beyond software development. Instructional designers should consider risks of AI misuse and disuse and focus on promoting desirable interaction outcomes, regardless of trust's connection to them.

</details>


### [110] [Physical and Augmented Reality based Playful Activities for Refresher Training of ASHA Workers in India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18959)
*Arka Majhi,Satish B. Agnihotri,Aparajita Mondal*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 比较增强现实(AR)和实体卡牌游戏作为社区卫生工作者培训工具的效果，发现AR版本在学习和知识保留方面表现更好


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度儿童营养不良和免疫接种率低的问题严重，传统的社区卫生工作者培训方法效果不佳。随着社区卫生工作者配备智能手机，需要重新思考使用ICT方法改进培训方式

Method: 开发了两种儿童免疫接种计划培训工具：实体卡牌游戏和增强现实(AR)版本。通过问卷调查在干预前后测试两种工具的效果和知识保留情况，比较了多个游戏会话前后的表现

Result: AR游戏在学习效果和知识保留方面表现更好，参与度更高，主要归因于其互动性和直观的游戏特性

Conclusion: AR技术可以作为有效的社区卫生工作者培训工具，通过其互动和直观的特性提高学习效果和知识保留

Abstract: Recent health surveys in India highlight the alarming child malnutrition levels and lower rates of complete child immunization in many parts of India. Previous researches report that the conventional training pedagogy of the CHWs (Community Healthcare Workers) or the ASHAs (Accredited Social Health Activists) in India is ineffective in enhancing their capacity. Considering that the CHWs are getting equipped with smartphones, it calls for a rethinking of their training pedagogy using the ICT approach. Two refresher training tools were developed to make learning the child immunization schedule more exciting and conceptually engaging for ASHAs. The physical and AR (Augmented Reality) versions of designed card games were compared for effectiveness and knowledge retention, pre, and post-intervention through questionnaire tests conducted immediately before and after playing multiple sessions. The AR-based play was found to be better in learning and knowledge retention with more engagement, mainly due to its interactive and intuitive nature of play.

</details>


### [111] [Analysis of AWW (Anganwadi Workers) Training Content, ILA (Incremental Learning Approach) Modules Following CDT (Component Display Theory)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19032)
*Arka Majhi,Satish B. Agnihotri*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究分析了POSHAN Abhiyan项目中针对基层卫生工作者(AWWs)的ILA培训模块，通过内容分析、学习目标制定和教学法设计，为开发基于游戏化学习的Android应用提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POSHAN Abhiyan项目通过ILA方法对基层卫生工作者进行能力建设，但面临以下挑战：AWWs缺乏正规营养学和流行病学背景；培训不均衡且缺乏结构性；延迟的复习课程导致工作准备不足；需要基于语言、地点和先验知识的定制化培训材料。

Method: 1. 对21个ILA培训模块进行内容分析，将内容分解为事实、概念、程序和原则四种类型；2. 为AWWs制定学习目标；3. 应用Merrill的组件显示理论(CDT)，按照目标规范图表将内容与学习目标进行映射；4. 基于此框架开发基于游戏化学习的Android应用。

Result: 建立了从培训文献到教学法开发的系统框架：通过内容分析识别内容类型，制定学习目标，应用CDT理论进行内容-目标映射，从而能够轻松地从新的培训文献中开发教学法。

Conclusion: 内容分析、学习目标制定和教学法开发的方法论有助于概念化游戏化应用，为AWWs提供定制化的复习培训，解决现有培训体系中的挑战，最终提升基层卫生服务的质量和效果。

Abstract: POSHAN Abhiyan envisages capacity building of AWWs or frontline health workers through 21 training modules of ILA (Incremental Learning Approach), modularising the net learning content into smaller learning topics to help them perform their daily activities. It envisions building skilled AWWs, strengthening supervisory hierarchies, and improving coordination between AWWs (ICDS) services and health programs to achieve common goals such as increasing awareness, improving access to health and nutrition services, and reducing deaths and malnutrition. To better understand the contents of ILA literature, we conducted a content analysis by further breaking down the modules into content types such as facts, concepts, procedures, and principles. Then we framed learning objectives for teaching AWWs. We applied CDT (Component Display Theory by David Merrill) to map the contents with the desired learning objective, following the Specification of Objective chart. In this way, one can easily develop pedagogies from a new training literature. The challenges in framing learning objectives and pedagogies are: The AWWs do not have a (formal/scientific) nutrition and epidemiology background. Therefore, it is important to teach them through examples, familiar to them. AWWs are not evenly and structurally trained across districts. Training materials should be customized based on language, location, and prior knowledge. Delayed refresher courses render them underprepared for their jobs. To overcome these problems, we are developing an Android app based on gamified learning to provide refresher training to AWWs. Conducting content analysis, framing learning objectives, and developing pedagogical approaches will help conceptualize the gamified application.

</details>


### [112] [Cultural Newcomers Dining Across Borders: Need-Based Design Envision of Mixed Media Integration in MR for Foreign Menu Understanding and Ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19088)
*Ying Zhang,Daoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究针对文化新来者在外国餐厅点餐时的认知障碍和社会焦虑，探索混合媒体集成（图像、视频、3D模型）如何改善点餐体验，通过访谈和参与式设计提出混合媒体点餐助手的四个关键维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化新来者（包括新移民和国际学生）在日常互动中面临文化术语不熟悉带来的认知障碍和社会焦虑，特别是在外国餐厅点餐场景中。现有翻译工具在信息传递和媒体呈现方式上存在显著局限性，无法有效解决这些问题。

Method: 通过访谈环节（N=13）研究文化新来者在外国餐厅点餐场景中的挑战和需求，并通过参与式设计环节（包含沉浸式餐厅体验）探索他们对混合媒体集成（图像、视频、3D模型）的期望。基于对参与者需求和期望的定性分析，概念化混合媒体点餐助手。

Result: 基于定性分析，混合媒体点餐助手被概念化为四个关键维度：关键功能、用户交互、媒体层次结构和信息呈现。这些维度旨在缓解文化障碍、语言障碍、认知负荷，并改善文化新来者的用餐体验。

Conclusion: 混合媒体集成（图像、视频、3D模型）在点餐场景中具有潜力，能够有效支持文化新来者克服文化障碍、语言障碍和认知负荷，提升他们的用餐体验。研究提出的四个关键维度为设计更有效的点餐辅助工具提供了框架。

Abstract: Cultural newcomers (CNs), including new immigrants and international students, often encounter cognitive barriers and social anxiety, exacerbated by unfamiliar cultural terminology in daily interactions. This research examines these challenges in the context of ordering in foreign restaurants. Current translation tools have significant limitations in their information delivery with current media presentation methods. This research investigates the challenges and needs of CNs in ordering scenarios in a foreign restaurant through interview sessions (N = 13) and explored their expectation of mixed media integration (Image, Video, 3D Model) through a participatory design session that featured an immersive restaurant experience to support brainstorming. Based on qualitative analysis of participants' needs and expectations, the mixed media ordering assistant is conceptualized across 4 key dimensions: Key Features, User interaction, Media hierarchy, and Information presentation, with the objective of alleviating cultural barrier, linguistic barrier, cognitive load and improving the dining experience for CNs.

</details>


### [113] [Revisiting Framing Codebooks with AI: Employing Large Language Models as Analytical Collaborators in Deductive Content Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19111)
*Diego Gomez-Zara,Hernán Valdivieso,Jorge Pérez,Denis Parra,Sebastián Valenzuela*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出使用大语言模型辅助新闻框架编码手册的创建与优化，将LLM作为分析协作工具而非自动分类器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统编码手册基于理论框架构建，但在应用于大规模新闻语料时面临歧义、边界案例和规则不明确等问题；新闻语料随时间演变且存在文化差异，需要重新审视理论基础

Method: 提出结合理论框架与数据驱动探索的工作流程，将LLM定位为分析协作工具，帮助外部化决策规则、发现潜在维度，通过研究者与数据的对话支持编码手册的迭代修订

Result: 使用拉丁美洲新闻数据集验证该方法，展示了LLM能力如何帮助发现潜在模式、生成框架区分，并使框架适应新语境

Conclusion: 该方法提供了LLM辅助策略，支持方法学创新同时保持研究者的解释权威，为编码手册的创建和优化提供了新途径

Abstract: Codebooks are central to framing research, providing theoretically grounded criteria for analyzing news content. While traditionally codebooks are built from theoretical frameworks and researchers' knowledge, applying these codebooks to large news corpora often exposes ambiguities, borderline cases, and underspecified rules that are difficult to resolve through theory alone. Moreover, news corpora evolve over time and differ across cultures, necessitating that researchers revisit the theoretical frameworks underlying these codebooks. In this article, we propose a workflow that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to augment the creation and refinement of framing codebooks by combining theoretical frameworks with data-driven exploration.
