Rather than treating LLMs as automated classifiers, this approach positions them as analytic collaborators that help externalize decision rules, surface latent dimensions, and support iterative revisions of codebooks through dialogues between researchers and their data. We illustrate this workflow using a dataset of Latin American news coverage, demonstrating how the application of LLMs' capabilities has led to the surfacing of latent patterns, the generation of frame distinctions, and the adaptation of frameworks to new contexts. This method provides an LLM-assisted strategy that supports methodology creativity while preserving researchers' interpretative authority.

</details>


### [114] [OOPrompt: Reifying Intents into Structured Artifacts for Modular and Iterative Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19114)
*Tengyou Xu,Detao Ma,Xiang 'Anthony' Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出面向对象提示（OOPrompt）交互范式，将提示从线性文本转变为结构化、可操作的对象，以解决多维度意图表达问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）催生了基于提示的交互系统，但线性文本提示难以捕捉用户多方面的意图，需要更结构化的表达方式。

Method: 1. 从现有工作中提炼设计空间；2. 构建早期原型并进行形成性研究（20名参与者）；3. 基于反馈扩展OOPrompt设计空间；4. 开发完整原型并进行验证研究。

Result: 通过形成性研究获得用户反馈，扩展了设计空间；验证研究进一步理解了OOPrompt的附加价值和权衡；建立了完整的OOPrompt设计空间。

Conclusion: OOPrompt设计空间为基于提示的LLM交互系统的设计和工程提供了理论和实证指导，统一并泛化了多个现有点系统。

Abstract: The rise of large language models (LLMs) has given rise to a class of prompt-based interactive systems where users primarily express their input in natural language. However, composing a prompt as a linear text string becomes unwieldy when capturing users' multifaceted intents. We present Object-Oriented Prompting (OOPrompt), an emergent interaction paradigm that enables users to create, edit, iterate, and reuse prompts as structured, manipulable artifacts, unifying and generalizing several existing point systems. We first outlined a design space from existing work and built an early prototype, which we deployed as a probe in a formative study with 20 participants. Their feedback informed an expanded OOPrompt design space. We then developed the full OOPrompt prototype and conducted a validation study to further understand OOPrompt's added values and trade-offs. We expect the OOPrompt design space to provide theoretical and empirical guidance to the design and engineering of prompt-based, LLM-enabled interactive systems.

</details>


### [115] [Designing Transparent AI-Mediated Language Support for Intergenerational Family Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19276)
*Sora Kang,Youjin Hwang,Joonhwan Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GenSync：通过翻译可见性支持代际理解的聊天界面，透明翻译优于黑盒翻译


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代际语言差异给家庭有效亲密沟通带来挑战，需要设计支持代际理解的AI沟通工具

Method: 开发GenSync聊天界面，在16个家庭对（32人）中进行受控组内研究，比较无翻译、黑盒翻译和透明翻译（显示原文和解释信息）三种条件

Result: 翻译可见性对对话体验有重要影响：透明翻译支持对话质量、亲密感和可用性，而黑盒翻译常破坏对话流畅性

Conclusion: 代际语言支持应被视为解释性调解形式，为社交敏感情境下的AI中介沟通提供设计启示

Abstract: Intergenerational linguistic differences pose challenges to effective and intimate family communication. This paper presents GenSync, a chat-based interface that supports intergenerational understanding through different forms of translation visibility. We conducted a controlled within-subjects study with 16 family dyads (32 participants), comparing three conditions: no translation, black-box translation, and transparent translation that displays both original and interpreted messages. The results show that translation visibility plays a critical role in shaping conversational experiences. Transparent translation supported conversational quality, intimacy, and usability, while black-box translation often disrupted conversational flow. These findings position intergenerational language support as a form of interpretive mediation and contribute design implications for AI-mediated communication in socially sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Semantic Similarity: A Component-Wise Evaluation Framework for Medical Question Answering Systems with Health Equity Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19281)
*Abu Noman Md Sakib,Md. Main Oddin Chisty,Zijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VB-Score评估框架揭示LLMs在医疗问答中存在语义准确性与实体准确性之间的重大差异，以及针对慢性病和少数群体的条件性算法歧视问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估大型语言模型在医疗问答中性能的指标主要关注语义相似度，无法真实反映模型的医学准确性或健康公平性风险，需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 提出VB-Score（验证性评分）评估框架，从实体识别、语义相似度、事实一致性和结构化信息完整性四个维度评估医疗问答模型。对三个知名LLMs在48个公共卫生主题上进行严格评估，数据来源于高质量权威信息源。

Result: 发现模型语义准确性与实体准确性之间存在重大差异；所有三个模型在评估标准下都表现出几乎一致的严重性能缺陷；存在明显的条件性算法歧视，涉及老年和少数群体慢性病的公共卫生主题性能平均低13.8%；提示工程无法弥补模型在提取医学实体方面的基本架构限制。

Conclusion: 仅依赖语义评估不足以衡量医疗AI安全性，需要更全面的评估框架如VB-Score来识别算法歧视和性能缺陷，这对确保医疗AI的公平性和安全性至关重要。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) to support patients in addressing medical questions is becoming increasingly prevalent. However, most of the measures currently used to evaluate the performance of these models in this context only measure how closely a model's answers match semantically, and therefore do not provide a true indication of the model's medical accuracy or of the health equity risks associated with it. To address these shortcomings, we present a new evaluation framework for medical question answering called VB-Score (Verification-Based Score) that provides a separate evaluation of the four components of entity recognition, semantic similarity, factual consistency, and structured information completeness for medical question-answering models. We perform rigorous reviews of the performance of three well-known and widely used LLMs on 48 public health-related topics taken from high-quality, authoritative information sources. Based on our analyses, we discover a major discrepancy between the models' semantic and entity accuracy. Our assessments of the performance of all three models show that each of them has almost uniformly severe performance failures when evaluated against our criteria. Our findings indicate alarming performance disparities across various public health topics, with most of the models exhibiting 13.8% lower performance (compared to an overall average) for all the public health topics that relate to chronic conditions that occur in older and minority populations, which indicates the existence of what's known as condition-based algorithmic discrimination. Our findings also demonstrate that prompt engineering alone does not compensate for basic architectural limitations on how these models perform in extracting medical entities and raise the question of whether semantic evaluation alone is a sufficient measure of medical AI safety.

</details>


### [117] [Co-Refine: AI-Powered Tool Supporting Qualitative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19309)
*Athikash Jeyaganthan,Kai Xu,Franziska Becker,Steffen Koch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Co-Refine：AI增强的质性编码平台，通过三阶段审计管道实时检测编码一致性漂移，确保质性分析的可信度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质性编码过程中，研究人员对代码的解释会随时间发生漂移（时间漂移），这会降低分析的可信度。现有的计算机辅助质性数据分析工具仅支持数据管理，缺乏实时检测这种漂移的工作流程。

Method: 开发Co-Refine平台，采用三阶段审计管道：第一阶段计算基于嵌入的确定性指标评估数学一致性；第二阶段将LLM判断结果限制在确定性分数±0.15范围内进行验证；第三阶段从先前模式生成代码定义，形成深度反馈循环。

Result: Co-Refine证明确定性评分能够有效约束LLM输出，为质性分析生成可靠、实时的审计信号，且不干扰研究人员的工作流程。

Conclusion: Co-Refine通过AI增强的连续、基于证据的反馈机制，解决了质性编码中的时间漂移问题，提高了质性分析的可信度和一致性。

Abstract: Qualitative coding relies on a researcher's application of codes to textual data. As coding proceeds across large datasets, interpretations of codes often shift (temporal drift), reducing the credibility of the analysis. Existing Computer-Assisted Qualitative Data Analysis (CAQDAS) tools provide support for data management but offer no workflow for real-time detection of these drifts. We present Co-Refine, an AI-augmented qualitative coding platform that delivers continuous, grounded feedback on coding consistency without disrupting the researcher's workflow. The system employs a three-stage audit pipeline: Stage 1 computes deterministic embedding-based metrics for mathematical consistency; Stage 2 grounds LLM verdicts within $\pm0.15$ of the deterministic scores; and Stage 3 produces code definitions from previous patterns to create a deepening feedback loop. Co-Refine demonstrates that deterministic scoring can effectively constrain LLM outputs to produce reliable, real-time audit signals for qualitative analysis.

</details>


### [118] [VIVA Stimuli: A Web-Based Platform for Eye Tracking Stimuli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19397)
*Suleyman Ozdel,Virmarie Maquiling,Kadir Burak Buldu,Yasmeen Abdrabou,Enkelejda Kasneci*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VIVA Stimuli是一个基于Web的眼动追踪刺激呈现平台，旨在解决眼动研究中刺激呈现标准化不足和可重复性挑战的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动研究中的可重复性日益重要，但精确复制仍然困难，主要原因是实验室实践和实验设置的差异。不一致的刺激呈现可能导致相同的眼动行为产生不同的度量指标，而刺激层在很大程度上尚未标准化。现有工具通常需要编程专业知识或依赖特定硬件供应商。

Method: 开发了基于Web的VIVA Stimuli平台，提供可配置的任务类型（包括注视、平滑追踪、认知负荷、眨眼、滑动、内容显示和问卷调查）。平台支持所有眼动追踪技术，包括可穿戴和屏幕式VOG追踪器、LFI传感器和EOG设备。使用ArUco标记实现带场景摄像头的追踪器同步，WebSocket架构确保无场景摄像头设备的时序同步。提供可视化实验流程编辑器，支持协议导出和共享。

Result: VIVA Stimuli创建了一个统一的刺激呈现环境，能够跨实验室实现完全相同的刺激复制，解决了眼动研究中刺激呈现标准化不足的问题，提高了研究的可重复性。

Conclusion: VIVA Stimuli平台通过提供标准化的刺激呈现解决方案，解决了眼动研究中的可重复性挑战，使研究人员能够在不同实验室设置中精确复制实验刺激，从而促进眼动研究的验证和复制。

Abstract: Reproducibility in eye-tracking research is increasingly important as researchers conduct diverse experiments and seek to validate or replicate findings. However, exact replication remains challenging due to differences in laboratory practices and experimental setups. Inconsistent stimulus presentation can yield divergent metrics from identical oculomotor behavior, yet the stimulus layer remains largely unstandardized. Existing tools often require programming expertise or depend on specific hardware vendors. We introduce VIVA Stimuli, a web-based platform for standardized eye-tracking stimulus presentation. It provides configurable task types, including fixation, smooth pursuit, cognitive load, blink, slippage, content display, and questionnaires within a unified environment. The platform supports any eye-tracking technology, including wearable and screen-based VOG trackers, LFI sensors, and EOG devices. ArUco markers enable synchronization for trackers with scene cameras, while a WebSocket architecture ensures temporal synchronization for those without. A visual experiment flow editor allows protocols to be exported and shared, enabling identical stimulus replication across laboratories.

</details>


### [119] [Understanding Password Preferences, Memorability, and Security through a Human-Centered Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19410)
*Duru Paker,Suleyman Ozdel,Enkelejda Kasneci*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 眼动追踪研究揭示AI密码生成器的有效性及用户感知：AI生成的密码更强但用户偏好自创密码，视觉注意力与密码熵相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码作为主要认证方式存在安全性与可用性的权衡问题，用户创建的密码往往较弱。尽管AI密码生成器正在兴起，但对其有效性和用户感知的了解有限，需要研究密码创建、选择和记忆过程中的行为如何影响客观和主观密码质量。

Method: 采用眼动追踪技术，研究密码创建、选择和记忆过程中的行为。使用四种密码模型：三个基于AI的模型（DeepSeek-API、ChatGPT-API、PassGPT）和一个基于规则的随机生成器，从参与者在四个网站上下文中自生成的密码生成建议。在整个实验过程中记录眼球运动。

Result: 结果证实了AI生成密码强度与人类记忆性之间的预期权衡，但也揭示了新的行为联系。尽管AI生成的密码更强，但参与者更偏爱自创密码。值得注意的是，对上下文线索的视觉注意力与更高的密码熵显著相关。

Conclusion: 安全性不仅由生成工具决定，还受用户对上下文线索的视觉参与度影响，这突显了注意力驱动安全设计的潜力。研究强调了在设计密码系统时需要考虑用户行为和心理因素。

Abstract: Passwords remain the primary authentication method, yet user-created passwords are often the weakest due to the security-usability trade-off. Although AI-based password generators are emerging, little is known about their effectiveness and user perceptions. This eye-tracking study examined how behavior during password creation, selection, and memorization relates to objective and subjective password quality. Four password models, three AI-based (DeepSeek-API, ChatGPT-API, PassGPT) and one rule-based random generator, generated suggestions from participants' self-generated passwords across four website contexts. Eye movements were recorded throughout the experiment. Results confirm the expected trade-off between AI-generated password strength and human memorability but also reveal a novel behavioral link. Despite stronger AI-generated passwords, participants favored self-generated ones. Notably, visual attention to contextual cues was significantly correlated with higher password entropy. This suggests that security is shaped not only by the generation tool but also by users' visual engagement with contextual cues, highlighting the potential of attention-driven security design.

</details>


### [120] [MER 2026: From Discriminative Emotion Recognition to Generative Emotion Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19417)
*Zheng Lian,Xiaojiang Peng,Kele Xu,Ziyu Jia,Xinyi Che,Zebang Cheng,Fei Ma,Laizhong Cui,Yazhou Zhang,Xin Liu,Liang Yang,Jia Li,Fan Zhang,Erik Cambria,Guoying Zhao,Bjorn W. Schuller,Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MER2026是MER系列挑战赛的第四版，延续了从判别式情感识别向生成式情感理解的转变趋势，包含四个赛道：从个体转向二元交互场景的MER-Cross、细粒度情感识别的MER-FG、预测人类对不同情感描述偏好的MER-Prefer，以及基于生理信号的情感识别MER-PS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MER系列挑战赛旨在为研究社区提供有价值的数据资源，并围绕最新研究趋势设计任务。随着研究重点从传统的判别式情感识别转向生成式情感理解，需要开发能够利用多模态大语言模型丰富词汇和跨模态理解能力的新方法，以实现更细粒度和可解释的情感分析。

Method: MER2026延续了前几届的研究轨迹，设计了四个不同的赛道：1) MER-Cross：将研究重点从个体场景转向二元交互场景；2) MER-FG：专注于细粒度情感识别；3) MER-Prefer：预测人类对不同情感描述的偏好；4) MER-PS：基于生理信号进行情感识别。这些任务旨在充分利用MLLMs的广泛词汇和多模态理解能力。

Result: MER2026作为该系列挑战赛的第四版，建立了从MER2023的判别式情感识别（固定基本标签）到MER2024-2025的生成式情感理解（细粒度情感识别和描述性情感分析）的连续发展轨迹。该挑战赛已成为该领域最大的挑战之一，提供了全面的数据集和基线模型。

Conclusion: MER2026继续推动情感计算领域从传统的判别式方法向生成式情感理解发展，通过四个不同但互补的赛道，探索了情感分析在交互场景、细粒度识别、描述偏好和生理信号等多个维度的前沿研究方向，为社区提供了重要的研究平台和数据资源。

Abstract: MER2026 marks the fourth edition of the MER series of challenges. The MER series provides valuable data resources to the research community and offers tasks centered on recent research trends, establishing itself as one of the largest challenges in the field. Throughout its history, the focus of MER has shifted from discriminative emotion recognition to generative emotion understanding. Specifically, MER2023 concentrated on discriminative emotion recognition, restricting the emotion recognition scope to fixed basic labels. In MER2024 and MER2025, we transitioned to generative emotion understanding and introduced two new tasks: fine-grained emotion recognition and descriptive emotion analysis, aiming to leverage the extensive vocabulary and multimodal understanding capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to facilitate fine-grained and explainable emotion recognition. Building on this trajectory, MER2026 continues to follow these research trends and contains four tracks: MER-Cross shifts the focus from individual to dyadic interaction scenarios; MER-FG centers on fine-grained emotion recognition; MER-Prefer aims to predict human preferences regarding different emotion descriptions; MER-PS focuses on emotion recognition based on physiological signals. More details regarding the dataset and baselines are available at https://zeroqiaoba.github.io/MER-Challenge.

</details>


### [121] [Allow Me Into Your Dream: A Handshake-and-Pull Protocol for Sharing Mixed Realities in Spontaneous Encounters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19423)
*Botao Amber Hu,Yilan Elan Tao,Bernhard Riecke,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TouchPort提出了一种基于握手的MR共享协议，将复杂的多阶段共享流程简化为单一手势，解决公共场景下MR交互的社会可读性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前混合现实系统缺乏在公共场合进入他人MR空间的社会可读性协议。MR共享需要复杂的多阶段流程（发现、同意、确认、允许、空间共置、对象同步、权限管理），但用户期望体验像文件传输一样简单。

Method: 提出TouchPort协议，通过握手和拉拽的单一手势同时传达意图、协商同意并启动临时共享层。该协议将多阶段流程压缩为单一动作，使用AirDrop和Apple Vision Pro SharePlay作为基线对比。

Result: 通过三个隐含场景展示了该协议在从孤立到自发共享现实过渡中的表达范围。手势协议能够解决无处不在的MR中的同意问题，同时探讨了MR未来中相遇协议的伦理张力。

Conclusion: TouchPort通过具体化手势为MR共享提供了社会可读性协议，将复杂流程简化为单一交互，同时需要平衡便利性与伦理考量，为未来MR交互设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Mixed reality systems support shared anchors and co-located interaction, yet they lack a socially legible protocol for entering another person's mixed reality in public settings. We frame this as a protocol problem: co-located MR sharing requires a staged sequence -- Discover, Consent, Confirm, Allow, Spatial Colocation, Sync Objects, Permission Management -- each demanding user understanding and agreement. Using AirDrop and Apple Vision Pro SharePlay as a baseline, we show that MR encounter complexity far exceeds file transfer, yet must feel equally effortless. We present TouchPort, an embodied sharing protocol that collapses this multi-stage sequence into a single gesture: a handshake and pull that simultaneously signals intent, negotiates consent, and initiates a temporary shared encounter layer between otherwise separate mixed realities. Through three implied scenarios, we demonstrate the protocol's expressive range in the transition from isolated to spontaneously shared realities. We discuss how embodied gestures can address the consent problem in ubiquitous MR and examine the ethical tensions of encounter protocols for MR futures.

</details>


### [122] [Seeing Your Mindless Face: How Viewing One's Live Self Interrupts Mindless Short-Form Video Scrolling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19424)
*Kyungjin Kim,Minjeong Kim,Soobeen Jeong,Jiyeon So,Hayeon Song*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究开发了一款通过显示自我相关线索（实时摄像头、自拍、文字姓名、黑屏）来帮助用户减少短视频过度使用的应用，发现这些线索能有效打断无意识观看，其中黑屏效果最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频的广泛成瘾性消费引发"脑退化"担忧，成为紧迫的公共健康问题。需要探索内在的自我反思策略来增强用户对媒体过度使用的自我控制能力。

Method: 开发了一款应用，通过周期性显示不同类型的自我相关线索（实时摄像头、自拍、文字姓名、黑屏）来使用户"去沉浸化"。在实验室实验中测试了这些线索的效果（N=84），并通过后续访谈收集用户反馈。

Result: 自我相关线索能有效打断无意识观看，使用户自愿停止短视频消费。有趣的是，作为对照的黑屏引发了最高的应用使用意愿，用户认为黑屏提供的微妙反思优于实时摄像头的显性图像。

Conclusion: 研究结果为在移动环境中实施自我意识干预提供了实用的设计指南，包括最佳模态选择以及实时情境锚定如何增强干预效果，为解决短视频成瘾问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: The widespread, addictive consumption of short-form videos, which allegedly causes "brain rot," has become an urgent public concern. This study proposes that self-related cues serve as an intrinsic, self-reflective strategy that enhances self-control over media overuse. We developed an app that de-immerses users by periodically displaying different self-related cues (live camera, selfie, name in text, and black screen) and tested their effects in a laboratory experiment (N=84). Overall, findings show that self-related cues effectively disrupt mindless viewing, enabling users to voluntarily stop short-form video consumption. Interestingly, the black screen, intended as a control, elicited the greatest intention to use the app: Participants noted in the follow-up interview that they preferred the subtler reflection on a black screen over the explicit image from a live camera. The findings offer practical design guidelines for implementing self-awareness interventions in mobile contexts, including which modalities work best and how real-time contextual anchoring enhances effectiveness.

</details>


### [123] [Discerning Authorship in Online Health Communities: Experience, Trust, and Transparency Implications for Moderating AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19429)
*Yefim Shulman,Agnieszka Kitkowska,Mark Warner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究在线健康社区中用户区分AI生成与人类撰写健康建议的能力，探讨透明度与信任对AI建议采纳的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成健康建议可能侵蚀社区信任，特别是当用户无法识别建议来源时。研究旨在探索社区检测健康建议作者身份的可行性，以及AI自我调节如何帮助提高建议利用率

Method: 通过在线实验评估人们区分AI生成与人类撰写健康建议的能力，考虑三个因素：1)对特定健康问题的生活经验；2)AI识别培训；3)用户对AI使用的透明度和信任态度。研究两种健康条件，结合定量和定性分析

Result: 1) 几乎没有证据表明人们能够区分建议的作者身份；2) 健康条件对判断有持续影响；3) 定性分析发现不可靠的信号导致启发式评估存在缺陷；4) 需要透明度与信任相结合

Conclusion: 研究指出改进基于LLM的AI自我调节和辅助社区AI调节的机会，强调透明度与信任对在线健康社区中AI建议采纳的重要性

Abstract: For online health communities, community trust is paramount. Yet, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) generating advice may erode this trust, especially if users cannot identify whether LLMs have been used. We investigate the feasibility of community-based detection of health advice authorship and how self-moderation of LLMs could help enhance advice utilization. In an online experiment, we evaluate people's ability to distinguish AI-generated from human-written advice across two health conditions, considering lived experience with a condition, AI-recognition training, and user attitudes towards transparency and trust around AI use. Our results indicate the need for transparency coupled with trust. We find little evidence of people's ability to discern advice authorship. However, we find a consistent effect of the health condition. Our qualitative findings identify unreliable signals, resulting in flawed heuristic evaluations of the advice. Our findings point to opportunities to improve the self-moderation of LLM-based AI and aid community-based AI moderation.

</details>


### [124] [InvestChat: Exploring Multimodal Interaction via Natural Language, Touch, and Pen in an Investment Dashboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19537)
*Sarah Lykke Tost,Adson Lucas de Paiva Sales,Henrik Østergaard,Vaishali Dhanoa,Gabriela Molina León*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: InvestChat是一款支持股票市场探索的多模态平板应用，结合了协调视图和LLM驱动的聊天功能，通过12名新手投资者的评估发现多模态交互能提升用户参与度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为股票市场探索开发更自然、高效的多模态交互界面，特别是针对新手投资者，通过结合自然语言、触摸和笔输入来提升用户体验和参与度

Method: 设计并实现了InvestChat，这是一个基于平板的多模态应用，包含多个协调视图和LLM驱动的聊天功能，然后对12名新手投资者进行了用户评估研究

Result: 研究发现多模态交互能促进用户参与，参与者以互补方式利用不同模态，享受选择自由，并认为自然语言交互最为有效

Conclusion: 在股票市场探索中结合自然语言、触摸和笔输入的多模态方法能够有效提升用户参与度和体验，自然语言交互尤其受到用户青睐

Abstract: We designed and implemented InvestChat, a multimodal tablet-based application that supports stock market exploration with multiple coordinated views and an LLM-powered chat. We evaluated the application with 12 novice investors. Our findings suggest that combining natural language, touch, and pen input during stock market exploration facilitates user engagement. Participants leveraged the modalities in complementary ways, enjoying the freedom of choice and finding natural language most effective.

</details>


### [125] [Remindful: Designing Reminder Systems for Caregiver Interpretation in Dementia Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19574)
*Joy Lai,Alex Mihailidis*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Remindful是一个针对痴呆症照护的数字提醒平台，不仅提供任务提示，还通过照护者警报、摘要和回顾功能支持长期参与度解读，强调提醒系统应作为支持照护者解释的辅助基础设施而非中性行为传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字提醒系统主要用于单向任务提示，缺乏帮助照护者解读长期参与度的功能。痴呆症家庭照护需要更全面的支持系统，能够帮助照护者理解提醒交互数据背后的含义和背景。

Method: 采用照护者访谈、生活经验顾问输入和两个照护者-痴呆症患者家庭的实地部署。通过形成性研究了解照护者需求，开发Remindful平台，并在真实家庭环境中测试其功能。

Result: 提醒系统能够支持照护者安心感、家庭协调和日常习惯的长期意识。但提醒交互数据高度依赖背景，受家庭参与度、提示归因、日常习惯不匹配、可访问性障碍和技术故障等因素影响。

Conclusion: 提醒系统不应被视为中性行为传感器，而应设计为支持照护者解释的辅助基础设施，保留不确定性并支持在真实家庭环境中进行背景意义建构。

Abstract: Digital reminder systems are widely used in dementia care to support everyday tasks, but they are typically designed for one-way prompting rather than helping caregivers interpret engagement over time. We present Remindful, a caregiver-informed reminder platform that extends task prompting with caregiver-facing alerts, summaries, and review features to support awareness in home-based dementia care. Drawing on formative caregiver interviews, lived-experience advisor input, and in-home deployments with two caregiver-PLwD dyads, we examine how reminder-based caregiver awareness functions in practice. Our findings show that reminder systems can support caregiver reassurance, household coordination, and awareness of routines over time, but that reminder interaction data is highly context-dependent. Household participation, prompt attribution, routine mismatch, accessibility barriers, and technical failures all shaped what reminder logs could reasonably mean. We argue that reminder systems should not be treated as neutral behavioral sensors, but designed as assistive infrastructures for caregiver interpretation that preserve uncertainty and support contextual sensemaking in real homes.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [The Public Health and Environmental Surveillance Open Data Model (PHES-ODM) Version 3: An Open, Relational Data Model and Interoperability Framework for Wastewater Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18762)
*Mathew Thomson,Jean-David Therrien,Nikho Hizon,Janet Lin,Martin Wellman,Eugen-Sorin Sion,Carol Bennett,Peter Van Rolleghem,Douglas Manuel*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PHES-ODM v3是一个用于废水监测数据标准化的开放数据模型，旨在解决数据碎片化、元数据不一致和互操作性差的问题，已在25个国家使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 废水监测已成为重要的公共卫生监测工具，但其长期效用受到数据系统碎片化、元数据实践不一致和互操作性差的限制，需要标准化框架来支持符合FAIR原则的透明、伦理数据使用。

Method: 开发PHES-ODM v3作为开放协作框架，新增公共卫生行动表、外部存储库链接（如GISAID、GenBank）、分析工作流文档等功能，支持跨站点、样本、测量和人群的复杂关系链接，并提供与其他数据格式（包括PHA4GE和US CDC NWSS）的映射工具。

Result: PHES-ODM已被加拿大公共卫生署采用并被欧盟污水哨兵系统改编，目前在25个国家使用。v3版本针对25个特征与其他6个废水监测数据标准进行了比较，平衡了鲁棒性和可用性。

Conclusion: PHES-ODM v3提供了一个可扩展、模块化的基础设施，能够适应多样化的废水监测和环境监测项目，解决了互操作性和数据效用的持续障碍。

Abstract: Wastewater surveillance (WWS) has emerged as a valuable tool for public health surveillance, particularly since the COVID-19 pandemic. Its long-term utility is constrained, however, by fragmented data systems, inconsistent metadata practices, and poor interoperability. The Public Health and Environmental Surveillance Open Data Model (PHES-ODM) was developed as an open, collaborative framework to standardize WWS data and support transparent, ethical data use aligned with FAIR principles. Adopted by the Public Health Agency of Canada and adapted by the EU Sewage Sentinel System, the model is now used in over 25 countries. This paper introduces version 3 of the model, which addresses persistent barriers to interoperability and data utility. Key enhancements include new tables for public health actions, external repository linkages (e.g., GISAID, GenBank), and analytical workflow documentation, as well as support for complex relational linkages across sites, samples, measures, and populations. Tools for mapping across other data formats, including PHA4GE and the US CDC National Wastewater Surveillance System, and for supporting long and wide data formats are also introduced. We compare PHES-ODM against six other WWS data standards across 25 features. Balancing robustness with usability, PHES-ODM v3 provides a scalable, modular infrastructure adaptable to diverse WWS and environmental surveillance programs.

</details>


### [127] [Heuristic Search Space Partitioning for Low-Latency Multi-Tenant Cloud Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19057)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak,Chandra Biksheswaran Mouleeswaran,Rama Teja Repaka*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: HSSPS系统通过动态谓词注入实现查询空间逻辑分区，显著降低大规模云安全平台中跨租户查询的延迟，将P95延迟从61秒降至2秒，吞吐量提升8-10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模云安全平台需要持续查询分布在数千租户账户中的数百万结构化云资源记录。跨账户的广泛查询会使数据库基础设施饱和，导致P95延迟超过60秒。研究发现缓冲区缓存压力是主要延迟驱动因素，查询计划优化无法解决此问题，唯一有效干预是减少每个查询必须访问的页面数量。

Method: 提出启发式搜索空间分区系统（HSSPS），这是一个查询时优化层，通过动态谓词注入实现搜索空间的逻辑分区，无需修改模式。采用两阶段启发式引擎：1）选择分区键值；2）在执行前对候选查询计划进行评分。客户端页面令牌维护跨分区遍历状态，无需服务器端会话，支持水平扩展。

Result: 在代表性查询类型的受控评估中，P95延迟降低50-97%（高基数查询降低95-97%），吞吐量提升8-10倍，平均活动会话减少41倍。在生产环境中，跨实时多租户流量将P95延迟从61秒降至2秒，每个测量窗口维持超过14,000个符合条件的查询。

Conclusion: HSSPS技术适用于任何需要在大规模共享数据库上执行广泛查询且物理模式修改不可行的多租户系统。该方法通过减少查询必须访问的页面数量，有效解决了缓冲区缓存压力导致的延迟问题。

Abstract: Large-scale cloud security platforms must continuously query millions of structured cloud resource records distributed across thousands of tenant accounts. Broad, account-spanning queries saturate database infrastructure, producing P95 latencies exceeding 60 seconds. We identify buffer cache pressure as the dominant latency driver: in a controlled experiment, the same query executing with the same plan completed in 3.7 seconds when its working set was memory-resident and 94 seconds when concurrent load had evicted those pages. No query plan optimization can address this; the only effective intervention is reducing the number of pages each query must touch. We present the Heuristic Search Space Partitioning System (HSSPS), a query-time optimization layer that logically partitions the search space through dynamic predicate injection, without schema modification. A two-phase heuristic engine selects partition key values and scores candidate query plans before execution. A client-side page token maintains cross-partition traversal state without server-side sessions, enabling horizontal scalability. Controlled evaluation across representative query types demonstrates 50-97% P95 latency reduction (95-97% on high-cardinality queries), 8-10x throughput improvement, and 41x reduction in average active sessions. Production rollout across live multi-tenant traffic reduced P95 latency from 61s to 2s across successive releases, sustained over 14,000 eligible queries per measurement window. The technique generalizes to any multi-tenant system where broad queries execute against large shared databases and physical schema modification is impractical.

</details>


### [128] [Demonstrating Online Schema Alignment in Decentralized Knowledge Graphs Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19205)
*Bryan-Elliott Tam,Pieter Colpaert,Ruben Taelman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种用于链接遍历查询处理的在线模式对齐方法，在查询执行期间动态发现、限定和应用对齐规则，以解决去中心化知识图谱查询中的词汇异构性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化知识图谱查询能够集成分布式数据而无需集中化，但对词汇异构性高度敏感。查询发起者无法实际预测所有词汇不匹配，特别是当对齐规则是局部的、限定范围的或在运行时发现的

Method: 提出在线模式对齐方法，在链接遍历查询处理过程中动态发现、限定和应用对齐规则，同时保留遍历行为。基于Comunica构建的LTQP引擎实现，提供Web界面、CLI和可重用库

Result: 演示表明在线模式对齐能够以低开销恢复完整的查询结果，为LTQP系统中的Web规模推理提供了实用基础

Conclusion: 在线模式对齐方法为链接遍历查询处理中的词汇异构性问题提供了有效的解决方案，能够在去中心化环境中动态处理模式对齐，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: Decentralized Knowledge Graphs querying enables integrating distributed data without centralization, but is highly sensitive to vocabulary heterogeneity. Query issuers cannot realistically anticipate all vocabulary mismatches, especially when alignment rules are local, scoped, or discovered at runtime. We present an online schema alignment approach for Link Traversal Query Processing (LTQP) that discovers, scopes, and applies alignment rules dynamically during query execution while preserving traversal behavior. This demo paper demonstrates the approach on a decentralized social-media scenario through a web interface built on a Comunica-based LTQP engine. Source code, a CLI, and a reusable library are publicly available. The demonstration shows that online schema alignment recovers complete query results with low overhead, providing a practical foundation for web-scale reasoning in LTQP systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Adding Compilation Metadata To Binaries To Make Disassembly Decidable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19628)
*Daniel Engel,Freek Verbeek,Pranav Kumar,Binoy Ravindran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种在二进制可执行文件中嵌入编译器意图元数据的格式，使二进制文件既能保持闭源特性，又能提供安全相关信息，支持可靠的分析、插桩和重新编译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二进制格式是软件分发和执行的标准方式，但也是最不透明的软件表示形式。如果二进制格式能包含安全相关的元数据（如编译器预期的可执行指令数据、内存区域边界等），将显著提升软件的安全性和可维护性。

Method: 提出一种介于剥离的黑盒二进制和开源代码之间的二进制格式。开发工具生成捕获编译器意图的元数据，并将其插入到二进制文件中。该元数据支持提升到正确且可重新编译的高级表示，使分析和插桩更可靠。

Result: 评估表明添加元数据不影响运行时行为或性能。与DWARF相比，元数据大小约为其17%。通过编译全面的真实世界C/C++二进制文件集验证正确性，证明它们可以被提升、插桩和重新编译而不改变行为。

Conclusion: 提出的二进制格式在保持二进制不透明性的同时，通过嵌入编译器意图元数据，为软件安全分析和维护提供了有效的中间方案，实现了安全性和可维护性的显著提升。

Abstract: The binary executable format is the standard method for distributing and executing software. Yet, it is also as opaque a representation of software as can be. If the binary format were augmented with metadata that provides security-relevant information, such as which data is intended by the compiler to be executable instructions, or how memory regions are expected to be bounded, that would dramatically improve the safety and maintainability of software. In this paper, we propose a binary format that is a middle ground between a stripped black-box binary and open source. We provide a tool that generates metadata capturing the compiler's intent and inserts it into the binary. This metadata enables lifting to a correct and recompilable higher-level representation and makes analysis and instrumentation more reliable. Our evaluation shows that adding metadata does not affect runtime behavior or performance. Compared to DWARF, our metadata is roughly 17% of its size. We validate correctness by compiling a comprehensive set of real-world C and C++ binaries and demonstrating that they can be lifted, instrumented, and recompiled without altering their behavior.

</details>


### [130] [Position: No Retroactive Cure for Infringement during Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18649)
*Satoru Utsunomiya,Masaru Isonuma,Junichiro Mori,Ichiro Sakata*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文认为生成式AI的后处理缓解方法（如机器遗忘和推理时护栏）无法追溯性地解决非法获取和训练数据带来的法律责任，因为合规性取决于数据来源而非输出结果。作者主张从后处理净化转向可验证的事前流程合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI面临日益严峻的法律挑战，机器学习社区越来越依赖后处理缓解措施来主张合规性。但作者认为这种后处理方法存在根本性缺陷，无法解决数据获取和训练过程中的违法问题，需要重新思考合规策略。

Method: 论文通过三部分论证构建理论框架：1) 分析未经授权的复制/摄取作为法律上已完成的侵权行为；2) 探讨合同和侵权/不正当竞争规则如何独立限制数据访问和使用；3) 论证保护性输入的价值如何在模型权重中持续存在，从而引发不当得利和剥夺收益等法律救济。

Result: 论文得出结论：后处理缓解方法无法追溯性地治愈非法数据获取和训练带来的法律责任，因为合规性核心在于数据来源而非输出结果。模型权重可能作为固定副本保留训练衍生的表达价值，使得后续过滤对侵权问题无关紧要。

Conclusion: 作者主张从"后处理净化"范式转向"可验证的事前流程合规"，强调在数据获取和训练阶段就需要建立合规机制，而非依赖训练后的技术修复。这种转变对于生成式AI的长期法律可持续性至关重要。

Abstract: As generative AI faces intensifying legal challenges, the machine learning community has increasingly relied on post-hoc mitigation -- especially machine unlearning and inference-time guardrails -- to argue for compliance. This paper argues that such post-hoc mitigation methods cannot retroactively cure liability from unlawful acquisition and training, because compliance hinges on data lineage, not the outputs. Our argument has three parts. First, unauthorized copying/ingestion can be a legally complete completed act, and model weights may operate as fixed copies that retain training-derived expressive value, making later filtering beside the point for infringement. Second, contract and tort/unfair-competition rules -- via licenses, terms of service, and anti-free-riding principles -- can independently restrict access and use, often bypassing copyright defenses (e.g., fair use or TDM exceptions). Third, since value from protected inputs can persist in weights, remedies such as unjust enrichment and disgorgement may require stripping gains and, in some cases, reaching the model itself. We therefore argue for a shift from Post-Hoc Sanitization to verifiable Ex-Ante Process Compliance.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Explicit Refusals: Soft-Failure Attacks on Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18663)
*Wentao Zhang,Yan Zhuang,ZhuHang Zheng,Mingfei Zhang,Jiawen Deng,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对RAG系统的隐蔽攻击方法DEJA，通过生成对抗性文档诱导模型产生流畅但无信息的"软失败"响应，而非显式拒绝或拒绝服务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对RAG系统的干扰攻击通常导致显式拒绝或拒绝服务行为，这些行为明显且易于检测。本文旨在形式化一种更隐蔽的可用性威胁——软失败，通过诱导流畅连贯但无信息的响应来降低系统效用

Method: 提出DEJA（欺骗性进化干扰攻击）框架，采用黑盒攻击方式，利用大语言模型的安全对齐行为生成对抗性文档。使用基于进化优化的方法，通过LLM评估器计算的细粒度答案效用分数（AUS）指导，系统性地降低答案确定性同时保持高检索成功率

Result: 在多个RAG配置和基准数据集上的实验表明，DEJA能持续将响应推向低效用软失败，SASR超过79%同时保持硬失败率低于15%，显著优于先前攻击。生成的对抗性文档具有高隐蔽性，能规避基于困惑度的检测，抵抗查询改写，并能跨模型家族迁移到专有系统

Conclusion: DEJA攻击揭示了RAG系统面临的新型隐蔽威胁，即通过诱导看似正常但无实际信息的响应来降低系统效用，这种攻击具有高隐蔽性和跨模型迁移能力，对RAG系统的安全防御提出了新挑战

Abstract: Existing jamming attacks on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems typically induce explicit refusals or denial-of-service behaviors, which are conspicuous and easy to detect. In this work, we formalize a subtler availability threat, termed soft failure, which degrades system utility by inducing fluent and coherent yet non-informative responses rather than overt failures. We propose Deceptive Evolutionary Jamming Attack (DEJA), an automated black-box attack framework that generates adversarial documents to trigger such soft failures by exploiting safety-aligned behaviors of large language models. DEJA employs an evolutionary optimization process guided by a fine-grained Answer Utility Score (AUS), computed via an LLM-based evaluator, to systematically degrade the certainty of answers while maintaining high retrieval success. Extensive experiments across multiple RAG configurations and benchmark datasets show that DEJA consistently drives responses toward low-utility soft failures, achieving SASR above 79\% while keeping hard-failure rates below 15\%, significantly outperforming prior attacks. The resulting adversarial documents exhibit high stealth, evading perplexity-based detection and resisting query paraphrasing, and transfer across model families to proprietary systems without retargeting.

</details>


### [132] [TrEEStealer: Stealing Decision Trees via Enclave Side Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18716)
*Jonas Sander,Anja Rabich,Nick Mahling,Felix Maurer,Jonah Heller,Qifan Wang,Thomas Eisenbarth,David Oswald*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrEEStealer：针对TEE保护决策树的高保真提取攻击，利用TEE侧信道窃取模型，无需强API假设


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在敏感应用中广泛部署，模型提取攻击威胁MLaaS商业模式。现有黑盒提取攻击查询密集或假设过强，而TEE本应提供硬件级保护，但存在侧信道漏洞

Method: TrEEStealer利用TEE特定侧信道（AMD SEV性能计数器、Intel SGX分支历史寄存器）获取控制流信息，结合被动信息跟踪最大化每查询信息量，针对OpenCV、mlpack、emlearn库

Result: 攻击在效率和提取保真度上优于现有方法，证实TEE无法防止控制流泄漏，建立了决策树提取的新技术标准

Conclusion: TEE硬件机制无法完全保护决策树模型，控制流泄漏是严重安全威胁，需要新的防护措施

Abstract: Today, machine learning is widely applied in sensitive, security-related, and financially lucrative applications. Model extraction attacks undermine current business models where a model owner sells model access, e.g., via MLaaS APIs. Additionally, stolen models can enable powerful white-box attacks, facilitating privacy attacks on sensitive training data, and model evasion.
  In this paper, we focus on Decision Trees (DT), which are widely deployed in practice. Existing black-box extraction attacks for DTs are either query-intensive, make strong assumptions about the DT structure, or rely on rich API information. To limit attacks to the black-box setting, CPU vendors introduced Trusted Execution Environments (TEE) that use hardware-mechanisms to isolate workloads from external parties, e.g., MLaaS providers. We introduce TrEEStealer, a high-fidelity extraction attack for stealing TEE-protected DTs. TrEEStealer exploits TEE-specific side-channels to steal DTs efficiently and without strong assumptions about the API output or DT structure. The extraction efficacy stems from a novel algorithm that maximizes the information derived from each query by coupling Control-Flow Information (CFI) with passive information tracking. We use two primitives to acquire CFI: for AMD SEV, we follow previous work using the SEV-Step framework and performance counters. For Intel SGX, we reproduce prior findings on current Xeon 6 CPUs and construct a new primitive to efficiently extract the branch history of inference runs through the Branch-History-Register.
  We found corresponding vulnerabilities in three popular libraries: OpenCV, mlpack, and emlearn. We show that TrEEStealer achieves superior efficiency and extraction fidelity compared to prior attacks. Our work establishes a new state-of-the-art for DT extraction and confirms that TEEs fail to protect against control-flow leakage.

</details>


### [133] [Towards Optimal Agentic Architectures for Offensive Security Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18718)
*Isaac David,Arthur Gervais*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究评估多智能体安全审计系统的拓扑结构选择，发现更广泛的协调能提升覆盖率，但综合考虑延迟、成本和验证难度后并非最优，白盒模式显著优于黑盒模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于工具使用LLM的智能安全系统通常固定单一协调拓扑结构，但缺乏对何时增加智能体有帮助、何时只会增加成本的实证研究，需要系统性地评估不同拓扑结构在安全审计中的效果

Method: 构建包含20个交互式目标（10个Web/API和10个二进制）的受控基准测试，每个目标暴露一个可通过端点访问的真实漏洞；在核心研究中执行600次运行，涵盖五种架构系列、三种模型系列和白盒/黑盒两种访问模式；使用引导置信区间和配对目标级差异进行统计分析

Result: 总体检测率为58.0%，已验证检测率为49.8%；MAS-Indep架构获得最高已验证检测率（64.2%），SAS架构在效率上表现最佳（每个已验证发现成本$0.058）；白盒模式显著优于黑盒模式（67.0% vs. 32.7%已验证检测率），Web目标显著优于二进制目标（74.3% vs. 25.3%）

Conclusion: 主要发现是非单调的成本-质量前沿：更广泛的协调可以提高覆盖率，但综合考虑延迟、令牌成本和漏洞验证难度后，并不总是最优选择；主要影响因素是可观察性和目标领域，而非拓扑结构本身

Abstract: Agentic security systems increasingly audit live targets with tool-using LLMs, but prior systems fix a single coordination topology, leaving unclear when additional agents help and when they only add cost. We treat topology choice as an empirical systems question. We introduce a controlled benchmark of 20 interactive targets (10 web/API and 10 binary), each exposing one endpoint-reachable ground-truth vulnerability, evaluated in whitebox and blackbox modes. The core study executes 600 runs over five architecture families, three model families, and both access modes, with a separate 60-run long-context pilot reported only in the appendix. On the completed core benchmark, detection-any reaches 58.0% and validated detection reaches 49.8%. MAS-Indep attains the highest validated detection rate (64.2%), while SAS is the strongest efficiency baseline at $0.058 per validated finding. Whitebox materially outperforms blackbox (67.0% vs. 32.7% validated detection), and web materially outperforms binary (74.3% vs. 25.3%). Bootstrap confidence intervals and paired target-level deltas show that the dominant effects are observability and domain, while some leading whitebox topologies remain statistically close. The main result is a non-monotonic cost-quality frontier: broader coordination can improve coverage, but it does not dominate once latency, token cost, and exploit-validation difficulty are taken into account.

</details>


### [134] [Temporal UI State Inconsistency in Desktop GUI Agents: Formalizing and Defending Against TOCTOU Attacks on Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18860)
*Wenpeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GUI智能体通过截图-点击循环控制桌面计算机时存在观察-行动间隙（平均6.51秒），这形成了TOCTOU漏洞窗口，攻击者可在此期间操纵UI状态。论文提出了三种攻击原语，并设计了PUSV三层防御机制来验证行动前的UI状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体在控制桌面计算机时，截图观察和实际执行点击动作之间存在时间延迟（平均6.51秒），这形成了TOCTOU漏洞窗口。攻击者可以在此期间篡改UI状态，导致智能体执行错误操作，但现有系统缺乏对此类漏洞的防御机制。

Method: 论文首先形式化定义了视觉原子性违规问题，并识别了三种具体攻击原语：通知覆盖劫持、窗口焦点操纵和Web DOM注入。然后提出了预执行UI状态验证（PUSV）防御机制，包含三层验证：点击目标的掩码像素SSIM（L1）、全局截图差异（L2a）和X窗口快照差异（L2b）。

Result: 攻击原语B（窗口焦点操纵）实现了100%的动作重定向成功率且观察时无视觉证据。PUSV防御在180次对抗试验中（135次原语A+45次原语B）实现了100%的动作拦截率，零误报，且开销小于0.1秒。但对于原语C（无视觉痕迹的DOM注入），PUSV存在结构性盲点（约0%拦截率）。

Conclusion: GUI智能体存在观察-行动间隙导致的TOCTOU漏洞，需要专门的防御机制。PUSV三层防御能有效对抗前两种攻击原语，但无法防御无视觉痕迹的DOM注入攻击，这需要操作系统和DOM层面的深度防御架构。单一防御层无法提供全面覆盖，验证了分层设计的必要性。

Abstract: GUI agents that control desktop computers via screenshot-and-click loops introduce a new class of vulnerability: the observation-to-action gap (mean 6.51 s on real OSWorld workloads) creates a Time-Of-Check, Time-Of-Use (TOCTOU) window during which an unprivileged attacker can manipulate the UI state. We formalize this as a Visual Atomicity Violation and characterize three concrete attack primitives: (A) Notification Overlay Hijack, (B) Window Focus Manipulation, and (C) Web DOM Injection. Primitive B, the closest desktop analog to Android Action Rebinding, achieves 100% action-redirection success rate with zero visual evidence at the observation time. We propose Pre-execution UI State Verification (PUSV), a lightweight three-layer defense that re-verifies the UI state immediately before each action dispatch: masked pixel SSIM at the click target (L1), global screenshot diff (L2a), and X Window snapshot diff (L2b). PUSV achieves 100% Action Interception Rate across 180 adversarial trials (135 Primitive A + 45 Primitive B) with zero false positives and < 0.1 s overhead. Against Primitive C (zero-visual-footprint DOM injection), PUSV reveals a structural blind spot (~0% AIR), motivating future OS+DOM defense-in-depth architectures. No single PUSV layer alone achieves full coverage; different primitives require different detection signals, validating the layered design.

</details>


### [135] [SAGE: Signal-Amplified Guided Embeddings for LLM-based Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19031)
*Zhengyang Shan,Xu Qian,Jiayun Xin,Minghui Xu,Yue Zhang,Zhen Yang,Hao Wu,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SAGE框架通过稀疏自编码器主动恢复LLM中淹没的漏洞信号，解决了信号淹没问题，在多个漏洞检测数据集上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在漏洞检测中存在信号淹没问题——漏洞相关特征在内部被激活但被主导的功能语义数值上淹没，这限制了LLM在漏洞检测中的性能。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，集成任务条件稀疏自编码器来隔离和放大微弱的漏洞信号，通过稀疏流形投影将内部信噪比提升12.7倍。

Result: 在BigVul、PrimeVul和PreciseBugs上实现SOTA性能；7B模型在未见分布上获得318% MCC增益，在经典数据集上获得319%增益；在13种编程语言上保持鲁棒性能，超越34B基线。

Conclusion: SAGE通过主动信号恢复机制解决了LLM中的信号淹没问题，为软件安全提供了比简单参数扩展更高效和可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities are a primary threat to modern infrastructure. While static analysis and Graph Neural Networks have long served as the foundation for vulnerability detection, the emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has introduced a transformative paradigm driven by superior semantic reasoning and cross-environment generalization. However, in the context of LLM-based vulnerability detection, we identify a fundamental bottleneck in these models termed \textbf{Signal Submersion}: a state where features related to vulnerability are activated internally but numerically overwhelmed by dominant functional semantics. To address this, we propose \textbf{SAGE} (\textbf{S}ignal-\textbf{A}mplified \textbf{G}uided \textbf{E}mbeddings), a framework that shifts from passive signal submersion to active signal recovery. SAGE integrates task-conditional Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to isolate and amplify these faint vulnerability signals. Extensive evaluations on BigVul, PrimeVul, and PreciseBugs demonstrate that SAGE achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, SAGE mitigates Signal Submersion by increasing the internal Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) by 12.7$\times$ via sparse manifold projection. This mechanistic intervention enables a 7B model to achieve up to 318\% Matthews Correlation Coefficient (MCC) gains on unseen distributions and a 319\% gain on classic datasets. By maintaining robust performance across 13 programming languages and outperforming 34B baselines, SAGE establishes a more efficient and scalable path to software security than simple parameter scaling.

</details>


### [136] [ProjLens: Unveiling the Role of Projectors in Multimodal Model Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19083)
*Kun Wang,Cheng Qian,Miao Yu,Lilan Peng,Liang Lin,Jiaming Zhang,Tianyu Zhang,Yu Cheng,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ProjLens是一个解释性框架，用于揭示多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中后门攻击的机制，发现后门关键参数编码在投影器的低秩子空间中，激活机制涉及语义偏移与输入范数的线性缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型在跨模态理解和生成方面取得了显著成功，但其部署受到关键安全漏洞的威胁。先前工作已证明通过微调数据投毒在MLLMs中植入后门的可行性，但后门攻击的底层机制仍然不透明，这阻碍了对攻击的理解和缓解。

Method: 提出ProjLens解释性框架，首先证明即使仅限于投影器微调的正常下游任务对齐也会引入后门注入漏洞。通过四种后门变体的广泛实验，分析后门注入的更新结构和激活机制。

Result: 发现：(1) 低秩结构：后门注入更新总体上呈现全秩且缺乏专门的"触发神经元"，但后门关键参数编码在投影器的低秩子空间中；(2) 激活机制：干净和中毒嵌入都朝着与后门目标对齐的共享方向发生语义偏移，但偏移幅度与输入范数线性缩放，导致中毒样本上出现明显的后门激活。

Conclusion: ProjLens框架揭示了MLLMs后门攻击的底层机制，为理解和缓解这类安全威胁提供了重要见解，有助于开发更安全的MLLMs部署方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in cross-modal understanding and generation, yet their deployment is threatened by critical safety vulnerabilities. While prior works have demonstrated the feasibility of backdoors in MLLMs via fine-tuning data poisoning to manipulate inference, the underlying mechanisms of backdoor attacks remain opaque, complicating the understanding and mitigation. To bridge this gap, we propose ProjLens, an interpretability framework designed to demystify MLLMs backdoors. We first establish that normal downstream task alignment--even when restricted to projector fine--tuning--introduces vulnerability to backdoor injection, whose activation mechanism is different from that observed in text-only LLMs. Through extensive experiments across four backdoor variants, we uncover:(1) Low-Rank Structure: Backdoor injection updates appear overall full-rank and lack dedicated ``trigger neurons'', but the backdoor-critical parameters are encoded within a low-rank subspace of the projector;(2) Activation Mechanism: Both clean and poisoned embedding undergoes a semantic shift toward a shared direction aligned with the backdoor target, but the shifting magnitude scales linearly with the input norm, resulting in the distinct backdoor activation on poisoned samples. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProjLens-8FD7

</details>


### [137] [Dual-Guard: Dual-Channel Latent Watermarking for Provenance and Tamper Localization in Diffusion Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19090)
*JinFeng Xie,Chengfu Ou,Peipeng Yu,Xiaoyu Zhou,Dingding Huang,Jianwei Fei,Zixuan Shen,Zhihua Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Dual-Guard是一个双通道潜在水印框架，通过高斯阴影水印和潜在指纹编解码器的组合，实现AI生成内容的溯源验证、对抗重提示攻击和区域级篡改定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于扩散的生成模型快速普及，AI生成内容（AIGC）的溯源和完整性验证问题日益突出。现有单域水印方法存在以下问题：在再生场景下失效、易受黑盒重提示攻击（对抗性框架攻击）、无法提供篡改区域的空间证据。

Method: 提出Dual-Guard双通道潜在水印框架，结合两种互补锚点：1）在初始扩散噪声中嵌入高斯阴影水印作为全局溯源信号；2）在最终去噪潜在空间中嵌入潜在指纹编解码器作为结构化内容锚点。重提示攻击倾向于保留前者而破坏后者，而局部编辑仅影响篡改区域的内容锚点。

Result: 在2400个样本的基准测试中，完整模式下Dual-Guard保持干净图像认证的假拒绝率和篡改假警报率低于0.5%，同时在重提示、扩散编辑和八种局部篡改攻击下保持接近完全的检测率。

Conclusion: Dual-Guard通过双通道水印设计有效解决了AIGC溯源验证的关键挑战，实现了对抗重提示攻击的鲁棒性、精确的篡改区域定位，为AI生成内容的完整性和可信度验证提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of diffusion-based generative models has intensified concerns over the attribution and integrity of AI-generated content (AIGC). Existing single-domain watermarking methods either fail under regeneration, remain vulnerable to black-box reprompting that enables adversarial framing, or provide no spatial evidence for tampered regions. We propose Dual-Guard, a dual-channel latent watermarking framework for practical provenance verification, framing resistance, and region-level tamper localization. Dual-Guard combines two complementary anchors: a Gaussian Shading watermark in the initial diffusion noise as a global provenance signal, and a Latent Fingerprint Codec in the final denoised latent as a structured content anchor. Reprompting tends to preserve the former while breaking the latter, whereas localized edits disturb the content anchor only in tampered regions. In Full mode on a 2,400-sample benchmark, Dual-Guard keeps clean-image authentication false rejection and tamper false alarm below one half of one percent, while maintaining near-complete detection under reprompting, diffusion editing, and eight local tampering attacks.

</details>


### [138] [DP-FlogTinyLLM: Differentially private federated log anomaly detection using Tiny LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19118)
*Isaiah Thompson,Tanmay Sen,Ritwik Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DP-FLogTinyLLM：一种基于参数高效小语言模型的隐私保护联邦日志异常检测框架，在分布式环境下实现协作学习而不共享原始日志数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的日志数据通常分布在多个组织之间，由于隐私和安全限制无法集中化处理。现有的日志异常检测方法（包括基于大语言模型的方法）主要依赖集中式训练，不适合这种分布式环境。

Method: 提出DP-FLogTinyLLM框架，结合联邦优化与差分隐私实现隐私保护协作学习。采用低秩适应（LoRA）技术对每个客户端的小型语言模型进行高效微调，以适应资源受限环境。

Result: 在Thunderbird和BGL数据集上的实验表明，该框架性能与集中式LLM方法相当，同时因隐私机制带来额外计算开销。相比现有联邦基线方法，DP-FLogTinyLLM在精度和F1分数上表现更优，特别是在Thunderbird数据集上显示出更强的异常检测能力和更低的误报率。

Conclusion: DP-FLogTinyLLM是一种有效的隐私保护联邦日志异常检测解决方案，能够在保护数据隐私的同时实现与集中式方法相当的检测性能，特别适合资源受限的分布式环境。

Abstract: Modern distributed systems generate massive volumes of log data that are critical for detecting anomalies and cyber threats. However, in real world settings, these logs are often distributed across multiple organizations and cannot be centralized due to privacy and security constraints. Existing log anomaly detection methods, including recent large language model (LLM) based approaches, largely rely on centralized training and are not suitable for such environments. In this paper, we propose DP-FLogTinyLLM, a privacy preserving federated framework for log anomaly detection using parameter efficient LLMs. Our approach enables collaborative learning without sharing raw log data by integrating federated optimization with differential privacy. To ensure scalability in resource constrained environments, we employ low rank adaptation (LoRA) for efficient fine tuning of Tiny LLMs at each client. Empirical results on the Thunderbird and BGL datasets show that the proposed framework matches the performance of centralized LLM based methods, while incurring additional computational overhead due to privacy mechanisms. Compared to existing federated baselines, DP-FLogTinyLLM consistently achieves higher precision and F1-score, with particularly strong gains on the Thunderbird dataset, highlighting its effectiveness in detecting anomalies while minimizing false positives.

</details>


### [139] [Sherpa.ai Privacy-Preserving Multi-Party Entity Alignment without Intersection Disclosure for Noisy Identifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19219)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin Del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sherpa.ai提出了一种用于纵向联邦学习的多方私有集合并协议，支持精确和容错匹配，保护交集成员隐私


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向联邦学习需要隐私保护的实体对齐，传统私有集合交集方法会泄露交集成员信息，而现有私有集合并方法通常仅限于两方且不支持容错匹配

Method: 提出Sherpa.ai多方PSU协议，将两方方法推广到多方，通信开销低，提供两种变体：保持顺序的精确对齐版本和无序的容错匹配版本

Result: 证明了协议的正确性和隐私性，分析了通信和计算复杂度，形式化了从本地记录到共享索引空间的通用索引映射

Conclusion: 该多方PSU协议为实际纵向联邦学习部署提供了可扩展、数学基础扎实的隐私保护实体对齐方案，适用于医疗、金融、电信等多领域协作

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training among multiple parties without centralizing raw data. There are two main paradigms in FL: Horizontal FL (HFL), where all participants share the same feature space but hold different samples, and Vertical FL (VFL), where parties possess complementary features for the same set of samples. A prerequisite for VFL training is privacy-preserving entity alignment (PPEA), which establishes a common index of samples across parties (alignment) without revealing which samples are shared between them. Conventional private set intersection (PSI) achieves alignment but leaks intersection membership, exposing sensitive relationships between datasets. The standard private set union (PSU) mitigates this risk by aligning on the union of identifiers rather than the intersection. However, existing approaches are often limited to two parties or lack support for typo-tolerant matching.
  In this paper, we introduce the Sherpa.ai multi-party PSU protocol for VFL, a PPEA method that hides intersection membership and enables both exact and noisy matching. The protocol generalizes two-party approaches to multiple parties with low communication overhead and offers two variants: an order-preserving version for exact alignment and an unordered version tolerant to typographical and formatting discrepancies. We prove correctness and privacy, analyze communication and computational (exponentiation) complexity, and formalize a universal index mapping from local records to a shared index space. This multi-party PSU offers a scalable, mathematically grounded protocol for PPEA in real-world VFL deployments, such as multi-institutional healthcare disease detection, collaborative risk modeling between banks and insurers, and cross-domain fraud detection between telecommunications and financial institutions, while preserving intersection privacy.

</details>


### [140] [Malicious ML Model Detection by Learning Dynamic Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19438)
*Sarang Nambiar,Dhruv Pradhan,Ezekiel Soremekun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DynaHug：一种基于动态分析和机器学习的方法，通过分析预训练模型在运行时的行为来检测恶意模型，相比现有方法在F1分数上提升高达44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的恶意预训练模型检测方法（如PickleScan）主要依赖规则、启发式或静态分析，忽略了模型的运行时行为，导致检测效果不佳：要么因黑名单机制而漏报恶意模型，要么因静态分析或白名单机制而误报良性模型。

Method: 提出DynaHug方法：1）使用动态分析捕获预训练模型在运行时的行为特征；2）针对特定任务，使用良性模型的运行时行为训练一个单类支持向量机（OCSVM）分类器；3）采用聚类技术来增强检测效果。

Result: 在超过25,000个来自Hugging Face和MalHug的良性和恶意预训练模型上评估，DynaHug在F1分数上比现有基线方法（包括静态、动态和基于LLM的检测器）提升高达44%。消融研究表明动态分析、OCSVM和聚类等设计决策对DynaHug的有效性均有积极贡献。

Conclusion: DynaHug通过结合动态分析和机器学习，有效解决了现有恶意预训练模型检测方法的局限性，显著提高了检测准确性，为ML供应链安全提供了更可靠的保护。

Abstract: Pre-trained machine learning models (PTMs) are commonly provided via Model Hubs (e.g., Hugging Face) in standard formats like Pickles to facilitate accessibility and reuse. However, this ML supply chain setting is susceptible to malicious attacks that are capable of executing arbitrary code on trusted user environments, e.g., during model loading. To detect malicious PTMs, state-of-the-art detectors (e.g., PickleScan) rely on rules, heuristics, or static analysis, but ignore runtime model behaviors. Consequently, they either miss malicious models due to under-approximation (blacklisting) or miscategorize benign models due to over-approximation (static analysis or whitelisting). To address this challenge, we propose a novel technique (DynaHug) which detects malicious PTMs by learning the behavior of benign PTMs using dynamic analysis and machine learning (ML). DynaHug trains an ML classifier (one-class SVM (OCSVM)) on the runtime behaviours of task-specific benign models. We evaluate DynaHug using over 25,000 benign and malicious PTMs from different sources including Hugging Face and MalHug. We also compare DynaHug to several state-of-the-art detectors including static, dynamic and LLM-based detectors. Results show that DynaHug is up to 44% more effective than existing baselines in terms of F1-score. Our ablation study demonstrates that our design decisions (dynamic analysis, OCSVM, clustering) contribute positively to DynaHug's effectiveness.

</details>


### [141] [Involuntary In-Context Learning: Exploiting Few-Shot Pattern Completion to Bypass Safety Alignment in GPT-5.4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19461)
*Alex Polyakov,Daniel Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"非自愿上下文学习"的攻击方法，通过抽象操作符框架和少量示例强制模式完成，从而绕过大型语言模型的安全对齐训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的安全对齐依赖于行为训练，但当足够强的上下文模式与学习到的拒绝行为竞争时，这种对齐可能被覆盖。研究者旨在探索如何通过上下文学习攻击来绕过安全训练。

Method: 提出非自愿上下文学习攻击方法，使用抽象操作符框架配合少量示例来强制模式完成。通过3479次探测测试10个OpenAI模型，并进行七项消融实验来识别攻击的有效组件。

Result: 关键发现：1) 语义操作符命名达到100%绕过率；2) 攻击需要抽象框架，相同示例在直接问答格式下为0%；3) 示例顺序影响显著；4) 温度参数无显著影响。在HarmBench基准测试中，IICL对GPT-5.4达到24.0%绕过率，而直接查询为0.0%。

Conclusion: 非自愿上下文学习攻击能够有效绕过大型语言模型的安全对齐，揭示了当前安全训练在面对抽象框架和模式完成时的脆弱性，需要新的防御机制来应对此类攻击。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models relies on behavioral training that can be overridden when sufficiently strong in-context patterns compete with learned refusal behaviors. We introduce Involuntary In-Context Learning (IICL), an attack class that uses abstract operator framing with few-shot examples to force pattern completion that overrides safety training. Through 3479 probes across 10 OpenAI models, we identify the attack's effective components through a seven-experiment ablation study. Key findings: (1)~semantic operator naming achieves 100\,\% bypass rate (50/50, $p < 0.001$); (2)~the attack requires abstract framing, since identical examples in direct question-and-answer format yield 0\,\%; (3)~example ordering matters strongly (interleaved: 76\,\%, harmful-first: 6\,\%); (4)~temperature has no meaningful effect (46--56\,\% across 0.0--1.0). On the HarmBench benchmark, IICL achieves 24.0\,\% bypass $[18.6\%, 30.4\%]$ against GPT-5.4 with detailed 619-word responses, compared to 0.0\,\% for direct queries.

</details>


### [142] [API Security Based on Automatic OpenAPI Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19471)
*Yarin Levi,Ran Dubin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MRG是一种无监督的HTTP REST API建模与安全方法，通过从真实流量学习API结构，自动生成OpenAPI文档，实现实时更新、可视化解释和异常检测，在检测精度和推理速度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着微服务架构的普及，HTTP REST API变得日益复杂和动态，传统基于规则或手动维护的API安全方法难以应对快速变化的API结构和潜在安全威胁，需要一种能够自动学习API结构、实时更新并检测异常的无监督方法。

Method: MRG采用三阶段流程：训练阶段从真实流量无监督学习API结构并生成OpenAPI兼容文档；更新阶段实时更新API模型；检测阶段使用基于图的验证和深度自编码器进行有效载荷分析，检测畸形请求、结构偏差和注入攻击。

Result: 与HRAL和FT-ANN等先进方法相比，MRG实现了高达11.4%的召回率提升，推理速度超过20倍，在多种API层攻击检测中达到100%精确度，同时支持实时更新和可解释可视化。

Conclusion: MRG为动态微服务环境提供了一种完全自动化、高效的实时API可见性、模式推断和异常检测管道，无需人工调优或标记数据，显著提升了API安全性和可维护性。

Abstract: This paper presents Map Reduce Graph (MRG), a novel unsupervised method for modeling and securing HTTP REST APIs. MRG learns API structure from real-world traffic without prior knowledge or labels, automatically generating OpenAPI-compliant documentation by reconstructing routes, methods, and parameter formats. MRG enables real-time updates, explainable visualization, and anomaly detection, helping identify undocumented or evolving behaviors. It detects malformed requests, structural deviations, and injection attacks using graph-based validation and a deep autoencoder for payload analysis. Compared to state-of-the-art methods like HRAL and FT-ANN, MRG achieves up to 11.4% higher recall, over 20 times faster inference, and perfect precision (100%) on multiple API-layer attacks. Designed for dynamic microservice environments, MRG operates in three phases - training, updating, and detection - and integrates smoothly with observability and security tools. This work contributes a fully automated, efficient pipeline for real-time API visibility, schema inference, and anomaly detection without manual tuning or labeled data.

</details>


### [143] [Evaluating LLM-Generated Obfuscated XSS Payloads for Machine Learning-Based Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19526)
*Divyesh Gabbireddy,Suman Saha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个使用大语言模型生成和评估混淆XSS攻击载荷的结构化流程，通过浏览器运行时评估验证行为有效性，发现微调能提升行为匹配率但仍有局限，强调运行时行为检查对安全数据生成的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XSS攻击载荷的混淆变换使得传统和基于机器学习的检测系统难以可靠识别攻击。现有混淆生成方法强调语法多样性但无法保证行为有效性，需要一种能生成行为有效混淆载荷的系统化方法。

Method: 提出结构化流程：结合确定性变换技术和基于LLM的生成，使用基于浏览器的运行时评估程序在受控执行环境中比较载荷行为。通过微调LLM学习行为保持的源-目标混淆对，评估生成样本的运行时行为匹配而非仅语法相似性。

Result: 未调优基线LLM的运行时行为匹配率为0.15，在行为保持的混淆对上微调后提升至0.22。下游分类器评估显示添加生成载荷未改善检测性能，但经过行为过滤的生成样本可被纳入而不显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 研究展示了生成模型在对抗性安全数据生成中的潜力和局限，强调运行时行为检查对提高生成数据质量的重要性。当前LLM仍难以生成保持观察运行时行为的混淆，需要更有效的生成策略。

Abstract: Cross-site scripting (XSS) remains a persistent web security vulnerability, especially because obfuscation can change the surface form of a malicious payload while preserving its behavior. These transformations make it difficult for traditional and machine learning-based detection systems to reliably identify attacks. Existing approaches for generating obfuscated payloads often emphasize syntactic diversity, but they do not always ensure that the generated samples remain behaviorally valid. This paper presents a structured pipeline for generating and evaluating obfuscated XSS payloads using large language models (LLMs). The pipeline combines deterministic transformation techniques with LLM-based generation and uses a browser- based runtime evaluation procedure to compare payload behavior in a controlled execution environment. This allows generated samples to be assessed through observable runtime behavior rather than syntactic similarity alone. In the evaluation, an untuned baseline language model achieves a runtime behavior match rate of 0.15, while fine-tuning on behavior-preserving source-target obfuscation pairs improves the match rate to 0.22. Although this represents a measurable improvement, the results show that current LLMs still struggle to generate obfuscations that preserve observed runtime behavior. A downstream classifier evaluation further shows that adding generated payloads does not improve detection performance in this setting, although behavior- filtered generated samples can be incorporated without materially degrading performance. Overall, the study demonstrates both the promise and the limits of applying generative models to adversarial security data generation and emphasizes the importance of runtime behavior checks in improving the quality of generated data for downstream detection systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Structural Verification for Reliable EDA Code Generation without Tool-in-the-Loop Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18834)
*Dinithi Jayasuriya,Aravind Saravanan,Nilesh Ahuja,Amanda Rios,Amit Trivedi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于结构依赖图验证的EDA代码生成方法，通过预执行验证消除工具在环调试，提高可靠性和效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的EDA工作流自动化面临可靠性挑战，失败主要源于设计对象间的隐式结构依赖违规，而非语法错误。工具在环调试方法虽然有效，但将正确性与重复工具调用耦合，导致高延迟和可扩展性差

Method: 将每个任务表示为结构依赖图作为显式执行契约，采用验证器引导的合成框架，通过图条件检索、约束生成和分阶段预执行验证（包含诊断驱动修复）来强制执行该契约

Result: 单步任务通过率从73.0%（LLM+RAG）和76.0%（工具在环）提升至82.5%，每个任务仅需一次工具调用，总工具调用减少2倍以上。多步任务通过率从30.0%提升至70.0%，结合轨迹级反思后达84.0%。不确定性感知过滤将验证器假阳性从20.0%降至6.7%，精确度从80.0%提升至93.3%

Conclusion: 在EDA代码生成中，通过预执行强制执行结构一致性可以将正确性与工具交互解耦，显著提高长视野任务的可靠性和效率

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled natural-language-driven automation of electronic design automation (EDA) workflows, but reliable execution of generated scripts remains a fundamental challenge. In LLM-based EDA tasks, failures arise not from syntax errors but from violations of implicit structural dependencies over design objects, including invalid acquisition paths, missing prerequisites, and incompatible API usage. Existing approaches address these failures through tool-in-the-loop debugging, repeatedly executing and repairing programs using runtime feedback. While effective, this paradigm couples correctness to repeated tool invocation, leading to high latency and poor scalability in multi-step settings. We propose to eliminate tool-in-the-loop debugging by enforcing structural correctness prior to execution. Each task is represented as a structural dependency graph that serves as an explicit execution contract, and a verifier-guided synthesis framework enforces this contract through graph-conditioned retrieval, constrained generation, and staged pre-execution verification with diagnosis-driven repair. On single-step tasks, our method improves pass rate from 73.0% (LLM+RAG) and 76.0% (tool-in-loop) to 82.5%, while requiring exactly one tool call per task and reducing total tool calls by more than 2x. On multi-step tasks, pass rate improves from 30.0% to 70.0%, and further to 84.0% with trajectory-level reflection. Uncertainty-aware filtering further reduces verifier false positives from 20.0% to 6.7% and improves precision from 80.0% to 93.3%. These results show that enforcing structural consistency prior to execution decouples correctness from tool interaction, improving both reliability and efficiency in long-horizon EDA code generation.

</details>


### [145] [From Business Problems to AI Solutions: Where Does Transformation Support Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18770)
*Abir Trabelsi,Imen Benzarti,Hafedh Mili,Darine Ameyed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过文献综述发现现有方法在将业务问题转化为机器学习解决方案方面存在系统性指导不足，提出了"分析翻译问题"概念和五个研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将业务问题转化为明确定义的机器学习解决方案是AI系统成功的前提，但这一上游翻译过程在现有方法论中是最缺乏支持的步骤。

Method: 对18种方法进行结构化叙事文献综述，涵盖需求工程、机器学习项目管理、自动化等领域，将这些方法组织成四个家族的分类法，并基于需求工程精化理论和ML生命周期过程的七个阶段转换框架进行比较。

Result: 研究表明，大多数方法将ML任务或算法规范列为预期输出，但只有四种方法提供部分指导，没有一种提供系统性指导。作者将这一差距定义为"分析翻译问题"，并提出了五个研究建议。

Conclusion: 需要解决分析翻译问题，重点关注多公式探索、任务推导指导、约束-算法过滤、概率可追溯性和数据触发修订五个研究方向，以更好地支持业务问题到ML解决方案的转化。

Abstract: Translating business problems into well-specified machine learning solutions is a prerequisite for successful AI systems, yet this upstream translation is still one of the least supported steps in existing methodologies. We conduct a structured narrative literature review of 18 approaches spanning requirements engineering (RE), machine learning (ML) project management, and automation. We organize these approaches into a taxonomy of four families and compare them across six input artifact categories, six output artifact categories, and a transformation framework of seven stages, grounded in RE refinement theory and ML lifecycle process. Our study shows that most approaches list ML task or algorithm specification among their expected outputs, yet only four provide partial guidance for deriving it, and none provides systematic guidance. We characterize this gap as the Analytics Translation Problem (ATP) and derive five research recommendations addressing multi-formulation exploration, task derivation guidance, constraint-algorithm filtering, probabilistic traceability, and data-triggered revision.

</details>


### [146] [Human-Machine Co-Boosted Bug Report Identification with Mutualistic Neural Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18862)
*Guoming Long,Shihai Wang,Hui Fang,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出MNALS框架，通过神经语言模型与主动学习的结合，实现GitHub仓库中缺陷报告的自动识别，并建立人机协同的互利关系来提升识别效率和可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷报告数量庞大且类型多样，手动识别和分配耗时耗力，需要自动化解决方案来提升软件维护效率。

Method: 提出互惠神经主动学习（MNALS）框架，结合神经语言模型（跨项目学习）和主动学习，通过人机协同选择最具信息量的报告进行标注，同时提升报告的可读性和可识别性。

Result: 在可读性方面减少95.8%工作量，在可识别性方面减少196.0%工作量，同时提升缺陷报告识别性能；框架与模型无关，能提升多种神经语言模型的性能。

Conclusion: MNALS框架通过建立人机互利关系，有效提升缺陷报告识别的自动化水平，减少人工工作量，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Bug reports, encompassing a wide range of bug types, are crucial for maintaining software quality. However, the increasing complexity and volume of bug reports pose a significant challenge in sole manual identification and assignment to the appropriate teams for resolution, as dealing with all the reports is time-consuming and resource-intensive. In this paper, we introduce a cross-project framework, dubbed Mutualistic Neural Active Learning (MNAL), designed for automated and more effective identification of bug reports from GitHub repositories boosted by human-machine collaboration. MNAL utilizes a neural language model that learns and generalizes reports across different projects, coupled with active learning to form neural active learning. A distinctive feature of MNAL is the purposely crafted mutualistic relation between the machine learners (neural language model) and human labelers (developers) when enriching the knowledge learned. That is, the most informative human-labeled reports and their corresponding pseudo-labeled ones are used to update the model while those reports that need to be labeled by developers are more readable and identifiable, thereby enhancing the human-machine teaming therein. We evaluate MNAL using a large scale dataset against the SOTA approaches, baselines, and different variants. The results indicate that MNAL achieves up to 95.8% and 196.0% effort reduction in terms of readability and identifiability during human labeling, respectively, while resulting in a better performance in bug report identification. Additionally, our MNAL is model-agnostic since it is capable of improving the model performance with various underlying neural language models. To further verify the efficacy of our approach, we conducted a qualitative case study involving 10 human participants, who rate MNAL as being more effective while saving more time and monetary resources.

</details>


### [147] [From Particles to Perils: SVGD-Based Hazardous Scenario Generation for Autonomous Driving Systems Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18918)
*Linfeng Liang,Xiao Cheng,Tsong Yueh Chen,Xi Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PtoP框架结合自适应随机种子生成与Stein变分梯度下降，为自动驾驶系统仿真测试生成多样化的故障诱导初始条件，显著提升安全违规率、场景多样性和地图覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于搜索的种子生成方法（如遗传算法）在高维空间中表现不佳，容易陷入有限模式，无法发现多样化的故障场景。自动驾驶系统仿真测试需要发现真实且多样化的故障，特别是在密集、异构的交通环境中。

Method: PtoP框架结合自适应随机种子生成与Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）。SVGD通过平衡向高风险区域的吸引力和粒子间的排斥力，生成既追求风险又分布良好的种子，覆盖多种故障模式。该框架是即插即用的，可为现有在线测试方法提供原则性种子。

Result: 在CARLA仿真环境中对三个自动驾驶系统（Apollo、Autoware和原生端到端系统）进行评估。PtoP相比基线方法显著提升了安全违规率（最高27.68%）、场景多样性（9.6%）和地图覆盖率（16.78%）。

Conclusion: PtoP框架通过SVGD生成多样化、风险导向的初始条件，有效解决了高维空间中种子生成的问题，显著提升了自动驾驶系统仿真测试的效果，能够发现更多样化和真实的故障场景。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing of autonomous driving systems (ADS) must uncover realistic and diverse failures in dense, heterogeneous traffic. However, existing search-based seeding methods (e.g., genetic algorithms) struggle in high-dimensional spaces, often collapsing to limited modes and missing many failure scenarios. We present PtoP, a framework that combines adaptive random seed generation with Stein Variational Gradient Descent (SVGD) to produce diverse, failure-inducing initial conditions. SVGD balances attraction toward high-risk regions and repulsion among particles, yielding risk-seeking yet well-distributed seeds across multiple failure modes. PtoP is plug-and-play and enhances existing online testing methods (e.g., reinforcement learning--based testers) by providing principled seeds. Evaluation in CARLA on two industry-grade ADS (Apollo, Autoware) and a native end-to-end system shows that PtoP improves safety violation rate (up to 27.68%), scenario diversity (9.6%), and map coverage (16.78%) over baselines.

</details>


### [148] [Proactive Detection of GUI Defects in Multi-Window Scenarios via Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19081)
*Xinyao Zhang,Rui Wang,Jinhao Cui,Haotian Huang,Wei Xue,Wenhua Hu,Jianwen Xiang,Rui Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出用于多窗口移动场景的GUI显示缺陷检测框架，通过主动触发多窗口状态、SoM对齐和MLLM推理，在50个真实Android应用中构建基准测试，显著提升缺陷检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多窗口移动场景（如分屏和折叠模式）迫使应用适应变化的窗口尺寸和动态布局重排，增加了GUI显示缺陷的可能性。现有检测技术存在两个主要局限：1）被动分析，仅在问题状态出现后分析截图；2）主要针对传统全屏界面设计，在多窗口场景中效果有限。

Method: 提出端到端框架：1）主动触发分屏、折叠和窗口过渡状态进行应用探索；2）使用Set-of-Mark（SoM）将截图与部件级界面元素对齐；3）利用多模态大语言模型结合思维链提示来检测、定位和解释显示缺陷。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）多窗口设置显著增加布局相关缺陷暴露，文本截断相比传统全屏设置增加184%；2）应用层面：检测出40个易缺陷应用，假阳性率10.00%，假阴性率11.11%，优于OwlEye和YOLO基线；3）细粒度层面：部件遮挡检测达到最佳F1分数87.2%。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了多窗口移动场景中GUI显示缺陷检测的挑战，通过主动探索、SoM对齐和MLLM推理的组合方法，显著提升了缺陷检测的准确性和覆盖率，为移动应用质量保障提供了新方案。

Abstract: Multi-window mobile scenarios, such as split-screen and foldable modes, make GUI display defects more likely by forcing applications to adapt to changing window sizes and dynamic layout reflow. Existing detection techniques are limited in two ways: they are largely passive, analyzing screenshots only after problematic states have been reached, and they are mainly designed for conventional full-screen interfaces, making them less effective in multi-window settings.We propose an end-to-end framework for GUI display defect detection in multi-window mobile scenarios. The framework proactively triggers split-screen, foldable, and window-transition states during app exploration, uses Set-of-Mark (SoM) to align screenshots with widget-level interface elements, and leverages multimodal large language models with chain-of-thought prompting to detect, localize, and explain display defects. We also construct a benchmark of GUI display defects using 50 real-world Android applications.Experimental results show that multi-window settings substantially increase the exposure of layout-related defects, with text truncation increasing by 184% compared with conventional full-screen settings. At the application level, our method detects 40 defect-prone apps with a false positive rate of 10.00% and a false negative rate of 11.11%, outperforming OwlEye and YOLO-based baselines. At the fine-grained level, it achieves the best F1 score of 87.2% for widget occlusion detection.

</details>


### [149] [MUCOCO: Automated Consistency Testing of Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19086)
*Chua Jin Chou,Khant That Lwin,Ezekiel Soremekun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MUCOCO是一种自动一致性测试方法，通过语义保持的变异分析来暴露代码LLM中的不一致行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码LLM经常表现出不一致的程序行为，现有基准测试大多是手工制作、静态的，且不针对一致性属性，需要自动发现代码LLM中的不一致行为

Method: 提出MUCOCO方法，通过语义保持的变异分析自动将程序转换为语义等价的变体（突变体），检测突变体与原始程序之间的不一致性（如不同输出或测试失败）

Result: 在4个编码任务和7个LLM上评估，MUCOCO能有效暴露不一致性，优于基线方法TURBULENCE，约15%的输入暴露了不一致性

Conclusion: 这项工作强调了测试代码LLM一致性属性的必要性，MUCOCO为自动发现不一致行为提供了有效方法

Abstract: Code LLMs often portray inconsistent program behaviors. Developers typically employ benchmarks to assess Code LLMs, but most benchmarks are hand-crafted, static and do not target consistency property. In this work, we pose the scientific question: how can we automatically discover inconsistent program behaviors in Code LLMs? To address this challenge, we propose an automated consistency testing method, called MUCOCO, which employs semantic-preserving mutation analysis to expose inconsistent behaviors in code LLMs. Given a coding query, MUCOCO automatically transforms its program into semantically equivalent programs (aka mutants) and detects inconsistencies between the mutants and the original program (e.g., different output or test failure). We evaluate MUCOCO using four (4) coding tasks and seven (7) LLMs. Results show that MUCOCO is effective in exposing inconsistency and outperforms the closest baseline (TURBULENCE). About one in seven (15%) inputs generated by MUCOCO exposed inconsistencies. Our work motivates the need to test Code LLMs for consistency property

</details>


### [150] [Towards More Empathic Programming Environments: An Experimental Empathic AI-Enhanced IDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19142)
*Justin Rainier Go,Kurt Christian Andaya,Roemer Gabriel Caliboso,Aaron Daniel Go,Jocelynn Cu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Ceci是一个注重学习与情感支持的编程IDE，与VSCode+ChatGPT相比，在错误修正方面感知帮助度更高，但整体学习效果和工作负荷无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在软件开发中的普及，存在过度依赖和批判性思维减弱的风险。研究旨在探索如何通过强调学习和情感支持而非直接代码生成的编程环境来支持新手程序员。

Method: 开发了Ceci（Caring Empathic C IDE），并与VSCode+ChatGPT进行对比研究。11名参与者完成编码任务，使用NASA-TLX工作负荷评估和可用性后测调查进行评估。

Result: 实验组（Ceci）与对照组在感知有效性、学习和工作负荷方面无显著差异，但Ceci用户在错误修正方面的感知帮助度显著更高（p=0.0220）。样本量较小（n=11）。

Conclusion: 仅靠共情响应本身不足以改善学习成果、感知或降低工作负荷。研究为未来探索提供了基础框架，建议扩大样本量、多样化编程任务、改进共情功能集成。

Abstract: As generative AI becomes integral to software development, the risk of over-reliance and diminished critical thinking grows. This study introduces "Ceci," our Caring Empathic C IDE designed to support novice programmers by prioritizing learning and emotional support over direct code generation. The researchers conducted a comparative pilot study between Ceci and VSCode + ChatGPT [9, 40]. Participants completed a coding task and were evaluated using the NASA-TLX workload assessment and a post-test usability survey. Although the sample size was small (n = 11), results show that there is no significant difference in perceived effectiveness, learning and workload between the Experimental Ceci group and the Control group, though Ceci users reported significantly greater perceived helpfulness in error correction (p = 0.0220). These findings suggest that empathic responses may not be sufficient on their own to enhance the learner's outcomes, perceptions, or reduce workload. Overall, this study provides a foundational framework for future research. Such research should explore larger sample sizes, diverse programming tasks, and additional empathic features to better understand the potential of empathic programming environments in supporting novice programmers; they must also ensure that the empathic features are well-integrated in the user interface.

</details>


### [151] [Cascaded Code Editing: Large-Small Model Collaboration for Effective and Efficient Code Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19201)
*Chaozheng Wang,Zezhou Yang,Shuzheng Gao,Cuiyun Gao,Zongjie Li,Yichen Li,Ting Peng,Hailiang Huang,Yuetang Deng,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出两阶段级联代码编辑框架：大模型生成编辑草图，小模型应用草图到代码，提升效率同时保持准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在代码编辑任务中效率低下，生成大量未修改代码；小模型缺乏处理长代码上下文的能力。需要平衡效率与准确性

Method: 将代码编辑分解为两阶段：1) 编辑草图生成（大模型）：生成简洁的修改草图；2) 编辑草图应用（小模型）：将草图集成到原始代码生成最终输出

Result: 级联设计减少大模型生成的token数量，大部分输出由更高效的小模型处理，提升整体效率

Conclusion: 两阶段级联方法在保持代码编辑准确性的同时显著提升效率，但小模型处理长上下文和跨文件依赖的能力仍需改进

Abstract: Code editing constitutes a fundamental practice in software development, wherein developers modify existing codebases according to natural language requirements. Accurate code editing necessitates a comprehensive understanding of both the existing codebase and the modification requirements. Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated promising performance in code editing tasks, they suffer from substantial inefficiency by generating entire modified files that largely consist of unchanged code. While smaller models could potentially address this inefficiency, they typically lack the capacity to effectively comprehend long code contexts required for accurate editing. To ensure both effectiveness and efficiency, we propose to decompose code editing into a two-stage cascade: \textbf{edit sketch generation}, wherein a large model first produces concise sketches representing the requisite modifications (the more challenging phase), and \textbf{edit sketch application}, wherein a smaller model integrates these sketches into the original code to produce the final output edited code (the simpler phase).
